### [1] [Personalized and Explainable Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG via Hybrid CNN--Morphological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13190)
*Myung-Kyu Yi,Jongshill Lee,Jeyeon Lee,In Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生理引导的混合框架，将CNN波形动态分支与形态学先验分支结合，用于个性化无袖带血压估计，在MIMIC-III子集上取得SBP MAE 3.77 mmHg、DBP MAE 2.36 mmHg，并提升了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPG无袖带血压监测方法存在两大局限：手工特征方法依赖精确基准点检测且仅适用于短期分析；深度学习虽精度高但多为黑箱，生理可解释性不足。因此需要一种兼具准确性与可解释性的个性化方案。

Method: 提出生理引导的混合框架：一支CNN分支捕捉全局与局部波形动态，另一支形态学先验分支显式编码个体特异性血管特征，通过嵌入基于形态的特征集增强个性化并降低对大规模训练数据的依赖，并采用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在MIMIC-III数据库子集上以受试者个性化测试协议评估，SBP的MAE为3.77 mmHg、DBP的MAE为2.36 mmHg，相对仅CNN的个性化基线分别提升43.7%和32.4%；SHAP分析证实形态学先验特征与个体血管特征一致。

Conclusion: 个性化、生理引导的混合学习结合新型形态学描述符，有望实现真实场景下准确且可解释的血压监测。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) offers a promising solution for personalized healthcare. However, existing methods have two major limitations. Handcrafted feature-based approaches rely on precise fiducial point detection and are limited to short-term analysis, while deep learning models, despite their accuracy, often operate as black boxes with limited physiological interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose a physiology-guided hybrid framework for personalized BP estimation that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) branch capturing global and local waveform dynamics with a morphology-prior branch that explicitly encodes person-specific vascular characteristics. By embedding a morphology-based feature set that explicitly encodes individual vascular characteristics, the proposed framework enhances personalization and reduces dependence on large-scale training datasets. Evaluated on a subset of the MIMIC-III database under a subject-specific (personalized) testing protocol, the proposed personalized physiology-guided hybrid approach achieved mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 3.77 mmHg for systolic BP and 2.36 mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to relative improvements of 43.7% and 32.4% over a subject-specific (personalized) CNN-only baseline. SHAP-based analysis confirmed that the introduced morphology-prior features align with individual vascular characteristics, reinforcing per-subject interpretability. These findings highlight the potential of personalized, physiology-guided hybrid learning with novel morphological descriptors for accurate and explainable BP monitoring in real-world settings.

</details>


### [2] [Part Grounding, Not Action Knowledge: Locating the Bottleneck in VLM Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13225)
*Sarthak Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型在低层操作推理上表现不佳，本文将其拆分为"识别作用部件"与"知道该部件所需动作"两步，发现部件定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只能给出总体准确率，无法指出模型失败在哪一步。affordance问题混淆了两个子能力：识别物体的哪一部分需要被操作，以及该部分需要什么动作。作者希望解耦这两步以定位真正的瓶颈。

Method: 在19个铰接物体上，测试8个来自3家开发者的模型，询问机器人应施加什么运动。设置开放提示与命名目标部件两种条件对比，并额外做自由文本描述、真实照片定位抓取点等实验。

Result: 开放提示下push仅64次中出现1次正确；模型常描述被评分部件之外的部位。命名目标部件后，各模型动作准确率提升0.32-0.63（从0.158-0.474升至0.684-0.947），push召回从0-1/8升至7-8/8。开放提示下无一模型超过忽略图像的常数答案，命名部件后8个模型全部超越。自由文本下对同一部件6-8/8给出按压类语言。真实照片上仅3/8模型抓取点定位优于常数基线，渲染物体上0/8。

Conclusion: 结果难以用"缺失动作知识"解释，指向部件定位是主导瓶颈，且该模式跨三个模型家族、不随能力提升而减弱。命名部件提供了定位变量，因而只界定了完美部件检测器能带来的上限，而非证明通用力学模型。作者同时自曝两处测量误差（让常数基线达0.929的阈值、4/19错误的标注规则），均由用平凡替代方案检验数值时发现。

Abstract: Benchmarks agree that vision-language models reason poorly about low-level manipulation, but an aggregate accuracy score does not say which step fails. We separate two steps that affordance questions conflate: identifying which part of an object to act on, and knowing what action that part requires. Across 19 articulated objects we asked eight models, spanning three developers, what motion a robot should apply. Under an open prompt, push was produced once in 64 evaluations where it was correct, despite being correct for 8 of 19 objects and appearing in the offered label set every time. Inspecting the outputs showed why: models described a different part than the one being scored, e.g. explaining how to pick up a camera rather than press its button. Naming the target part raises action accuracy by 0.32 to 0.63 for every model, from 0.158-0.474 to 0.684-0.947, and push recall from 0-1/8 to 7-8/8. No model beats a constant answer that ignores the image under the open prompt; once the part is named, all eight do. Asked to describe the same part in free prose with no label set, models produce pressing language for 6 to 8 of 8. These results are hard to reconcile with missing action knowledge, and instead point to part grounding as the dominant bottleneck, a pattern that holds across all three model families and does not diminish with capability. Naming the part supplies the grounding variable, so this bounds what a perfect part detector would offer rather than demonstrating a general model of mechanics. Two supporting results agree: on real photographs only three of eight models localize grasp points better than a constant baseline, and on rendered objects none do. We also document two measurement errors of our own, a threshold that let a constant baseline score 0.929 and a labelling rule wrong on 4 of 19 objects, both caught only by testing our numbers against trivial alternatives.

</details>


### [3] [Synthetic Leprosy Image Generation Using Mask-Conditioned Latent Diffusion and Transfer Learning from Large Chronic Wound Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13226)
*Yusuf Abdulkadir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建了"分割-生成-微调"三阶段流水线，将充足的慢性伤口摄影数据迁移到麻风病病灶图像合成这一低数据场景，证明底层外观迁移有效，剩余瓶颈在于语义控制而非图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麻风病（汉森氏病）等被忽视热带疾病的机器学习受限于数据而非算法：公开标注图像仅数百张，远低于生成模型所需规模。作者希望验证：在数据充足的慢性伤口摄影上训练的模型，能否迁移至这一低数据领域。

Method: 三阶段流水线。第一步：用DeepLabV3-ResNet50分割网络（验证集Dice 0.876，IoU 0.799）为两个缺乏掩码的伤口数据集生成病灶掩码。第二步：基于Stable Diffusion 1.5组件构建掩码条件潜扩散模型，在3,280个感兴趣区域伤口裁剪图上训练，将UNet输入卷积从4通道扩展至11通道，以接纳三张掩码特征图和一个模糊低频上下文潜变量。第三步：在来自约150名患者的764张图像中抽取的708对麻风病图像-掩码上微调该模型。

Result: 采用LPIPS感知距离评估，并以242张锚点图像上的真实-真实基线作为参照。生成集无模式崩溃：其内部感知多样性（0.662）与真实麻风病集（0.672，95% CI [0.664, 0.680]）在统计上不可区分。生成图像位于真实分布外0.044 LPIPS，虽可测量地分离，但不到一个标准差的二分之一。微调仅边际性地改变了输出分布，作者将其归因于病灶几何信息仅通过输入拼接传入网络。

Conclusion: 慢性伤口摄影是麻风病病灶合成的可行供体域：底层外观迁移良好，剩余障碍是语义控制而非图像质量。

Abstract: Machine learning for neglected tropical diseases is limited by data, not algorithms: public annotated image sets for leprosy (Hansen's disease) number in the hundreds, orders of magnitude below what generative models require. We ask whether a model trained on abundant chronic wound photography transfers to this low-data regime. We build a three-stage pipeline. First, a DeepLabV3-ResNet50 segmentation network (validation Dice 0.876, IoU 0.799) supplies lesion masks for two wound datasets that ship without them. Second, we assemble a mask-conditioned latent diffusion model from Stable Diffusion 1.5 components and train it on 3,280 region-of-interest wound crops, widening the UNet input convolution from 4 to 11 channels to admit three mask feature maps and a blurred low-frequency context latent. Third, we fine-tune this model on 708 leprosy image-mask pairs drawn from 764 images of approximately 150 patients. We evaluate with LPIPS perceptual distance, anchored by a real-versus-real baseline computed on the same 242 anchor images as the cross-set comparisons; without that reference the cross-set distances cannot be interpreted. The generated set shows no mode collapse: its internal perceptual diversity (0.662) is statistically indistinguishable from that of the real leprosy set (0.672, 95% CI [0.664, 0.680]). Generated images sit 0.044 LPIPS outside the real distribution - measurably apart, but under half of one standard deviation. Fine-tuning shifted the output distribution only marginally, which we trace to lesion geometry reaching the network through input concatenation alone. Chronic wound photography is therefore a viable donor domain for leprosy lesion synthesis: low-level appearance transfers well, and the remaining barrier is semantic control rather than image quality.

</details>


### [4] [What Does the Encoder Actually Decide? A Controlled Comparison of Vision Backbones on Joint Tree Segmentation and Stereo Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13232)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定数据、解码器、损失与训练配置的条件下，系统比较[N]种编码器（约25M参数，含CNN、Transformer、混合、MLP-mixer、状态空间模型）对细长植被的语义分割与立体深度的联合影响，发现卷积与混合编码器最强、参数量不预示质量、两任务排序高度一致，且部分编码器退化为"全树"分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修剪机器人需要逐像素判断是否属于树木并给出距离，但编码器通常凭"声誉"而非实测来选择。作者希望量化：在其它条件不变时，编码器选择对细长植被上的联合语义分割与立体深度性能究竟有多大影响。

Method: 构建硬参数共享网络，单一编码器同时驱动分割与深度两个分支；仅更换编码器且不重新调优下游。在约25M参数预算下从零训练[N]种编码器，覆盖[M]个架构家族（CNN、Transformer、混合、MLP-mixer、状态空间模型）。深度仅在树木像素上评估；分割采用边界F1与背景IoU，以避免"把一切都标成树"的捷径。

Result: 一，最强编码器为卷积与混合架构而非Transformer：[BestEncoder]取得[MIoU]分割mIoU与[Delta]深度δ₁，而[Y]个纯视觉Transformer中有[X]个从零训练时崩溃。二，参数量不能预测质量：[SmallEncoder]仅[P]M参数却优于大两个数量级的模型。三，分割与深度排序高度一致（Spearman ρ=[RhoValue]），无任务冲突。四，[N]个编码器中有[K]个退化为全树分割，边界F1可发现而区域IoU会掩盖。

Conclusion: 编码器选择对细长植被的联合分割与深度任务影响显著，且不应依据架构声誉或参数量来选取；卷积与混合编码器在该设定下更优。同时说明评估指标选择很关键：边界F1能揭示退化行为，而区域IoU会隐藏它。建议在实际机器人修剪系统中以实测指标而非默认偏好来选择编码器。

Abstract: A robot pruning trees needs two facts per pixel: whether it belongs to a tree, and its distance. Both are usually obtained via task heads attached to a vision backbone chosen by reputation rather than measurement. Holding dataset, decoders, losses, schedule, and evaluation fixed, we ask: how much does the encoder choice change joint semantic segmentation and stereo depth on thin vegetation? We build a hard parameter-sharing network with one encoder feeding both branches, swapping only the encoder without downstream retuning. We evaluate [N] encoders across [M] architecture families (CNNs, transformers, hybrids, MLP-mixers, state-space models) near a ~25M budget, trained from scratch. Depth is evaluated on tree pixels only; segmentation uses boundary F1 and background IoU to prevent "label-everything-tree" shortcuts. Three findings stand out. First, the strongest encoders are convolutional and hybrid, not transformers: [BestEncoder] leads with [MIoU] segmentation mIoU and [Delta] depth $δ_1$, while [X] of [Y] plain vision transformers collapse when trained from scratch. Second, parameter count does not predict quality --- [SmallEncoder] at only [P]M parameters outranks models two orders of magnitude larger. Third, segmentation and depth rankings agree strongly (Spearman $ρ$ = [RhoValue]), showing no task conflict. Finally, [K] of [N] encoders collapse to degenerate all-tree segmentation --- exposed by boundary F1 but hidden by region IoU.

</details>


### [5] [EMCStereo: Attention-Enhanced Stereo Matching for Thin-Structure Depth Estimation with a Synthetic Tree-Branch Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13233)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对细结构（如树枝）立体匹配难题，提出EMCStereo网络、VirtualTree合成数据集，并通过八向消融实验验证注意力模块效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细结构（如树枝）是立体匹配中最难的场景之一：树枝仅几个像素宽、背景杂乱，且真实树枝的稠密真值几乎无法手工标注。现有方法在这类场景下精度不足，且缺乏带精确视差的训练/评测数据。

Method: 提出三项贡献：（1）EMCStereo在PSMNet式代价体积骨干中集成三个轻量注意力模块——对深层语义特征使用高效多尺度注意力EMA，用多尺度融合块MSFblock以学习空间金字塔权重替代拼接，对最终匹配特征使用坐标注意力CoordAtt；MSFblock将四个金字塔分支合并为一个，使网络缩小2.0%，推理时间仅增加1.7%。（2）VirtualTree：在Unreal Engine 5中用模拟ZED Mini双目装置渲染的合成立体数据集，提供5,520对带精确视差的细树枝样本。（3）八向消融实验，建立0.009 px端点到端点误差（EPE）的运行时噪声下限。

Result: EMCStereo在VirtualTree测试集上达到1.31 px EPE（D1-all 5.96%），SceneFlow上1.00 px，KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015、ETH3D、Middlebury上分别为0.80、0.73、0.62、3.19 px，深度精度delta_1从92.6%提升至98.7%。对照噪声下限，在100轮训练预算下注意力堆栈对精度中性，而缺少EMA时MSFblock和CoordAtt会带来0.03–0.05 px的误差代价。

Conclusion: 通过在轻量骨干中集成EMA、MSFblock与CoordAtt，并以合成数据VirtualTree补充监督，可在几乎不增加模型与推理开销的前提下有效处理细结构立体匹配；消融表明各注意力模块的作用存在依赖关系，EMA是维持精度中性的关键。

Abstract: Thin structures such as tree branches are among the hardest cases for stereo matching: a branch is only a few pixels wide, the background is cluttered, and dense ground truth for real branches is nearly impossible to label by hand. We make three contributions. First, EMCStereo integrates three lightweight attention modules into a PSMNet-style cost-volume backbone: Efficient Multi-scale Attention (EMA) on deep semantic features, a Multi-Scale Fusion block (MSFblock) learning spatial pyramid weights instead of concatenating them, and Coordinate Attention (CoordAtt) on final matching features. Because MSFblock collapses four pyramid branches into one, the modules leave the network 2.0% smaller and add only 1.7% inference time overhead. Second, VirtualTree is a synthetic stereo dataset rendered in Unreal Engine 5 with a simulated ZED Mini rig, providing 5,520 pairs with exact disparity for thin branches. Third, an eight-way ablation establishes a run-to-run noise floor of 0.009 px end-point error (EPE). EMCStereo achieves 1.31 px EPE (5.96% D1-all) on the VirtualTree test split, 1.00 px on SceneFlow, and 0.80, 0.73, 0.62, and 3.19 px on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury, with depth accuracy delta_1 from 92.6% to 98.7%. Evaluated against the noise floor, the attention stack is accuracy-neutral at a 100-epoch budget, while MSFblock and CoordAtt cost 0.03-0.05 px unless EMA is present.

</details>


### [6] [ArtSociety: Multi-Agent Multimodal Collaboration for Art Emotion Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13240)
*Jian Li,Fanfan Ji,Jinxiang Lai,Ying Tai,Jian Yang,Xiao-Tong Yuan,Chengjie Wang,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtSociety 是一个多智能体框架，通过组装异构多模态专家并用免训练控制器协调，在艺术作品多维情感理解任务上取得了总体 0.8870 的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务要求同时预测细粒度情感（12 类，头尾比 1549:1）、二元效价/唤醒度以及五个属性描述，这些子任务之间存在强烈的经验性权衡，单一模型难以同时优化所有子任务。

Method: 框架汇集了异构多模态专家：DINOv2-Giant 视觉智能体（A1）、场景化 CoT 微调的 MLLM（A2）以及三个闭源推理智能体（A3-A5），并用两个免训练控制器协调：(i) 稀有类感知投票仲裁器，为尾部情感降低一致性阈值，利用不同智能体家族间去相关的错误模式；(ii) 描述优先推理智能体，其 DESCRIBE-then-CLASSIFY 思维链强制先形成视觉证据再确定标签。另设任务路由策略，将困难情感任务交给完整五智能体集成，而将近饱和的效价/唤醒度及生成描述任务分配给单个最强推理智能体。

Result: 在官方测试集（1000 件艺术品）上，ArtSociety 总体得分 0.8870（分类 0.7789，描述 0.9952）。十一变体消融实验表明，当方法与规模在约 0.76 处饱和后，决定性增益来自智能体协作与数据侧监督——用旧数据训练的 30B MoE 模型并不优于用更好数据训练的 8B 模型。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作与优质数据监督比单纯扩大模型规模更能带来性能提升，为多维艺术情感理解提供了有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The AffectiveArt Multidimensional Art Emotion Understanding task asks to jointly predict an artwork's fine-grained emotion (12 classes, 1549:1 head-to-tail ratio), binary valence/arousal, and five attribute-grounded descriptions -- sub-tasks that exhibit strong empirical trade-offs, so the single-model solutions we tried do not jointly optimize all of them well. We present ArtSociety, a multi-agent framework that assembles heterogeneous multimodal experts -- a DINOv2-Giant vision agent (A1), a scene-grounded CoT fine-tuned MLLM (A2), and three closed-source reasoning agents (A3-A5) -- and coordinates them with two training-free controllers: (i) a rare-class-aware voting arbiter that lowers the agreement threshold for tail emotions, exploiting decorrelated error patterns across agent families; and (ii) a description-first reasoning agent whose DESCRIBE-then-CLASSIFY chain of thought forces visual evidence before label commitment, yielding near-perfect grounded descriptions. A task-routing policy directs the hard emotion task to the full five-agent ensemble while assigning the near-saturated valence/arousal and generative description tasks to the single strongest reasoning agent. On the official test set (1,000 artworks), ArtSociety achieves an Overall Score of 0.8870 (Classification 0.7789, Description 0.9952). An eleven-variant ablation study reveals that, once method and scale saturate at around 0.76, the decisive gains come from agent collaboration and data-side supervision -- a 30B MoE model trained on older data does not outperform an 8B model trained on better data. Code is available at https://github.com/swordlidev/ArtSociety

</details>


### [7] [SJD-SV: Speculative Jacobi Decoding with Semantics Verification for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13245)
*Baoquan Zhang,Bingqi Shan,Shihao Fang,Kenghong Lin,Xutao Li,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义验证的投机Jacobi解码方法SJD-SV，通过语义感知子序列级验证解决视觉token歧义问题，加速自回归图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机Jacobi解码（SJD）在token验证阶段存在token歧义问题，但其原因尚未被很好地解释，限制了SJD的性能提升。

Method: 首先对视觉token进行可视化分析，发现视觉token通常对应局部、细小、模糊的视觉细节，单个token难以准确表达语义。基于此提出SJD-SV：利用token间的强纠正特性识别语义感知的token子序列，将验证从token级转为语义感知子序列级。该方法是插件式的，可直接集成到现有SJD及其变体中。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，集成SJD-SV后，现有SJD方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 视觉token的局部模糊特性是导致SJD token歧义的根本原因，采用语义感知子序列级验证可有效缓解该问题并加速自回归图像生成。

Abstract: Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) is an important approach for accelerating autoregressive image generation. Although SJD has shown superior performance, recent studies point out that it usually suffers from a token ambiguity issue during token verification but its reason can not be well explained. To figure out this reason, in this paper, we conduct a visualization analysis on vision token and find that different from text tokens, vision tokens generally corresponds to some local, small, and unclear vision details, which means only using single token is difficult to accurately express a certain semantic, thereby causing token ambiguity issue. To this end, we propose a novel Speculative Jacobi Decoding with Semantics Verification (called SJD-SV), for accelerating autoregressive image generation. The key idea is that leveraging the strong correction characters between tokens to recognize semantic-aware token subsequence and then instead of perform token-by-token verification, turning to perform verification on semantic-aware token subsequence level for accelerating image generation. In particular, our method is plug-in, which can be directly integrated into existing SJD and its variants. Extensive experiments on various datasets show that existing SJD methods achieve significant performance improvement after integrating our SJD-SV method.

</details>


### [8] [Pixel-wise Planarity for High-Precision Monocular Plane Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13246)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Alpay Ozkan,Rémi Pautrat,Shaohui Liu,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出逐像素平面性预测框架，结合深度、法向量与平面性置信度进行单目平面分割，提升几何精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张RGB图像的平面分割面临区域分组不精确与几何不一致监督问题，常导致过分割和错误平面检测。

Method: 基于预训练单目几何骨干网络（预测深度和表面法向量），引入专门的平面性头估计逐像素平面性置信度；推理时通过轻量区域生长过程结合深度、法向量和平面性，强制几何一致性来形成平面段。

Result: 分析现有平面真值标注，揭示在严格距离阈值下存在显著几何不一致；在多个数据集上相比先前SOTA实现更优几何精度和分割质量，同时提升计算效率。

Conclusion: 逐像素平面性预测框架能有效提升单目平面分割的鲁棒性，兼顾精度与效率，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Plane segmentation from a single RGB image remains challenging due to imprecise region grouping and geometrically inconsistent supervision, often leading to over-segmentation and false planar detections. We propose instead a pixel-wise planarity prediction framework for robust monocular plane segmentation. Building on a pretrained monocular geometric backbone predicting depth and surface normals, we introduce a dedicated planarity head that estimates per-pixel planarity confidence. During inference, predicted depth, normals, and planarity are combined in a lightweight region-growing procedure that enforces geometric consistency when forming plane segments. We further analyze existing plane ground-truth annotations and demonstrate substantial geometric inconsistencies under strict distance thresholds. Across multiple datasets, our method achieves improved geometric precision and segmentation quality compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches, while improving computational efficiency. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/PixelwisePlanarity.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluation of MLLM-Agnostic Plug-and-Play Keyframe Selection Methods for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13250)
*Dilip Sarkar,Md. Safayet Islam,Liang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对五种无需训练的即插即用（PaP）关键帧选择方法进行了统一评测：在3个MLLM和3个长视频理解基准上共15组聚合设置中，QAaF在其中13组表现最佳，FOCUS总体排名第二，为这类方法提供了共同的实验参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型受视觉token与计算预算限制，无法处理长视频的每一帧。已有三类增强长视频理解的方案：重训练/扩展输入长度、为特定MLLM训练适配器、以及无需训练且与MLLM无关的即插即用（PaP）关键帧选择。前两类成本高，PaP成本最低且通用性最强，但过去一年仅报道了五种PaP方法，且它们在不同基准、不同MLLM上评测，缺乏可比性，因此需要一个统一的评测基准。

Method: 作者对现有五种无需训练的PaP关键帧选择方法进行全面的横向评测，统一使用3个多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和3个长视频理解基准，合计构成15组聚合评测设置，从而在同一实验条件下比较各方法的性能。

Result: 在15组聚合评测设置中，QAaF在其中13组取得最佳性能，FOCUS总体排名第二，其余方法表现相对较弱。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了无需训练的MLLM关键帧选择方法的统一比较结果，为后续研究提供了共同的实验参考，并表明QAaF与FOCUS是当前表现最优的两种PaP方法。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) cannot process every frame of a long video because of limitations in visual-token and computational budgets. Three primary approaches have been proposed to enhance their long-video understanding capabilities: (i) Retraining an MLLM on a large video corpus and/or extending its input length; (ii) Training an adapter for a specific MLLM that takes the entire video and the query as input and selects the most relevant video frames; and (iii) Developing a training-free, plug-and-play (PaP) adapter that is MLLM-agnostic. We refer to the third approach as PaP keyframe selection. A PaP method may use only candidate video frames without considering the query, or it may use both candidate video frames and the query. The first approach is prohibitively expensive. The second approach requires substantial training time and computational resources, but it is accessible to many because an adapter contains significantly fewer trainable parameters than an entire MLLM. The third approach has the lowest computational cost and is therefore broadly accessible. To the best of our knowledge, only five PaP methods have been reported within the past year. All of these methods have been evaluated on one or more video question-answering benchmarks and have demonstrated improvements in long-video understanding. However, the methods were evaluated on different benchmarks using different MLLMs. We present a comprehensive evaluation of these five methods using three MLLMs across three long-video understanding benchmarks. Our results show that QAaF achieves the best performance in 13 of the 15 aggregate evaluation settings, while FOCUS ranks second overall. These results provide a common experimental reference for comparing training-free keyframe-selection methods for MLLMs.

</details>


### [10] [Preserving Subject-Clarity in Image Outpainting with Multiscale Wavelet Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13251)
*Abhilash Neog,Taewan Kim,Yi Wu,Xu Chen,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出主题清晰度外扩框架，结合VLM语义引导与小波监督，提升商业广告图像的扩展质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业与广告图像常因构图不当、主体被裁切、文字或logo被截断及上下文缺失，导致主题清晰度下降。现有基于扩散的外扩方法虽能生成视觉上合理的补全，却会通过结构不一致、语义漂移或细节丢失降低主题保真度。

Method: 提出主题清晰度外扩框架：以视觉语言模型(VLM)引导的语义条件化，配合多尺度小波监督实现主题局部细节保持；并设计以主题为中心的数据构建流程，从广告与自然图像中构造与主体相交的外扩图像对。该目标不增加推理成本，可与扩散骨干网络兼容。

Result: 在四个广告与自然图像基准上，方法提升了主题清晰度：相比匹配的监督微调，主题中心DreamSim误差与FID平均降低3.0%和2.4%；相比各数据集上最强的SOTA方法，分别降低10.8%和7.7%。

Conclusion: VLM语义引导与多尺度小波监督相结合，可在不增加推理开销的前提下有效改善外扩图像的主题清晰度，优于现有监督微调与SOTA方法。

Abstract: Commercial and advertising images are frequently affected by poor framing, partially cropped subjects, truncated text or logos, and insufficient context, all of which can reduce subject clarity, i.e., the ability of an image to clearly communicate its primary subject. Image outpainting offers a scalable solution by extending image boundaries and recovering missing content and context. However, existing diffusion-based outpainting methods often produce visually plausible completions while degrading subject fidelity through structural inconsistencies, semantic drift, or loss of fine-grained detail. To address this limitation, we propose a subject clarity outpainting framework that combines vision-language model (VLM)-guided semantic conditioning with multiscale wavelet supervision for subject-localized detail preservation. To support training, we develop a subject-centric data curation pipeline that constructs subject-intersecting outpainting pairs from advertising and natural images. The resulting objective introduces no additional inference cost and is designed to be compatible with diffusion-based backbones. Across four advertising and natural-image benchmarks, our method improves subject clarity, reducing subject-centered DreamSim error and FID on average by 3.0% and 2.4% over matched supervised fine-tuning, and by 10.8% and 7.7% over the strongest state-of-the-art approach per dataset, respectively.

</details>


### [11] [(How) Do MLLMs Report Bistable Images Like Humans?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13254)
*Ryota Takatsuki,Tomoki Doi,Amane Watahiki,Anil K. Seth,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）处理双稳态图像时是否表现出与人类相似的报告行为及其内部计算机制。以LLaVA系列为对象，发现模型报告可受自下而上视觉线索和自上而下语言先验影响，且反应以单一解释（排他性）为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双稳态图像（如鸭兔图）是人类经典的心理物理学刺激，同一图像支持多种互不兼容的解释，人类通常一次只报告一种。作者希望探究MLLMs是否表现出类似的报告行为，以及背后支撑这种行为的内部计算。

Method: 使用LLaVA系列模型，从两个可处理维度入手：可调制性（报告能否受自下而上视觉线索与自上而下语言先验影响）和排他性（回答是否只承诺单一解释）。测试对象包括经典鸭兔图及合成的Visual Anagrams，以减少记忆带来的混淆。

Result: 行为层面，视觉与语言操纵均以与人类一致的方式系统性地改变模型报告，且回答仍以排他性为主。机制层面，这些效应源自相互竞争的图像token表征、自下而上与自上而下调制的不同通路，以及排他性报告与物体计数编码之间的联系。

Conclusion: MLLMs在处理双稳态图像时表现出与人类一致的报告特征（可调制且排他），其背后有可识别的内部表征与通路机制支撑，为理解多模态模型的视觉解释过程提供了行为与机制证据。

Abstract: Bistable images such as the duck-rabbit are classic stimuli in which one image supports multiple mutually incompatible interpretations, typically reported one at a time in humans. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show similar report behavior and what internal computations support it. Using the LLaVA family, we study two tractable dimensions: modulability, whether reports can be biased by bottom-up visual cues and top-down linguistic priors, and exclusivity, whether responses commit to a single interpretation. We test both on the canonical duck-rabbit and on synthetic Visual Anagrams to mitigate memorization confounds. Behaviorally, both visual and linguistic manipulations systematically shift reports in human-consistent ways, while responses remain predominantly exclusive. Mechanistically, these effects arise from competing image-token representations, distinct pathways for bottom-up and top-down modulation, and a link between exclusive reporting and object-count encoding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/rtakatsky/mllm-bistable-images.

</details>


### [12] [A Comprehensive Review of Multimodal Facial State Analysis: Tasks, Methods, and Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13255)
*Xuri Ge,Tianshuo Zhang,Ruihan Li,Hui Ye,Kaiwen Zheng,Junchen Fu,Da Huo,Joemon M. Jose,Hu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦多模态面部状态分析，强调多模态学习带来上下文语义理解与可解释性，多任务学习实现表情、动作单元与软生物特征联合分析，并梳理核心任务、方法与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模态视觉方法受环境敏感性影响大、可解释性弱，亟需借助多模态互补信息提升面部状态分析能力。

Method: 综述性研究：围绕两个关键方面展开——(1) 多模态学习用于上下文语义理解、提升推理并利用可解释语言生成增强模型可解释性；(2) 多任务学习同时分析表情、动作单元(AU)与人脸软生物特征(如年龄、性别)。

Result: 系统回顾了多模态面部状态分析中的核心任务、代表性方法与数据集，重点覆盖面部表情识别、AU检测及人脸软生物特征估计，并突出语言在提供上下文语义、增强推理与生成解释方面的独特价值。

Conclusion: 旨在提供该领域最新文献概览，并指出面向多模态、可解释与多任务自适应面部状态分析的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Facial state analysis plays a crucial role in understanding human expressions, psychological modeling, and human computer interaction. Traditional unimodal vision-based methods are often limited by environmental sensitivity and weak interpretability. Multimodal facial state analysis addresses these issues by integrating complementary cues from visual, audio, textual, physiological, and other related modalities. This survey emphasizes two key aspects: on one hand, multimodal learning enables contextual semantic understanding for improved facial state reasoning and leverages interpretable language generation to enhance model explainability; on the other hand, multi-task learning allows simultaneous analysis of expressions, action units (AUs), and face-based soft biometrics (e.g., age, gender), effectively capturing fine-grained expressions and improving cross-scene generalization. This survey reviews core tasks, representative methods, and datasets in multimodal facial state analysis, focusing on facial expression recognition, AU detection, and face-based soft biometric estimation, and emphasizing the unique value of language in providing contextual semantics, enhancing reasoning, and generating explanations. The survey aims to provide an up-to-date overview of the literature and to highlight future research directions for multimodal, interpretable, and multi-task adaptive facial state analysis.

</details>


### [13] [Sampling headroom is not selection gain: a compute-value audit of test-time scaling for video world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13257)
*Yuhua Jiang,Junjie Lu,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Compute-Value Audit (CVA)框架，检验测试时扩展(TTS)带来的采样余量能否转化为可靠的部署收益，发现在视频世界模型中存在"采样余量≠选择增益"的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS仅当额外计算能产生更好候选、且系统能可靠识别它们时才有效。视频世界模型中，更大的采样池可能包含更强的rollout，却无法改善最终被选中的输出。作者希望区分"采样是否创造了机会"与"机会能否被转化为收益"。

Method: 提出Compute-Value Audit (CVA)，一个顺序框架，检验四个阶段：额外采样是否创造机会、可观测信号是否提供可靠状态、该状态是否支持有益动作、最终增益是否超过生成与验证的完整"入场费"。实验在Physics-IQ的192个场景上进行，对比Flow、Cycle、VideoReward等验证器，测试三种生成器上的十二种自适应深度策略，并在VideoPhy2上做匹配60-NFE的Predict-and-Perturb干预，另设PRM800K、MMLU-Pro和特权配对未来作为对照。

Result: Physics-IQ上候选池从4扩到16使oracle质量提升+9.23 IQ（95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]），但Flow、Cycle、VideoReward均无法可靠回收这一余量；三种生成器的十二种自适应深度策略无一优于均匀计算，仅回收42-69%的入场费；VideoPhy2的匹配干预在三个新种子副本上同样为负。对照实验中，anchor-explorer在稀疏PRM800K上通过全部四阶段，MMLU-Pro暴露预测状态与有用动作之间的差距，特权配对未来确立了正向的视频上界。

Conclusion: 采样余量只有能被转化为可靠决策、且收益能覆盖全部计算成本时，才具有部署价值。视频世界模型中TTS的收益瓶颈在于选择而非采样。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) can improve generation only when additional compute produces better candidates and the system can reliably identify them. This distinction is especially important for video world models, where a wider sample pool may contain stronger rollouts without improving the output that is ultimately selected. We introduce the Compute-Value Audit (CVA), a sequential framework that asks whether extra sampling creates opportunity, observable signals provide a reliable state, that state supports a beneficial action, and the resulting gain exceeds the full entry fee of generation and verification. On 192 Physics-IQ scenes, expanding the pool from 4 to 16 candidates increases oracle quality by +9.23 IQ (95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]), but Flow, Cycle, and VideoReward fail to recover this headroom reliably. Across three generators, none of twelve adaptive-depth policies outperforms uniform compute; they recover only 42-69% of the measured entry fee. A matched-60-NFE Predict-and-Perturb intervention on VideoPhy2 is likewise negative across three fresh-seed replicas. These negative results are not universal: anchor-explorer passes all four stages in a sparse PRM800K setting, MMLU-Pro exposes the gap between predictive state and useful action, and a privileged paired future establishes a positive video upper bound. Together, these results show that sampling headroom has deployment value only when it can be converted into a reliable decision whose benefit survives the complete compute charge. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/sampling-headroom-is-not-selection-gain.

</details>


### [14] [Interpretable Temporal Video Reasoning with EventGraph and EventField](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13258)
*Durgendra Narayan Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于离散EventGraph、连续EventField与可读EventGlyph的结构化时序视频推理流程，在EPIC-KITCHENS校准子集上准确率达0.98，显著优于字幕基线与VLM直接问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频时序推理需要兼顾性能与可解释性，而现有字幕类描述和端到端VLM问答难以同时保留符号结构与时间连续性，也缺乏人类可读的诊断信息。

Method: 构建由离散EventGraph、连续EventField和人类可读EventGlyph视图组成的三层结构化时序推理流程；在10个视频、50个时序推理问题构成的校准EPIC-KITCHENS子集上评测，并比较人工、启发式及启发式+Gemini等不同标注来源设置，同时进行跨视频配对基准测试。

Result: EventField+Glyph整体准确率为0.98，较字幕基线提升+0.40（配对p=1.1×10^-5），较VLM直接问答提升+0.20（p=0.0063）；在各标注来源设置下，最佳结构化方法均持续优于字幕基线。

Conclusion: 结构化时序表示能够同时支持性能与可审查性：通过保留符号结构、捕捉时间连续性并提供人类可读诊断，为视频推理提供更可靠且可解释的方案。

Abstract: We present a structured temporal video reasoning pipeline built around a discrete EventGraph, a continuous EventField, and a human-readable EventGlyph view. On a calibrated EPIC-KITCHENS subset of 10 videos and 50 temporal reasoning questions, EventField+Glyph achieves 0.98 overall accuracy, which is higher than the caption baseline by +0.40 (paired p = 1.1 \times 10^{-5}) and direct VLM-only QA by +0.20 (p = 0.0063) on this subset. We further evaluate annotation-source variations, including manual, heuristic, and heuristic+Gemini pipelines, and find that the best structured method stays above the caption baseline across settings. We also include cross-video pair benchmarking and an appendix gallery of glyph outputs for all studied videos. Overall, the results indicate that structured temporal representations can support both performance and inspectability by preserving symbolic structure, capturing temporal continuity, and providing human-readable diagnostics for video reasoning.

</details>


### [15] [TryOnReward: Learning Foveated Consistency for Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13259)
*Xueheng Li,Yong Liu,Xiaolong Fu,Wen Xue,Chengjun Xie,Yipeng Sun,Yan Li,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TryOnReward，一种面向虚拟试穿(VTON)的细粒度奖励模型，通过注视校准目标与成对偏好+分维度打分联合优化，显著提升与人类偏好的一致性，并在强化微调中抑制奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTON 目标是生成符合人类偏好的试穿结果，但经典保真度指标与人类判断相关性弱，通用 VLM 缺乏试穿质量评估所需的细粒度判别力（需忠实保留服装与人像细节），在强化微调(RFT)中会导致严重的奖励黑客。

Method: 基于视觉-语言骨干构建细粒度奖励模型；采用注视(foveation)校准目标，将每个质量维度锚定到相关区域以避免全局捷径学习；通过 margin-aware 监督联合优化成对偏好与分维度质量分数，同时利用相对与绝对质量信号。并构建 TryOnReward-100K 人工标注分维度评分数据集，以及 TryOn-Bench、TryOnRewardBench 两个基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，TryOnReward 在人类偏好对齐上显著优于通用评判模型；作为 RFT 奖励函数时，在多个基线上持续生成人类更偏好的试穿结果。

Conclusion: TryOnReward 通过细粒度、区域锚定的奖励建模有效解决 VTON 偏好对齐与奖励黑客问题，可作为 VTON 强化微调的可靠奖励函数。

Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to dress a person with the reference garment, producing visually reasonable results aligned with human preferences. Turning this preference-oriented goal into an actionable objective relies on a scoring function aligned with human taste. However, classic fidelity metrics exhibit weak correlation with human judgments, and generic VLMs fail to provide the discriminative granularity demanded by try-on quality evaluation, which hinges on faithfully preserving garment and person details. This shortcoming is further exacerbated in the reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) optimization and leads to severe reward hacking. To this end, we present TryOnReward, a fine-grained reward model tailored for VTON. Built on a vision-language backbone, it adopts a foveation calibration objective that grounds each quality dimension in the relevant region to avoid global shortcut learning. Meanwhile, TryOnReward jointly optimizes pairwise preferences and per-dimension quality scores via margin-aware supervision, leveraging both relative and absolute quality signals. For model training and evaluation, we build TryOnReward-100K, a human-annotated per-dimension rating dataset, alongside TryOn-Bench and TryOnRewardBench, two benchmarks covering diverse real scenarios. Extensive experiments confirm that TryOnReward significantly outperforms generic judges in human preference alignment, and when serving as the RFT reward function, it consistently yields human-preferred try-on results across multiple baselines.

</details>


### [16] [Filling the Unseen: Scene Extrapolation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13262)
*Yunlai Zhou,Yiren Lu,Tuo Liang,Disheng Liu,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个整体框架解决3D高斯泼溅在分布外新视角上的外推与内插退化问题，通过独立相机视角检测实现并行无冲突外推，并设计即插即用的质量感知掩码（QA-Mask）选择性利用生成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在训练视角内可达到照片级重建，但在分布外新视角上性能退化，表现为未观测区域出现空洞、可观测区域出现伪影。现有工作将任务分解为外推与内插并借助生成模型解决，但外推尺度与质量受限，且依赖"生成-重建-移位"循环逐步构建场景，每步都以先前结果为条件，导致误差累积。此外现有方法忽略生成图像与原始图像之间的不一致，损害了原本重建良好的区域。

Method: 1) 设计独立的相机视角检测机制，实现并行且无冲突的外推，规避易出错的循环流程；2) 在此基础之上构建分层流水线，将独立相机视角与依赖相机视角分开外推；3) 提出即插即用的质量感知掩码（QA-Mask）模块，以像素级渲染质量校准学习权重，实现生成数据的选择性利用，避免生成引入的退化。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架性能优越，且QA-Mask可泛化到多种生成式重建模型。

Conclusion: 该工作通过并行无冲突外推与质量感知掩码的统一框架，同时提升了外推与内插的重建质量，并具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting achieves photorealistic reconstruction within training view distribution, yet it degrades on out-of-distribution novel views, exhibiting holes in unobserved regions and artifacts in observable areas. Recent works formulate this task as extrapolation and interpolation and try to address it with generative models, but remain limited in extrapolation scale and quality. They repeat a generate-reconstruct-shift cycle to progressively build a scene, which introduces accumulated errors with every step conditioning on previous outcomes. In this work, we propose a holistic framework for extrapolation and interpolation. We devise an independent camera view detection mechanism to enable parallel conflict-free extrapolation, circumventing the reliance on the aforementioned error-prone cycle. Building upon this, we design a hierarchical pipeline that extrapolates independent and dependent camera views separately. Additionally, previous methods overlook inconsistency between generated and original images, resulting in compromising well-reconstructed areas. We propose a plug-and-play Quality-Aware Mask (QA-Mask) module, enabling selective utilization of generated data. By calibrating learning weights with pixel-wise rendering quality, it prevents generation-induced degradations on well-constructed areas. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of our framework, with QA-Mask generalizing to multiple generative reconstruction models.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-View Structure-from-Motion Enables Oriented Projective Shape Analysis in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13263)
*Musab Alamoudi,Robert L. Paige,Vic Patrangenaru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次实现三维有向投影形状（OPS）分析，用多视角SfM重建替代两视图立体重建，对一个经典三立方体物体（q=14个非框架地标）进行蓝图假设检验，发现SfM重建精度显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影形状（PS）分析中，单对未标定立体图像只能重建到相差一个三维投影变换，且可能反转方向，因此只能使用"符号盲"的PS摘要。多视角SfM的捆绑调整可消除方向反转问题，但此前尚缺乏三维OPS的统计分析方法。

Method: 用Agisoft Metashape Professional 2.3.0对经典三立方体物体构建n=8个SfM重建，并通过复现原数据的立体构造验证实现正确性；在OPS框架下计算外在总方差指标并进行统计推断和蓝图假设检验；开展样本量、照片数量和框架顺序的稳健性分析。

Result: 由于SfM数据高度集中，OPS指标渐近为PS指标的一半（是集中度的结构性结果而非物体属性）；q=14个非框架地标的蓝图假设均未被拒绝；SfM重建的集中度约为立体重建的26倍，重建精度大幅提升。

Conclusion: 本文实现了首个三维有向投影形状分析，验证了SfM在投影形状推断中相对立体重建的显著精度优势，样本量、照片数量和框架顺序分析均支持结论的稳健性。

Abstract: Projective shape analysis provides a geometric framework for studying landmark configurations in digital images acquired by pinhole cameras. In the classical projective shape (PS) model, three-dimensional configurations ($k$-ads) are represented as points in $(\mathrm{RP}^3)^q$, $q = k - 5$. A nonparametric test is developed in Patrangenaru et al. [12], for this framework, to determine whether an object matches a design blueprint, with each configuration reconstructed from a single uncalibrated stereo pair. Such two-view reconstructions are identified only up to a 3D projective transformation, which may reverse orientation, so that the sign-blind PS summary was the only available, before oriented projective shape (OPS) was recently considered. Multi-view Structure-from-Motion (SfM) technology removes this obstruction: its bundle adjustment is identified up to an orientation-preserving projective transformation. In this paper we revisit a well-cited three-cube object, from Patrangenaru et al. [12], with $n = 8$ SfM reconstructions built in Agisoft Metashape Professional 2.3.0, and validate our implementation by reproducing the published stereo construction from the original data. This allows us to perform what is to the best of our knowledge the first three-dimensional OPS analysis, compute its extrinsic total-variance index and perform statistical inference in this novel setting. Due to the high concentration of SfM data, the OPS index is asymptotically one-half the PS index, a structural consequence of concentration rather than a property of the object. Here our blueprint hypothesis is not rejected for any of the $q = 14$ non-frame landmarks, while the SfM reconstructions are about 26 times more concentrated than the stereo ones, a substantial gain in reconstruction precision. Sample-size, photograph-count, and frame-ordering analyses support the robustness of these conclusions.

</details>


### [18] [BEACON: Behavior and Appearance Control for Subject-Specific Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13264)
*Pokrzywa Baptiste,Nabyl Quignon,Yara Bahram,Muhammad Osama Zeeshan,Antitza Dantcheva,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEACON 是一个轻量级框架，通过将视觉身份与表达行为解耦，同时以参考图像和参考视频为条件，实现更具表现力的个人专属视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的视频生成需要同时保持视觉身份和个人特有的表达行为，但现有方法多以单张参考图像为条件，缺乏时间动态信息，导致身份保真但表情变化有限、主体特异性弱。

Method: BEACON 将视觉身份与表达行为解耦，联合条件于两个互补信号：编码身份的参考图像和捕捉主体面部动态的参考视频，并支持身份-表情迁移；仅微调约 2000 对数据、更新约 1% 的预训练 Wan 视频扩散模型参数。

Result: 在 MEAD 和 RAVDESS 数据集上，BEACON 相比最先进的视频生成方法提升了面部表现力，同时保持了有竞争力的身份保真度。

Conclusion: 通过解耦身份与表达行为并引入视频动态条件，BEACON 以极低的微调成本实现了更富表现力的个人专属视频生成，兼顾身份保持与身份-表情迁移。

Abstract: Generating human-centric videos that preserve both visual identity and person-specific expressive behavior remains a fundamental challenge. In addition to reproducing appearance, a model must replicate the facial behaviors that characterize how a subject expresses emotion over time. However, most state-of-the-art methods condition generation on a single reference image, which contains no information about these temporal dynamics. As a result, they tend to preserve the subject's visual identity but often produce expressions with limited variation and weak subject specificity. To mitigate this issue, we introduce BEACON, a lightweight framework for person-specific video generation that produces more expressive videos by disentangling visual identity from expressive behavior. BEACON conditions generation on two complementary signals: a reference image encoding the identity and a reference video capturing subject-specific facial dynamics. By conditioning on these complementary signals, BEACON generates videos that better preserve both the subject's appearance and characteristic facial dynamics, while also supporting identity-expression transfer. Our experiments on the MEAD and RAVDESS datasets show that by fine-tuning on approximately 2,000 pairs and updating about 1% of the pretrained Wan video diffusion model, BEACON improves facial expressivity over state-of-the-art video generation methods while maintaining competitive identity preservation.

</details>


### [19] [Forward-Facing Near-Infrared Adds Little to Colour for Farm-Machinery Traversability: A Site-Disjoint Evaluation of Sensor-Dependent Spatial Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13265)
*Sungwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 消除空间数据泄漏后，近红外（NIR）成像在白天农业可通行性任务上并不优于标准彩色相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基准显示NIR具有优势，但这些基准采用序列级划分，导致空间自相关图像泄漏进测试集，人为抬高了NIR表现，尤其在困难的田埂边界分割任务上，因而需要重新评估该结论。

Method: 在AI Hub自动驾驶语料库上，采用严格留出的记录地点进行评估，比较四种配置（彩色、NIR、亮度对照及其融合），并在旋转留出地点时检验结果的可复现性。

Result: 四种配置在五个可通行性类别中均无法稳定超越标准彩色；单一时点的表观性能提升在轮换留出地点后无法复现。校正空间泄漏后，NIR的表观领先完全消失，而非所有传感器类型均匀性能下降。

Conclusion: 在白天农业车辆传感器套件中增加前向NIR相机仍缺乏证据支持；受地点泄漏影响的评估会扭曲传感器排序并误导采购决策。

Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR) imaging does not consistently outperform standard color cameras for daytime agricultural traversability once spatial data leakage is eliminated. Prior benchmarks suggesting an NIR advantage used sequence-level splits that permitted spatially autocorrelated imagery into test sets, artificially inflating NIR performance, especially on difficult paddy-boundary segmentation.
  When evaluated across strictly held-out recording sites using the AI Hub autonomous driving corpus, none of the four tested configurations (color, NIR, a luminance control, or their fusion) reliably surpasses standard color across any of the five traversability classes. Furthermore, apparent performance gains observed at a single held-out site completely fail to replicate when the held-out site is rotated, demonstrating that the observed benefits were site-specific artifacts rather than generalizable improvements.
  Correcting for spatial leakage erases the apparent lead of NIR entirely rather than uniformly degrading performance across all sensor types. Consequently, adding a forward-facing NIR camera to a daylight agricultural vehicle sensor suite remains unproven, and evaluations compromised by location leakage risk distorting sensor rankings and misleading procurement decisions.

</details>


### [20] [Structure-Token Evidence-Anchored Reasoning for Scientific Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13267)
*Alberlucia Rafael Soarez,Camila Ferreira,Daniel Kim,Mariana Costa,Alejandro Torres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STEER框架，通过冻结视觉编码器并插入图表结构图编码、证据锚定推理和弱解析强推理对齐三个模块，显式绑定坐标框架与推理步骤，提升科学图表问答的推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型仍将科学图表当作自然照片处理，坐标框架未被显式暴露，视觉上下文示例无法传达坐标信息，而无约束的思维链会臆造从未从图表中读取的数值。

Method: STEER冻结Llama-3.2-Vision编码器，插入三个模块：(1)图表结构图编码器CSGE，绑定刻度、图例项与图形标记；(2)证据锚定分步推理EASR，强制每一步算术推理引用图节点；(3)弱解析器强推理器对齐WPSR，将专用表格抽取器仅用作节点属性的教师模型。

Result: 在ChartQA上达到82.70平均宽松准确率，优于ChartGemma的80.16和同数据LLaVA-CoT骨干的76.40；在CharXiv推理上33.60对InternVL Chat V1.5的29.20，ChartQAPro CoT上40.70对Qwen2-VL-7B的37.17。消融显示去除节点序列化或数值候选约束会大幅削弱推理增益。

Conclusion: 显式的图表结构建模与证据锚定的推理约束能有效抑制OCR捷径与数值臆造，在多个图表推理基准上稳定超越现有模型。

Abstract: Scientific charts encode quantities in axes, legends, and geometric marks, yet large vision-language models still treat them as natural photographs. Visual in-context examples do not expose the coordinate frame; unconstrained chain-of-thought can name a plausible number that was never read from a bar. We present STEER (Structure-Token Evidence-anchored Reasoning), which freezes a Llama-3.2-Vision encoder and inserts three modules: a chart structure graph encoder (CSGE) that binds ticks, legend items, and marks; evidence-anchored step reasoning (EASR) that forces every arithmetic step to cite a graph node; and weak-parser strong-reasoner alignment (WPSR) that uses a specialized table extractor only as a teacher of node attributes. On ChartQA, STEER reaches 82.70 average relaxed accuracy versus 80.16 for ChartGemma and 76.40 for a LLaVA-CoT backbone trained on the same mix. Gains widen on CharXiv reasoning (33.60 vs. 29.20 InternVL Chat V1.5) and ChartQAPro CoT (40.70 vs. 37.17 Qwen2-VL-7B), where OCR shortcuts disappear. Ablations show that dropping node serialization or numeric candidate constraints undoes most of the reasoning lift.

</details>


### [21] [Capability-Routed Visual Retrieval and Evidence Threading for Long-Context Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13268)
*Amirul Rahman,Aisha Karim,Kenji Nakamura,Yi-Fan Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在冻结的Qwen2.5-VL-7B生成器与ColPali/VisRAG页面索引之上插入CAVR、WSPS、VET三个模块，面向年报、图表与信息图文档的视觉检索增强问答，在DocVQA/ChartQA/InfographicVQA及VisRAG、MMLongBench-Doc、ViDoRe等基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年报、尽职调查报告和信息图仪表盘把关键数字埋在页面图像中（坐标轴、单元格网格、脚注），OCR流水线会将其压平，而页面级视觉检索器仍把这些内容当作可互换的上下文样例来处理，导致检索与生成质量受限。

Method: 保持Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct生成器与ColPali/VisRAG-Ret页面索引冻结，新增三个模块：(1)能力感知视觉路由器CAVR，将检索页标记为文本、表格、图表、布局或混合类型，并在生成前混合专家；(2)弱到强页面选择WSPS，将冻结的7B可回答性教师蒸馏到3B选择头，使排序不再依赖单一InfoNCE分数；(3)视觉证据串联VET，构建长度不超过三的布局锚定路径，让生成器读取证据链而非平坦的top-k列表。

Result: 在gold-page设定下，DocVQA/ChartQA/InfographicVQA分别达到96.3/90.1/85.4；VisRAG top-3协议下平均生成准确率为62.74，优于同骨干拼接方式的59.39；MMLongBench-Doc检索后阅读设定中F1由19.2升至22.6，多页准确率由16.4升至21.2；ViDoRe经WSPS重排后nDCG@5为83.6，TAT-DQA财务报告为70.4。

Conclusion: 通过能力感知路由、弱到强页面选择与视觉证据串联三种即插即用模块，可在不微调生成器与索引的前提下显著提升复杂视觉文档的检索与问答性能，验证了面向页面图像结构化证据的检索增强范式的有效性。

Abstract: Annual reports, diligence packs, and infographic dashboards bury numbers in page images: axes, cell grids, and footnotes that OCR pipelines flatten and that page-level visual retrievers still treat as interchangeable in-context examples. We keep a frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct generator and a ColPali / VisRAG-Ret page index, and insert three modules. A capability-aware visual router (CAVR) tags each retrieved page as text, table, chart, layout, or mixed and mixes specialist experts before generation. Weak-to-strong page selection (WSPS) distils a frozen 7B answerability teacher into a 3B selection head so ranking is no longer a single InfoNCE score. Visual evidence threading (VET) builds layout-anchored paths of length at most three and lets the generator read the thread rather than a flat top-$k$ list. On gold-page DocVQA / ChartQA / InfographicVQA the 7B system reaches 96.3 / 90.1 / 85.4. Under the VisRAG top-3 protocol the mean generation accuracy is 62.74 versus 59.39 for the same backbone with concatenation. On MMLongBench-Doc retrieve-then-read, F1 moves from 19.2 to 22.6 and multi-page accuracy from 16.4 to 21.2. ViDoRe nDCG@5 after WSPS reranking is 83.6, with TAT-DQA financial reports at 70.4.

</details>


### [22] [Frame-Synchronous Hand Gesture Detection by Projected Winding Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13269)
*Amey Thakur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对手势同步问题，提出一种无需分类器、无需训练数据、无需标定的精确解：将手掌绕长轴旋转的检测转化为一个标量的零交叉，从而给出精确时刻而非区间，并证明该判据对镜像、手部尺度与左右手均不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频手势识别通常被当作分类问题（逐帧打标签再执行动作）。这对控制足够（命令延迟几帧用户不易察觉），但对同步不足——同步要求输出对齐到手势实际发生的那一帧。作者以"张开手掌绕其长轴旋转"这一常见动作为例，研究同步问题，并希望得到无需分类器、训练数据和标定的精确解。

Method: 令 s 为手腕处两掌边与外指节在相机投影下的归一化二维叉积。作者证明 s 可分解为 k(theta)cos(theta) 且处处 |k|>0，因此 s 恰在手掌侧向（edge-on）时为零，其符号指示朝向相机的面。于是检测转化为单标量的零交叉，得到瞬时时刻而非区间，并从代数形式（而非依赖关键点估计器）证明判据对图像镜像、手部尺度与左右手不变。双手交互部分用四个指尖作为同一场景风格化版本的窗口，给出在双手交叉时仍正确的覆盖谓词（常规三角剖分在此失效），并从噪声统计而非人工观察推导三个风格化算子的每个自由参数。

Result: 在具有精确真值的语料上，该判据检测到 95% 的翻转，在 240 个近失误序列中无假阳性；平均将每个翻转定位到其发生时刻 6.7 ms 以内，为手部观测间隔的六分之一，而报告括号采样点为 23.0 ms。比采样更精细地报告事件，源于将手势视为连续量的零点而非标签。

Conclusion: 将手势视为连续量的零点而非离散标签，可以在不借助分类器、训练数据与标定的情况下，以亚采样精度精确同步检测手势事件（本文以手掌长轴旋转为例）。

Abstract: Gesture recognition on video is normally posed as classification: label each frame, then act on the label. That is adequate for control, where a command may be obeyed several frames late without a user noticing, and inadequate for synchronisation, where an output must be aligned to the frame on which the gesture physically occurred. We take the synchronisation problem for one common movement, the rotation of an open hand about its long axis, and show that it admits an exact solution needing no classifier, no training data and no calibration.
  Let s be the normalised two-dimensional cross product of the two palm edges at the wrist and the outer knuckles, under the projection the camera already performs. We prove that s factorises as k(theta)cos(theta) with |k| > 0 everywhere, so s vanishes exactly when the palm is edge-on and its sign tracks the face presented to the camera. Detection is therefore a zero crossing of one scalar, which yields an instant rather than an interval, and we prove the criterion invariant to image mirroring, hand scale and handedness, all from its algebraic form rather than from the landmark estimator.
  A two-handed interaction uses four fingertips as a window onto a restyled version of the same scene. We give a coverage predicate that stays correct when the hands cross, where the usual triangulation does not, and derive each free parameter of the three stylisation operators from a noise statistic rather than by inspection.
  Against a corpus with exact ground truth the criterion detects 95% of flips with no false positive in 240 near-miss sequences, and places each within 6.7 ms on average of the instant it occurred, a sixth of the interval at which the hand is observed, against 23.0 ms for reporting the bracketing sample. Reporting an event more finely than one samples follows from treating a gesture as the zero of a continuous quantity rather than a label.

</details>


### [23] [CANAL: Channel-Aware Noise Allocation for Differentially Private Feature Distillation in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13271)
*Armaghan Butt,Shuya Feng,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私特征蒸馏中的三个被忽视问题，提出"每图像一次释放"机制与基于任务梯度能量的CANAL信道噪声分配方法，在固定隐私预算下最小化重要性加权失真，并给出诚实的隐私预算划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割需要多样化训练数据，但医院因隐私与监管原因无法共享互补的扫描图像。知识蒸馏可通过导出特征表示而非图像来架起桥梁，然而这些表示仍编码了患者特异性解剖信息，易受成员推断与特征反演攻击。加校准高斯噪声可恢复差分隐私保证，但已有DP特征蒸馏流程存在三个被忽视的问题：噪声在每个学生迭代重采样导致隐私代价随释放次数复合增长、均匀噪声忽视通道任务重要性差异、裁剪阈值与重要性分数本身数据依赖却明文释放破坏隐私保证。

Method: 1) 提出"每图像一次释放"机制，通过单次预计算实现每名患者仅贡献一次释放，避免隐私代价随迭代复合；2) 以任务梯度能量作为重要性度量，推导出CANAL——一种闭式注水（water-filling）分配方案，对重要通道分配更少噪声，并证明其在固定预算下严格最小化重要性加权失真；3) 针对裁剪上限与重要性分数数据依赖的问题，给出DP诚实预算划分，将二者均计入隐私预算，使报告epsilon为真实epsilon。

Result: 在覆盖皮肤镜、结肠镜和超声的三种医学分割基准上，相同隐私预算下CANAL比均匀噪声保留了更多任务相关信号。

Conclusion: 通过每图像单次释放、基于任务梯度能量的闭式注水噪声分配以及诚实的隐私预算划分，CANAL在固定隐私预算下优于均匀噪声方案，为不可共享医学数据下的隐私保护特征蒸馏提供了更高效且保证真实的方案。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation needs diverse training data, but hospitals hold complementary scans they cannot share for privacy and regulatory reasons. Knowledge distillation can bridge this gap by exporting learned feature representations instead of images, but those representations still encode patient-specific anatomy and remain vulnerable to membership-inference and feature-inversion attacks. Adding calibrated Gaussian noise restores a differential-privacy guarantee, yet three issues have been overlooked. First, prior DP feature-distillation pipelines re-sample noise at every student iteration, so each patient image is released many times and the privacy cost composes over those releases, growing by orders of magnitude. We present a sample-once-per-image release, realized by a single precomputation pass, under which each patient contributes one release. Second, uniform noise is wasteful because channels differ in task importance. Using task-gradient energy as the importance measure, we derive CANAL, a closed-form water-filling allocation that gives important channels proportionally less noise, and prove it strictly minimizes importance-weighted distortion at a fixed budget. Third, the clipping caps and importance scores that drive the allocation are themselves data-dependent, so releasing them in the clear silently breaks the guarantee. We give a DP-honest budget split that charges each to the privacy budget, so the reported epsilon is the true epsilon. Across three medical segmentation benchmarks spanning dermoscopy, colonoscopy, and ultrasound, CANAL retains more task-relevant signal than uniform noise at the same privacy budget.

</details>


### [24] [SomBench: Benchmark Dataset for Advancing Machine Learning in Lunar Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13277)
*Himanshu Patil,Gabby Nyirjesy,Rachel A. Slank,Vishal Gaur,Daniela Szwarcman,Paolo Fraccaro,Nikolaos Dionelis,Michael K. Barker,Andrew Annex,Vishnu Viswanathan,Zachary Morse,Ethan I. Schaefer,Hiyam Debary,Ankur Kumar,Rohit Lal,Geoffrey Dawson,Campbell Watson,Rebekah I. Dawson-Rigas,Manil Maskey,Juan Bernabé-Moreno,Rahul Ramachandran,Sujit Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SomBench，一个统一、空间对齐、适合机器学习（ML）的月球数据集，整合了四次任务、十种仪器的30多个共配准图层，并提供配套基准与基线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有月球轨道任务（如 LRO、Kaguya/SELENE、GRAIL、Lunar Prospector）虽提供丰富的多仪器观测，但在采样方式、投影和制图约定上存在异质性，阻碍了可复现的机器学习研究。

Method: 构建 SomBench 数据集：汇聚10种仪器、4次任务的30多个共配准图层，分辨率覆盖1米至20公里/像素，覆盖纬度82度，采用90个月球横向墨卡托（LTM）分带和两个极区立体投影帽；设计以影像为锚的切片流水线，生成预训练可用的多模态瓦片视图与防泄漏划分，并以 netCDF 格式发布、附 Parquet 目录；同时构建覆盖撞击过程、火山历史和极区挥发物的应用基准套件。

Result: 使用 ResNet-50 与 SwinV2-B 的基线实验表明，基准套件中的每个任务均可从所发布的输入中学习。

Conclusion: SomBench 为月球多仪器数据的可复现 ML 研究提供了统一基础，并为未来模型开发建立了参考基线。

Abstract: Lunar orbital missions, such as Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter, Kaguya/SELENE, Gravity Recovery and Interior Laboratory, and Lunar Prospector, among others, provide rich multi-instrument observations, but their heterogeneity in sampling, projection, and conventions limits reproducible machine learning (ML). We introduce SomBench, a unified, spatially-aligned, ML-ready lunar dataset aggregating 30+ co-registered layers from ten instruments across four missions, spanning 1 meter to 20 kilometer/pixel and covering 82 degree latitude in 90 Lunar Transverse Mercator zones with two polar stereographic caps. An image-anchored tiling pipeline yields pretraining-ready multimodal tile views with leakage-safe splits, distributed as netCDF with Parquet catalogs. An application benchmark suite spans impact processes, volcanic history, and polar volatiles. Baseline experiments with ResNet-50 and SwinV2-B models confirm that each benchmark task is learnable from the released inputs, establishing reference points for future model development.

</details>


### [25] [The MODA General Attribute Suite: A Four-Track Evaluation Benchmark for Fashion Attribute Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13279)
*Arkid Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布 MODA General Attribute Suite，一个四轨时尚属性抽取基准，通过分轨设计与严格协议解决评测不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚属性抽取的评测存在三类不一致：结果以跨图像类型的单一汇总数字报告、图像中不可见字段被当作普通负样本计分、数据集间词汇不匹配的影响被承认但从未测量。因此需要一个从构造上就隔离这些问题的评测框架。

Method: 提出四轨基准 MODA General Attribute Suite，分别对应局部服装裁剪、商品目录图、考虑适用性的全身照、商品文本；每轨拥有独立冻结测试集、输入契约、指标与泄漏单元，且不做跨轨平均。协议要求标签盲预测、开标签前对预测文件做 SHA-256 承诺、fail-closed 评分器，以及在每轨自然泄漏单元上进行 10,000 次配对聚类自助法；晋升标准要求每轨均为正向区间而非平均表现良好。同时发布评分器、划分构建脚本、含哈希的自身预测文件（包括失败运行）以及三轨的 MODA_NER(V) 检查点。

Result: 报告了基线结果、六项未带来提升的干预措施，以及证据的局限性。文本模型未分发，但其基准已发布。

Conclusion: 通过分轨、承诺式预注册与聚类自助法，该基准将时尚属性抽取的评测问题分离并加以可审计的约束，为可比、可复现的评测提供基础设施。

Abstract: Fashion attribute extraction is evaluated inconsistently: results are reported as single aggregate numbers across image types that pose different problems, fields that are not visible in an image are scored as ordinary negatives, and the effect of vocabulary mismatch between datasets is acknowledged but not measured. We release the MODA General Attribute Suite, a four-track benchmark that keeps these problems separate by construction. Each track (localized garment crops, catalogue product images, applicability-aware full-body photographs, and product text) carries its own frozen test set, input contract, metric, and leakage unit, and the tracks are never averaged. The protocol requires label-blind prediction, SHA-256 commitment of prediction files before any label is opened, a fail-closed scorer, and a 10,000-sample paired cluster bootstrap at each track's natural leakage unit; promotion requires a positive interval on every track rather than a favourable mean. We release the scorers, the split builders, our own prediction files with their hashes including the runs we lose, and the MODA_NER(V) model checkpoints for three of the four tracks. The text model is not distributed; its benchmark is. We report baseline results, six interventions that did not improve them, and the limitations of the evidence.

</details>


### [26] [RxScribe Bench: A Multi-Axis Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Indian Outpatient Prescriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13280)
*Somil,Vijay Saini,Vidit Verma,Riya,Aastha Batta,Vibhuti Malhotra,Chayan Khetan,Piyush Mittal,Puneet Poonia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RxScribe Bench，一个针对手写处方数字化的视觉语言模型评测基准，将性能分解为与临床严重程度相关的四个维度（正确性、幻觉、参与度、鲁棒性），而非单一聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处方转录错误并非可互换的：编造药物与误读清晰剂量带来的临床风险截然不同，但现有评测通常用单一混合指标将二者等同对待，掩盖了不同失败模式的差异化风险。

Method: 给定处方图像和输出schema，模型生成结构化记录，随后与形状相同的人工标注金标准逐字段比对，每个字段还标注可见性与可读性。四个维度分别隔离不同失败模式；其中鲁棒性维度在支撑样本低于最小证据阈值时不予报告结果，以避免证据不足的结论。

Result: 在真实处方上对前沿视觉语言模型进行评测，每张图像采用独立的冷启动运行，结果显示没有任何单一模型能在全部四个维度上同时胜出。

Conclusion: 以临床严重程度为导向的多维度分解评测比单一聚合分数更能揭示模型的实际风险差异，且评测应引入最小证据阈值以保证结论可靠性。

Abstract: Prescription transcription errors are not interchangeable. A model that fabricates a drug and a model that misreads a legible dose pose very different clinical risks, yet prescription-transcription accuracy is typically reported as a single blended figure that treats the two as equivalent. We introduce RxScribe Bench, a benchmark for evaluating vision-language models on handwritten prescription digitization that decomposes performance into four axes tied to clinical severity, rather than folding everything into a single aggregated score. Given only a prescription image and an output schema, a model produces a structured record, which is then compared field by field against a human-authored ground truth of identical shape, with each field also labeled for visibility and legibility. The four axes isolate distinct failure modes, namely Correctness, Hallucination, Engagement, and Robustness. The Robustness axis withholds its hard-field results rather than reporting one when the supporting sample falls below a minimum-evidence threshold. We evaluate frontier vision-language models on real prescriptions across independent cold runs per image, and find that no single model wins across all four axes.

</details>


### [27] [GaugeDefect: Detecting Surface Anomalies by Curvature of Feature Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13282)
*Yefan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GaugeDefect，一种基于特征传输曲率的几何方法，用于工业表面异常定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征、重建、蒸馏方法多通过判断局部外观或特征的异常程度来打分，但对于细划痕、小凹坑、重复纹理被打断等缺陷，异常区域内部局部特征仍可能看似正常，只是破坏了邻近特征的变化与连接关系，因此需要一种互补的几何线索。

Method: 在特征格点上为每个节点估计局部特征标架，计算相邻标架间的正交传输；沿小闭合回路累积传输得到和乐矩阵（holonomy matrix），其与单位矩阵的偏离度量特征传输曲率。在正常训练图像上校准后，曲率异常大则指示特征场局部不一致。该曲率为表示空间中的邻域不一致度量，而非物体物理曲率。

Result: 曲率信号能提升细微表面扰动的定位效果，并常在缺陷边界附近自然产生更尖锐的响应；方法适用于曲面、纹理材料及非平面工业物体。

Conclusion: GaugeDefect以特征传输曲率为互补几何线索，弥补了仅依赖局部外观/特征异常程度的局限，可用于复杂表面上的工业异常定位。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly localization has advanced rapidly with feature-based, reconstruction-based, and distillation-based methods. Most of these methods score a region by asking how unusual its local appearance or feature representation is with respect to normal training images. This is a strong and practical formulation. In this work, we study a complementary geometric cue for cases where an abnormal region may still contain locally plausible visual features. Thin scratches, small dents, and disrupted repeated patterns often do not make every local patch individually abnormal; instead, they disturb how nearby features vary and connect across the surface. We propose GaugeDefect, a geometric method for surface anomaly localization based on the curvature of feature transport. Given a feature lattice, we estimate a local feature frame at each node and compute orthogonal transports between neighboring frames. The accumulated transport around a small closed loop gives a holonomy matrix, whose deviation from identity measures feature-transport curvature. After calibration on normal training images, unusually large curvature indicates a local inconsistency in the feature field. The curvature here is not the physical curvature of the inspected object, but a representation-space measure of neighborhood inconsistency. This makes the method applicable to curved surfaces, textured materials, and non-planar industrial objects. Its main role is to improve localization of subtle surface disruptions, while often producing sharper responses near defect boundaries as a natural consequence of the curvature signal.

</details>


### [28] [Multimodal-Multiresolution Foundation Model for Lunar Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13283)
*Paolo Fraccaro,Gabby Nyirjesy,Daniela Szwarcman,Himanshu Patil,Vishal Gaur,Rohit Lal,Rachel A. Slank,Geoffrey Dawson,Hiyam Debary,Michael K. Barker,Andrew Annex,Vishnu Viswanathan,Zachary Morse,Ethan I. Schaefer,Nikolaos Dionelis,Ankur Kumar,Campbell D. Watson,Manil Maskey,Rebekah I. Dawson-Rigas,Juan Bernabé-Moreno,Rahul Ramachandran,Sujit Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个月球遥感多模态基础模型，基于SomBench数据集预训练，在多个月球任务上匹配或超越ImageNet基线，并验证了架构与预训练的双重贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球遥感领域缺乏专门的多模态基础模型，现有方法多依赖ImageNet预训练，难以充分利用多模态、多分辨率的月球观测数据及采集几何信息。

Method: 采用TerraMind掩码token架构，引入两项月球特定扩展：将采集几何作为显式上下文；联合训练米级与百米级瓦片使单一权重覆盖双分辨率。结合FlexiViT patch嵌入支持不同patch尺寸适配，模态级输入支持灵活的多模态微调。在近200万共配准瓦片、11种模态的SomBench语料上从头预训练。

Result: 在四个基准（WAC与NAC尺度陨石坑检测、IMP分割、极地冰勘探回归）上，预训练模型匹配或超越ImageNet基线及随机初始化对照。多模态冰勘探回归中预训练变体最佳；WAC陨石坑检测中，预训练模型仅用50%数据即超过全量数据的ImageNet最强基线。LoRA在陨石坑检测与IMP分割上匹配或超越全量微调且参数更少，全量微调在冰勘探回归最佳。

Conclusion: 预训练与架构均带来性能提升；LoRA与全量微调各有适用场景。作者开源预训练权重、基准数据集与微调代码以支持可复现的月球AI研究。

Abstract: We present a multimodal foundation model for lunar remote sensing, pretrained from scratch on SomBench, a geographically partitioned corpus of nearly two million co-registered tile bundles spanning 11 modalities at two spatial scales (1 m/pixel and 100 m/pixel). The model adapts the TerraMind masked-token architecture with two lunar-specific extensions: acquisition geometry is provided as explicit context, and meter- and hundred-meter-scale tiles are trained jointly so that a single set of weights covers both resolutions. FlexiViT patch embeddings allow adaptation to different patch sizes without retraining, while modality-wise inputs enable flexible multimodal fine-tuning. Qualitative generation experiments suggest the model learns meaningful cross-modal correspondences, including terrain derivatives from elevation and illumination-consistent reflectance from geometry. We evaluate on four benchmarks: crater detection at WAC and NAC scales, irregular mare patch (IMP) segmentation, and polar ice prospectivity regression. Across tasks, the pretrained model matches or outperforms ImageNet-pretrained baselines and an architecturally identical random-init control. On multimodal ice prospectivity regression, pretrained variants achieve the best results, while the random-init model outperforms most baselines, suggesting gains arise from both the architecture and pretraining. Label efficiency is notable for WAC crater detection, where the pretrained model trained on 50% of the data exceeds the strongest ImageNet baseline trained on the full dataset. Among adaptation strategies, LoRA matches or surpasses full fine-tuning on crater detection and IMP segmentation while using far fewer trainable parameters, whereas full fine-tuning performs best for ice prospectivity regression. We release the pretrained checkpoint, benchmark datasets, and fine-tuning code to support reproducible lunar AI research.

</details>


### [29] [LLaDA-UI: Bringing Block-wise Diffusion to Vision-Language GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13287)
*Zhangxuan Gu,Haoxing Chen,Qi Qin,Yi Xin,Kai Gan,Lin Liu,Long Cui,Xiaomei Wang,Beitong Zhou,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Changlong Gao,Weizhi Chen,Rongchao Zhang,Haoyuan Wu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Weiqiang Wang,Jianguo Li,Zhenzhong Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LLaDA-UI，一个基于MoE的16.7B参数块级扩散视觉语言GUI智能体，在多项GUI基准上显著优于Qwen2.5-VL-7B，并在六个基准中的四个超越Qwen3-VL-8B，证明块级扩散可作为多模态GUI智能体的实用生成范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过块并行、任意顺序生成实现高解码效率，适合延迟敏感场景。GUI智能体需实时感知屏幕状态并输出结构化、空间定位的动作，是这一范式的理想测试平台。但dLLMs能否在保持并行解码优势的同时扩展为强大的多模态GUI智能体，仍是待解问题。

Method: LLaDA-UI为16.7B参数的MoE架构、块级扩散视觉语言GUI智能体，采用两阶段训练：先进行通用多模态预训练，将原生分辨率视觉编码器与LLaDA2.0-mini-base扩散语言骨干对齐；随后在移动、桌面、网页及定位等多种数据上进行GUI智能体监督微调。

Result: 在多个平台的广泛定位基准和导航基准上，LLaDA-UI大幅超越Qwen2.5-VL-7B，并在六个报告的GUI基准中的四个上超越Qwen3-VL-8B。

Conclusion: 结果表明块级扩散是多模态GUI智能体的一种实用生成范式。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) achieve high decoding efficiency through block-parallel, arbitrary-order generation, making them attractive for latency-sensitive applications. GUI agents represent a natural testbed for this paradigm, as they must repeatedly perceive screen states and emit structured, spatially grounded actions in real time. However, whether dLLMs can be extended into capable multimodal GUI agents while preserving their parallel decoding advantage remains an open question. We present LLaDA-UI, a 16.7B-parameter MoE-based, block-wise diffusion vision-language GUI agent. LLaDA-UI follows a two-stage training pipeline: general multimodal pre-training aligns a native-resolution vision encoder with the LLaDA2.0-mini-base diffusion language backbone, followed by GUI-agent supervised fine-tuning on diverse mobile, desktop, web, and grounding data. Across widely adopted grounding benchmarks and navigation benchmarks spanning multiple platforms, LLaDA-UI substantially outperforms Qwen2.5-VL-7B and surpasses Qwen3-VL-8B on four of six reported GUI benchmarks. These results establish block-wise diffusion as a practical generative paradigm for multimodal GUI agents.

</details>


### [30] [Target-Checked Reliability Score Refinement for Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13288)
*Guoxiang Ren,Rohitash Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在不重新训练模型、不改变答案的前提下，利用响应图和目标试点数据改进视频语言模型答案级可靠性评分，在多个留出集上显著降低AURC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型常对多选题给出高置信度却错误的答案，亟需在不重训练、不改答案的条件下提升答案级可靠性评分在目标偏移下的表现。

Method: 从三个固定视频语言模型在四种确定性视频采样下收集选项概率列表，将跨视角变化与跨模型一致性表示为响应图；使用带标签的目标试点，比较原始分数（所选答案概率）、基于开发集训练的直方图梯度提升（HGB）分数以及基于目标试点训练的正则化逻辑回归分数；仅当重复的视频级检查显示正向且稳定的改进时才替换原始分数；构建Video Hallucination Diagnosis（VHD）诊断数据集。

Result: 在963题的HERBench留出集上，三模型平均AURC降低16.64%（95% CI，12.12–22.61%），最小模型级增益11.39%；在911题Perception Test留出集上平均降低18.87%（95% CI，15.43–22.14%）；InternVL3.5的目标检查保留原始分数；相同输出下在两个数据集上均优于七个免训练基线，并改善AUROC、降低校准误差，在50%覆盖率下错误率分别降低6.50和6.58个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重训练即可有效提升视频语言模型答案级可靠性评分，在多个基准上稳定优于训练无关基线，兼顾排序质量、校准与选择性预测性能。

Abstract: Video-language models can answer multiple-choice questions with high confidence yet be wrong. We study whether answer-level reliability scores can be improved under target shift without retraining the models or changing their answers. We collect option-probability lists from three fixed video-language models under four deterministic video samplings and represent cross-view changes and cross-model agreement as a response graph. Using a labeled target pilot, we compare the original score, defined as the probability assigned to the chosen answer, with a histogram-based gradient-boosting (HGB) score trained on the development datasets and a regularized logistic-regression score trained on the target pilot. A candidate replaces the original score only when repeated video-level checks indicate a positive, stable improvement. We develop this rule on public VideoQA benchmarks and Video Hallucination Diagnosis (VHD), a controlled diagnostic dataset for shared high-confidence errors. Ranking quality is measured by the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC), where lower is better. On a held-out 963-question HERBench split, the method reduces mean AURC across the three models by 16.64% (95% confidence interval (CI), 12.12 to 22.61%); the smallest model-level gain is 11.39%. On a separate held-out 911-question Perception Test split, the mean reduction is 18.87% (95% CI, 15.43 to 22.14%). For InternVL3.5, the target check retains the original scores. Using the same outputs, the method outperforms seven training-free baselines in mean AURC on both datasets. It also improves AUROC, reduces calibration error, and lowers the error rate at 50% coverage by 6.50 and 6.58 percentage points.

</details>


### [31] [Reliable Egocentric Action Anticipation via Temporal Reliability Suppression and Compositional Graph Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13293)
*Mahsa Mohammadi,Sareh Rowlands*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量框架TRS+RVG，提升可穿戴动作预判在时序损坏下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一视角动作预判方法大多假设观测干净，但可穿戴系统实际面临丢帧、掩码与传感器噪声；本文识别出时序损坏下的两类失效模式：编码阶段时序证据不可靠，以及解码阶段出现低支持度的不合理动宾组合。

Method: 框架包含两部分：TRS（Temporal Reliability Suppression）根据投影输入嵌入预测逐帧抑制分数，作为学习到的键侧注意力惩罚用于每个编码器块，并用于可靠性加权时序池化；RVG（Robust Verb-Noun Graph）基于训练标签构建PMI兼容性图，对动宾对进行重排序；并结合损坏增强训练。

Result: 在六种损坏（含三种训练中未出现的机制）下达到29.1%平均损坏准确率和88.2%相对鲁棒性，稀有动宾预测从15.4%降至1.1%；多种子与诊断实验表明TRS对合成掩码有响应，打乱图与仅频率对照说明RVG收益依赖真实成对兼容性而非仅边缘频率效应。

Conclusion: TRS与RVG从编码和解码两方面互补地提升时序损坏下的动作预判鲁棒性与组合合理性，且增益来源经对照实验验证。

Abstract: Wearable action anticipation systems must remain reliable despite missing frames, masking, and sensor noise, yet existing egocentric anticipation methods largely assume clean observations. We identify two complementary failure modes under temporal corruption: unreliable temporal evidence during encoding and implausible, low-support verb-noun compositions during decoding. We address them with a lightweight framework combining Temporal Reliability Suppression (TRS) and Robust Verb-Noun Graph (RVG) decoding. TRS predicts a per-frame suppression score from the projected input embedding and uses it as a learned key-side attention penalty at every encoder block and to derive reliability-weighted temporal pooling. RVG re-ranks verb-noun pairs using a PMI-based compatibility graph constructed from training labels. Under corruption-augmented training, TRS+CA+RVG reaches 29.1% average corrupted accuracy and 88.2% relative robustness across six corruptions, including three mechanisms absent during training, while reducing rare verb-noun predictions from 15.4% to 1.1%. Multi-seed and diagnostic experiments show that TRS responds to synthetic masking; shuffled-graph and frequency-only controls further indicate that RVG gains depend on genuine pairwise compatibility rather than marginal-frequency effects alone.

</details>


### [32] [VectorHarness: Recovering Editable, Relation-Preserving Structure from Scientific Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13294)
*Jiahao Tang,Yiren Song,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"栅格到可编辑"重建新目标与多智能体框架VectorHarness，将科学图形中的异构元素按类型还原为原生可编辑对象，并构建VectorHarness-Bench评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图形元素异构、布局复杂，现有图像转代码的多智能体重建系统多采用"复制粘贴"范式：重建图像虽近似原图，但复杂区域实际仍不可编辑，无法满足原生、定制化的编辑需求。

Method: 提出raster-to-authoring重建目标；设计VectorHarness多智能体框架，对文本、公式、形状、连接线、图标、图表、表格采用类型适配的原生表示进行重建，本质为图像的区域保留为栅格内容；构建VectorHarness-Bench，从渲染保真度、栅格回退覆盖率、可执行对象编辑、关系保持编辑四方面评估。

Result: VectorHarness在可执行编辑成功率与关系保持上均有提升，减少了可避免的栅格回退，并在异构图形上保持高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 以"可编辑性"而非"视觉复制"为目标的重建范式，可有效提升科学图形的可编辑还原质量，为图像到代码生成提供新方向与评测基准。

Abstract: Converting scientific graphics into editable representations remains a challenging problem for image-to-code generation because of their heterogeneous elements and complex layouts. Recent multi-agent reconstruction systems have advanced this line of work, but often follow a copy-paste paradigm: the reconstructed image closely resembles the original, while complex regions remain effectively uneditable. We instead formulate a different objective, raster-to-authoring reconstruction, which aims to recover an authoring representation that supports native, customized editing rather than mere visual replication. To this end, we present VectorHarness, a multi-agent framework for raster-to-authoring reconstruction that recovers heterogeneous components using type-appropriate native representations. Text, formulas, shapes, connectors, icons, charts, and tables are reconstructed as natively editable objects, while intrinsically image-based regions remain raster content. To systematically evaluate reconstruction quality, we introduce VectorHarness-Bench, which jointly assesses rendering fidelity, raster fallback coverage, executable object edits, and relation-preserving edits. Experiments show that VectorHarness improves executable edit success and relation preservation, reduces avoidable raster fallback, and maintains high visual fidelity across heterogeneous graphics.

</details>


### [33] [Harnessing Image Question Dependence for Better VLM Test-time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13296)
*Xinrui He,Ting-Wei Li,Junting Wang,Mengting Ai,Xinyu He,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TTIQ，一种面向视觉语言模型的测试时强化学习框架，利用图像-问题依赖关系估计响应级奖励，以提升无标签目标数据上的适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共识的测试时强化学习信号可靠性存在两个局限：性能提升主要来自答案归一化而非内容纠错；且当VLM因无法联合利用图像与问题进行推理而产生错误初始答案时，共识奖励可能保留而非纠正这些grounding错误。因此需要一种更可靠的奖励机制。

Method: TTIQ对每个采样响应分别在原始图像-问题对及其图像消融、问题消融变体下进行teacher forcing，利用token级似然变化估计响应对各输入的依赖程度；将图像依赖、问题依赖与校准置信度结合，构建响应级奖励以偏好联合grounding的响应；同时利用token级信号为同时受图像与问题支持的token分配更多正向策略信用。

Result: 在八个VQA数据集和多个VLM规模上的实验表明，TTIQ在每个模型规模上均取得最佳平均性能；方法可跨VLM家族泛化；在单一数据集上训练的模型无需进一步训练即可在未见数据集上提升性能。

Conclusion: TTIQ通过利用图像-问题依赖关系构建更可靠的测试时强化学习奖励，优于仅依赖共识的流行度信号，实现了更强的VLM适配与泛化能力。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning can adapt vision-language models (VLMs) to unlabeled target data, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by the reliability of self-generated learning signals. To assess the reliability of consensus-based learning signals, we analyze VLM test-time reinforcement learning across diverse VQA datasets and model sizes, revealing two limitations. First, gains from consensus-based test-time training largely come from answer normalization rather than content correction. Second, many initial VLM responses are incorrect due to the model's limited ability to jointly use the image and the question; consensus rewards derived from these outputs may preserve the resulting grounding errors rather than correct them. Motivated by these, we propose TTIQ, a test-time reinforcement learning framework that harnesses image-question dependence for better vlm adaptation. TTIQ teacher-forces each sampled response under the original image-question pair and its image- and question-ablated variants, using the resulting token-level likelihood changes to estimate dependence on each input. It combines image and question dependence with calibrated confidence to construct a response-level reward that favors jointly grounded responses, and uses the token-level signals to assign greater positive policy credit to tokens supported by both inputs. This design favors responses that are jointly grounded in the image and the question and sufficiently confident, rather than merely popular. Experiments across eight VQA datasets and multiple VLM sizes show that TTIQ achieves the best average performance at every model scale. It further generalizes across VLM families, while models trained on one dataset improve performance on unseen datasets without further training.

</details>


### [34] [Variational Template Matching with Statistical Fusion for Anomaly Detection in Patterned Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13298)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出变分模板匹配框架，将异常模板表示为变换实例族，结合KDE密度统计异常分数与统一融合公式，在生物细胞图像上无需训练即达到与ResNet-50竞争的性能，并具有可解释的定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化图像的小样本异常检测中，深度学习方法成本高或不实用；传统模板匹配简单可解释，但缺乏对尺度、旋转、透视等几何变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 变分模板匹配：将异常模板建模为一系列变换后的实例族，在变换空间上通过归一化互相关进行检测；2) 基于密度的统计异常分数：利用核密度估计（KDE）从局部强度分布中导出，比直方图方法更鲁棒，能刻画分布的集中性与尾部行为；3) 通过统一的融合公式整合结构与统计信号，互补建模几何相似性与分布偏差。

Result: 在生物细胞图像实验中，该方法优于经典基线，在完全无需训练的设置下与ResNet-50性能相当，并能提供显式的异常定位。

Conclusion: 该方法是面向结构化图像异常检测的高效、可解释且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in structured images is challenging in small-data settings where deep learning approaches are costly or impractical. Classical template matching is simple and interpretable but lacks robustness to geometric variations such as scale, rotation, and perspective.
  We propose a variational template matching framework that represents anomaly templates as a family of transformed instances and performs detection via normalized cross-correlation over this transformation space. To further improve robustness, we introduce a density-based statistical anomaly score derived from local intensity distributions using kernel density estimation (KDE). This produces a smooth representation that captures distributional concentration and tail behavior more robustly than histogram-based methods.
  The structural and statistical signals are integrated through a unified fusion formulation, enabling complementary modeling of geometric similarity and distributional deviation. Experiments on biological cell images demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms classical baselines and achieves competitive performance with ResNet-50 under a fully training-free setting, while providing explicit localization. The approach offers an efficient, interpretable, and practical solution for anomaly detection in structured image domains.

</details>
