The benchmark renders each reconstruction from its animated camera and evaluates it on two axes: (1) Dual VQA measures how many spatiotemporal facts the reconstruction preserves. (2) Latent Similarity measures how closely the reconstruction matches the source video perceptually. Our overall score, a square-root mean, favors balanced performance. We evaluate 51 configurations from 10 model families and analyze semantic retention, perceptual similarity, reasoning effort, and cost. The best model reaches 88.6 Latent Similarity but retains only 53.7% of the source-correct spatiotemporal answers. Additional reasoning improves visual similarity but does not close this gap in factual accuracy. In a blind study with 15 raters and five configurations, Latent Similarity correlates strongly with human preference. These results show that programmatic reconstruction is a viable test of agentic video understanding, and that semantic retention remains the main challenge.

</details>


### [172] [CatchMonitor: a machine learning system for automated fish discard quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15484)
*Geoff French,Michal Mackiewicz,Mark Fisher,Helen Holah,Rebecca Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为CatchMonitor的计算机视觉系统原型，可从渔船远程电子监控（REM）视频中自动量化被丢弃的鱼类，通过半监督学习提升物种识别精度，并以人工分析结果作为基准评估系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渔船上的实际监控条件复杂，对拖网渔船监控视频进行分析是一项具有挑战性的任务，而人工量化被丢弃鱼类的工作存在主观性和差异性，因此有必要开发自动化的丢弃量化系统。

Method: 在前期工作基础上，采用半监督学习提升物种识别准确率；使用一种简单且鲁棒的目标跟踪方法，并在此基础上构建丢弃量化系统原型；同时分析多位人类专家进行人工丢弃量化时的变异性，作为系统性能比较的基准。

Result: 半监督学习提升了物种识别的准确率；所构建的跟踪与量化原型系统得以实现；通过分析多位专家人工量化的变异性，获得了可用于评估系统性能的基准参照。

Conclusion: CatchMonitor的持续开发成功产出了可自动量化丢弃鱼类的计算机视觉系统原型，并通过与人工分析变异性基准的对比来评估其性能，为渔获丢弃监测的自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We report on the continued development of CatchMonitor, resulting in a prototype computer vision system designed to automatically quantify discarded fish from video footage collected from Remote Electronic Monitoring (REM) systems on fishing trawlers. The analysis of trawler surveillance footage is a challenging problem due to the real-world conditions on board fishing vessels. Building on our prior work we improve the accuracy of species identification through the application of semi-supervised learning. We utilise a simple and robust object tracking approach, upon which we build our prototype discard quantification system. Finally we analyse the variability of manual discard quantification performed by multiple expert human analysts, using it as a benchmark against which we compare the performance of our system.

</details>


### [173] [SyntheticDoc: A Large Synthetic Dataset for Document Unwarping and Illumination Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15503)
*Daniel Woortmann,Tanguy Magne,Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SyntheticDoc，一个包含 100 万张高分辨率程序化生成样本的大规模高质量文档去畸变数据集，提供 UV 图、法线图、反照率和阴影等像素级标注，基于物理模拟器和路径追踪渲染，并验证其在文档去畸变与光照校正任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已成为文档去畸变与光照校正的标准工具，但其性能受训练数据限制。近十年来社区主要依赖 Doc3D，而该数据集在规模和 质量上日益受限，成为研究瓶颈。因此需要构建一个更大规模、更高质量的文档去畸变数据集。

Method: 构建 SyntheticDoc 数据集：包含 1,000,000 张高分辨率程序化生成的训练样本，以及大量验证集和测试集。每个样本配有像素级精确标注，包括 UV 图、法线图、反照率和阴影。纸张几何通过基于物理的模拟器生成，并使用路径追踪渲染器渲染，以保证物理准确性与照片级真实感。在 SyntheticDoc 上训练一个简单基线模型。

Result: 在文档去畸变和光照校正两项任务上，使用 SyntheticDoc 训练的简单基线模型与当前最先进方法进行了对比评估，验证了该数据集带来的性能收益。数据集和生成代码已公开。

Conclusion: SyntheticDoc 为文档去畸变研究提供了大规模、高质量、标注丰富的训练资源，有望突破长期受限于 Doc3D 的数据瓶颈，推动该领域发展。数据集与生成代码均已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become the standard tool for document rectification and illumination correction, yet their performance is fundamentally bound by their training data. For nearly a decade, the community has heavily relied on Doc3D, a pioneering but increasingly limited document unwarping dataset in terms of scale and quality. To address this bottleneck, we introduce SyntheticDoc, a massive, high-quality dataset designed to push the boundaries of document unwarping. SyntheticDoc is composed of 1,000,000 high-resolution procedurally generated training samples, alongside extensive validation and test sets. Each sample is paired with rich, pixel-perfect annotations, including UV maps, normal maps, albedo and shading. To ensure physical accuracy and photorealism, the paper geometries are generated via a physics-based simulator and rendered using a path tracer. To demonstrate the benefit of our dataset, we train a simple baseline model on SyntheticDoc and report on its performance in comparison to state-of-the-art methods on both document unwarping and illumination correction tasks. Our dataset is available at https://igl.ethz.ch/projects/SyntheticDoc/ and the code used to generate it at https://github.com/tanguymagne/SyntheticDoc .

</details>


### [174] [Assessing nnU-Net Generalization across Brain Tumor Populations in BraTS-GoAT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15524)
*Tristan Kirscher,Vivian Metzger,Philippe Meyer,Xavier Coubez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在BraTS-GoAT数据集上评估异构人群的肿瘤分割，采用3D nnU-Net五折交叉验证训练，比较源域与验证集性能差异，并分析失败病例的形态学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BraTS-GoAT旨在评估肿瘤分割在异构人群中的泛化能力，本研究关注模型跨人群性能下降的原因及失败病例特征。

Method: 在1,351个标注病例上训练常规3D nnU-Net，采用五折交叉验证、每折1,000轮；最终预测器平均所有折并应用测试时镜像。使用fold-0匹配推断比较源OOF与汇总验证性能，并尝试残差编码器替代方案。

Result: 汇总官方验证的全局DSC为ET 0.7805、TC 0.8288、WT 0.8854。fold-0推断下平均区域Dice从源OOF的0.9058降至汇总验证的0.8310（差-0.0747）。镜像带来小幅单折增益但无明显集成收益；残差编码器替代方案达到0.8282平均Dice。失败病例参考ET体积明显更小；调整ET和WT体积后，较低Dice仍与更多断开的ET组件及最大组件中ET比例较小相关。

Conclusion: 模型在异构验证集上性能显著下降，失败主要与较小的ET体积、更多断开组件及碎片化ET相关，表明ET形态复杂性是分割失败的关键因素。

Abstract: BraTS-GoAT evaluates tumor segmentation across heterogeneous populations. We trained a conventional 3D nnU-Net on 1,351 labeled cases using five-fold cross-validation and 1,000 epochs per fold. The final predictor averaged all folds and applied test-time mirroring. On pooled official validation, global DSC values were 0.7805, 0.8288, and 0.8854 for enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), and whole tumor (WT). Under matched fold-0 inference, mean regional Dice decreased from 0.9058 on source out-of-fold (OOF) cases to 0.8310 on pooled validation (difference--0.0747). Mirroring gave small single-fold gains but no clear ensemble benefit; a residual-encoder alternative reached 0.8282 mean Dice. In labeled OOF predictions, failure cases had substantially smaller reference ET volumes; after adjustment for ET and WT volume, lower Dice remained associated with more disconnected ET components and a smaller fraction of ET contained in the largest component.

</details>


### [175] [PIVOT: Physics-Grounded Verification for AI-Generated Audio-Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15562)
*Bo Zheng,Kangran Zhao,Xiaoyu Zhang,Weinan Guan,Zhiheng Li,Yize Chen,Haizhou Li,Qingshan Liu,Siwei Lyu,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PIVOT，一种基于物理定律约束验证的AIGC检测器，并构建了PhysForensics-Bench音视频基准，通过检验事件是否满足可测量物理约束来区分真实与生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型进步，AIGC内容愈发逼真，传统依赖伪影线索的检测器逐渐失效。但现有生成器仍难以忠实复现真实世界事件的物理行为，因此可借助物理定律的可测量约束来检测AIGC。

Method: PIVOT从视频与音频中估计物理量，为每个片段选择相关物理定律，并验证其可测量约束；除真/假判断外，还输出支持性证据，包含验证结果、相关时间窗口及各适用定律的支持量。框架在此以音视频为实例，原则上可扩展至其他模态。

Result: 在PhysForensics-Bench（含九个事件场景族、两种近期音视频生成器的配对真实/生成片段）上，PIVOT在Real+Seedance达70.30%准确率、64.29% F1，Real+VEO达72.16%准确率、65.82% F1；而Gemini 3.1 Pro直接检测仅为53.96%/60.09%与57.22%/63.44%。

Conclusion: 结果表明物理一致性验证作为一种结构化、可检验的证据来源具有实际潜力，可补充基于伪影的AIGC检测方法。

Abstract: As generative models continue to advance, AI-generated content (AIGC) is becoming increasingly realistic, weakening the artifact cues commonly exploited by existing detectors. Nevertheless, faithfully reproducing the physical behavior of real-world events remains challenging for current generators. We therefore explore detecting AIGC by assessing whether the depicted event satisfies measurable constraints derived from physical laws. We introduce PIVOT, a physics-grounded AIGC detector, instantiated here for audio-video clips, that estimates physical quantities from video and audio, selects physical laws relevant to each clip, and verifies their measurable constraints. Beyond a real/fake decision, PIVOT returns supporting evidence that records the verification outcome, relevant time window, and supporting quantities for each applicable law. Although instantiated and evaluated here on audio-video data, the framework can, in principle, extend to other AIGC modalities whenever the physical quantities required for verification can be estimated reliably. We also introduce PhysForensics-Bench, comprising paired real and generated audio-video clips from nine event-centric scene families and two recent audio-video generators. On PhysForensics-Bench, PIVOT achieves 70.30% accuracy and 64.29% F1 score on Real+Seedance, and 72.16% accuracy and 65.82% F1 on Real+VEO. In comparison, direct inspection with Gemini 3.1 Pro obtains 53.96% accuracy and 60.09% F1 on Real+Seedance, and 57.22% accuracy and 63.44% F1 on Real+Veo. These results demonstrate the practical promise of physical-consistency verification as a structured and inspectable source of evidence that complements artifact-based AIGC detection.

</details>


### [176] [What a gated sensing pipeline never looks at: bandwidth reduction and the misses behind it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15579)
*Raghu Venkat,Tricha Anjali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一条“门控感知”流水线：在受限链路上用“发现”而非视频回传，并结合学习式目标检测、图文比对与确定性调度/门控/证据累积/传输规则，报告压缩比与检测结果，并给出可复现协议与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受对抗/受限链路上，机载传感器无法发送视频，因此有吸引力的是只发送“发现”并报告两者之比（压缩比）。但压缩比同时反映场景本身，且产生压缩的机制也决定哪些观测能进入决策，二者不可分开报告，故需要对该门控感知流水线进行诚实评估与追踪。

Method: 一条门控感知流水线：学习式目标检测 + 图文比较 + 确定性调度、门控、证据累积与传输规则。在分阶段（staged）影像上运行语义阶段；给出单次飞行的 tick 级追踪（3,187 行），定位三个漏报事件各自停滞的阶段；并报告代码修正前后对比（修正后移除了跟踪器测速伪影，使正态模型不再“预热”）。

Result: 修正前：211 s 内发送 38,736 bits，压缩 41,977x，4 个分阶段事件中命中 1 个且无误报。修正后：4 次飞行、7 个分阶段事件中命中 1 个。控制飞行（无事件）不报告任何内容，压缩 155,830x（研究中最大但信息最少）。漏报定位：1 个未产生航迹；1 个在结构位置检验处以 0.129 失败；1 个通过 539 个结构 tick 但仅达 3.192（阈值 3.807）。另报告一例已知失败模式：在线正态模型吸收了它日后要判定的目标。

Conclusion: 总体证据为：4 次分阶段飞行中 1 次检测、6 次漏报，外加 1 次干净对照，本文将其作为案例研究。提供复现协议与生成结果，指出哪些支撑性产物未分发，并说明哪些实验未运行。

Abstract: An airborne sensor on a contested link cannot send video, so the appealing move is to send findings instead and report the ratio between the two. We evaluate a gated sensing pipeline that does this, combining learned object detection and image-text comparison with deterministic scheduling, gating, evidence accumulation and transmission rules. On staged footage with the semantic stage live it sends 38,736 bits over 211 s, a reduction of 41,977x, and names 1 of 4 staged events with no false report. That detection has since been superseded: a correction to how the tracker measures speed removed the measurement artefact the normality model had been learning from, and the flight no longer warms. Under the corrected code the pipeline names 1 of 7 staged events across four flights, and we report both. On a control flight where nothing was staged it reports nothing, a reduction of 155,830x: the largest number in the study and the least informative, because a reduction ratio measures the scene. The mechanisms that produce the reduction also decide which observations ever reach a decision, so the two cannot be reported apart. We give a tick-level trace of one flight (3,187 rows) that places each of three missed events at the stage where it stopped progressing: one produced no track, one failed the structural place test at 0.129, and one passed 539 structural ticks but reached only 3.192 against a boundary of 3.807. We also report one instance of a known failure mode, an online normality model absorbing the object it will later judge, measured against the threshold that object then failed. The evidence is one detection and six misses across four staged flights, beside one clean control, and we treat it as a case study. We give the reproduction protocol and generated results, identify which supporting artifacts are not distributed, and state which experiments did not run.

</details>


### [177] [A Unified Vision-Language Model for PSMA PET/CT Report Generation, Visual Question Answering, and Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15603)
*Yang Xing,Jiong Wu,Savas Ozdemir,Yang Zhou,Boxiao Yu,Ying Zhang,Zheren Zhu,Chenyu You,Wei Shao,Yang Lu,Kang Wang,Tinsu Pan,Yang Yang,Kuang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的PSMA PET/CT视觉-语言模型，可同时完成报告生成、视觉问答和病灶分割三项任务，并在各项任务上优于现有专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的PSMA PET/CT解读对前列腺癌管理至关重要，但现有PET/CT AI模型通常只处理单一孤立任务，缺乏统一、可交互且可解释的分析框架。

Method: 采用LLaVA式架构，包含PET/CT视觉编码器、MLP-Mixer投影模块、LoRA微调的大语言模型以及3D分割分支；训练分四阶段：视觉编码器预训练、投影层对齐、VLM微调、多任务最终调优。语言任务使用5,747例配报告数据集，分割任务使用AutoPET的PSMA子集。

Result: 在标准报告生成指标上优于PET2REP和基于CT的基线；在各类VQA问题上均有提升；Dice和病灶级重叠F1高于SegAnyPET和nnUNet。

Conclusion: 结果证明在单一多任务模型架构内实现结构化、交互式、可解释且具备体素级定位的PSMA PET/CT分析是可行的。

Abstract: Accurate PSMA PET/CT interpretation is central to prostate cancer management, yet existing PET/CT AI models typically address isolated tasks. We propose a unified PSMA PET/CT vision-language model for report generation, visual question answering, and lesion segmentation. The framework adopts an LLaVA-style architecture, comprising a PET/CT vision encoder, an MLP-Mixer projection module, a LoRA-tuned large language model, and a 3D segmentation branch. Training followed a four-stage strategy: vision encoder pretraining, projection-layer alignment, VLM fine-tuning, and final multitask tuning. Language tasks used 5,747 PSMA PET/CT datasets with paired reports, while segmentation used the PSMA subset of AutoPET. The model outperformed PET2REP and a CT-based baseline across standard report-generation metrics, improved performance across VQA question types, and achieved higher Dice and lesion-level overlap F1 than SegAnyPET and nnUNet. These results support the feasibility of a unified framework for structured, interactive, interpretable PSMA PET/CT analysis with voxel-level grounding within a single multitask model architecture.

</details>


### [178] [VideoScout: Learning Agentic Active Exploration with Adaptive Reasoning Pacing for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15606)
*Weixin Xu,Zhenyu Yang,Bing Wang,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoScout，将长视频理解建模为"序列证据获取(SEA)"问题，通过多轮推理智能体自适应控制观看节奏，在有限视觉上下文窗口内高效遍历长视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在短视频理解上进展显著，但受限于视觉上下文窗口，在长视频上表现有限。现有方法依赖均匀帧采样或粗到细的智能体缩放，难以在足够长的视频中定位稀疏且决定性的证据。

Method: 作者将长视频理解形式化为 SEA 问题：智能体沿时间轴逐轮读取视频，每次决定观看速度、保留哪些证据、何时回访不确定片段、何时停止作答。基于此提出 VideoScout——一个通过自适应推理节奏实现 SEA 范式的多轮推理智能体，动态控制观看节奏以在有限窗口内高效遍历。训练上构建了 VideoScout-66K（来自1万条答案验证轨迹的6.6万余高质量探索轮次），采用两阶段流程：冷启动监督微调教授逐轮输出格式，再用 DAPO 算法进行轨迹级强化学习，复合奖励综合考虑答案准确性、输出格式合规性以及智能体观看进度与教师答案时机的时序对齐（用 IoU 衡量）。

Result: 在长视频理解与推理基准上的大量实验表明，其7B模型相比现有已训练的7B智能体模型取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过将长视频理解转化为序列证据获取问题并引入自适应推理节奏，VideoScout 在有限视觉上下文下实现了对长视频的高效理解与推理，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress on short video understanding yet remain limited on long videos due to the limited visual context window. Prevailing approaches rely on uniform frame sampling or recent coarse-to-fine agentic zooming, both of which struggle to localize sparse, decisive evidence in sufficiently long videos. We formulate long video understanding as a \textbf{Sequential Evidence Acquisition (SEA)} problem, in which an agent reads the video turn by turn along the temporal axis, deciding at each turn how fast to watch, what evidence to retain, when to revisit uncertain segments, and when to stop and answer. Inspired by this view, we propose \textbf{VideoScout}, a multi-turn reasoning agent that instantiates the SEA paradigm through adaptive reasoning pacing. Specifically, by dynamically controlling the viewing pace, VideoScout enables efficient traversal of long videos within a bounded visual context window, allowing the agent to access more video content while balancing content analysis depth with reading efficiency. To train VideoScout, we construct VideoScout-66K, a set of over 66K high-quality exploration turns from 10K answer-verified trajectories, and adopt a two-stage pipeline: cold-start supervised fine-tuning teaches the agent per-turn output format, while the Decoupled Clip and Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO) algorithm performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning with a composite reward that jointly considers answer accuracy, output format compliance, and the temporal alignment between the agent's viewing progress and the teacher's answer timing measured by intersection-over-union (IoU). Extensive experiments on long video understanding and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our 7B model achieves strong performance compared with existing trained 7B agentic models.

</details>


### [179] [ModaLens: Measuring Image Sensitivity in Report-Conditioned Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15635)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Maximin Lange,Quang Bui,Anqi Peter Li,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Rafi Al Attrach,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Xinyue Zhang,Ashley Zhang,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ModaLens 成对图像替换审计方法，测量放射学报告可用性如何改变视觉-语言模型对图像的敏感性。在 MIMIC-CXR 的 3,199 对病例上，MedGemma-27B 在有报告时答案改变率为 4.26%，无报告时为 20.94%，配对差值 16.7 个百分点（95% CI 15.6–17.7），表明报告可用会降低模型对图像替换的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告本身已能回答临床问题，因此难以判断视觉-语言模型是否真正使用了图像信息，还是仅依赖文本报告作答。作者希望建立一种能区分"报告驱动"与"图像驱动"回答的审计方法，从而评估模型的多模态真实性。

Method: 提出 ModaLens 成对图像替换审计：在同一临床问题与固定报告条件下，将每张图像替换为来自另一项检查（通常为同一患者）的图像，观察模型生成答案是否改变。在 MIMIC-CXR 上选取 293 名患者的 3,199 对病例，每例包含 14 个问题（13 个针对具体征象，1 个复合问题）。通过显式答题指令与原始提示两种协议，分别统计有/无报告条件下答案变化率，并按患者聚类计算置信区间。

Result: 在显式答题指令下，有报告时答案改变率为 4.26%，无报告时为 20.94%，配对增加 16.7 个百分点（患者聚类 95% CI 15.6–17.7）。原始提示配合首 token 小写读取时，对应数值为 4.70% 与 17.07%。此外，即使二元预测未变，图像替换仍会改变连续答案分数。该方向性结论在另外两个模型系列中可复现。

Conclusion: 报告可用性在该协议下降低了模型对图像替换的敏感性，说明报告的存在会削弱模型对图像的依赖。受限于标签源自报告本身，结论无法直接推断视觉正确性。代码、完整提示词及每个数字的运行记录已公开。

Abstract: A radiology report can already answer a clinical question, so it is hard to tell whether a vision-language model also uses the image. ModaLens, a paired image-swap audit, measures how report availability changes image sensitivity: MedGemma-27B on 3,199 paired MIMIC-CXR cases from 293 patients, all 14 questions per case (13 finding-specific and one composite), each image replaced by one from another study, usually of the same patient, with question and report fixed. Under an explicit answer instruction, the model's generated answer changes on 4.26 percent of trials with the report and 20.94 percent without it, a paired increase of 16.7 points (patient-clustered 95 percent CI 15.6 to 17.7), so report availability reduces image-swap sensitivity under this protocol; the original prompt with a lowercase first-token readout gives 4.70 percent against 17.07 percent, and substitutions also move continuous answer scores where the binary prediction does not change. The labels are derived from reports, which limits conclusions about visual correctness; the direction replicates in two further model lineages. Code, the exact prompts and a run record for every number are at https://github.com/criticaldata/MODALENS.

</details>


### [180] [SAM3D-Part: Interactive Part Selection and Generation from 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15639)
*Jiahao Chang,Dong Du,Wanhu Sun,Yujian Zheng,Chuanyu Pan,Bowen Zhao,Chongjie Ye,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAM3D-Part，从三维网格中按提示选择性生成完整部件网格，提升对齐精度并降低条件成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D资产创建常需按部件编辑、复用、动画或制造，用户往往只需若干特定部件而非完整分解。但现有3D生成方法无论意图都生成全部部件，可提示的3D分割方法多输出部分表面而非可复用完整网格；图像条件部件生成器在缺少源网格直接条件时难以保持隐藏几何与准确摆放。

Method: SAM3D-Part为提示驱动的选择性部件生成框架：输入源网格与部件提示，先将源几何编码为紧凑网格特征，并与渲染图像、选择性掩码、点图观测通过逐像素通道融合对齐；融合表示驱动前馈生成模型仅生成被查询部件为完整网格；再预测稠密逐体素对应关系，从分布空间证据估计部件变换以放回源坐标系；多部件顺序查询时用部件缓存作为上下文约束。

Result: 大量实验与消融表明，SAM3D-Part显著改善源对齐、降低条件成本，并实现一致的选择性部件生成，达到当前最优性能。代码与权重将公开。

Conclusion: SAM3D-Part实现提示驱动的选择性完整部件网格生成，兼顾对齐、成本与多部件一致性，达到最优水平，并提供公开代码与权重。

Abstract: Part-level control is essential for modern 3D asset creation, where objects are frequently edited, reused, animated, or fabricated through their individual components. In many such workflows, users need only several specific components rather than a complete object decomposition. However, existing 3D generation methods produce all parts regardless of user intent, while promptable 3D segmentation methods typically output partial surfaces instead of reusable complete meshes. In addition, image-conditioned part generators further struggle to preserve hidden geometry and accurate placement without directly conditioning on the source mesh. To address these problems, we present SAM3D-Part, a prompt-driven framework for selective part generation from input 3D object meshes. Given a source mesh and a part prompt, SAM3D-Part first encodes the source geometry into compact mesh features and aligns them with the rendered image, selective mask, and point-map observations via pixel-wise channel fusion. The fused representation conditions a feed-forward generative model to produce only the queried component as a completed mesh. To place the generated part back into the source coordinate frame, SAM3D-Part predicts dense per-voxel correspondences and estimates the part transformation from distributed spatial evidence rather than a single global pose code. For sequential multi-part queries, previously generated parts are stored in a part cache and reused as contextual constraints, reducing conflicts among independently requested components. Extensive experiments and ablations demonstrate that SAM3D-Part can significantly improve source alignment, reduce conditioning cost, and enable consistent selective part generation, achieving state-of-the-art. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/Jiahao620/sam3d-part.

</details>


### [181] [MedDiME: Efficient Latent Diffusion with Adaptive Masking for Medical Counterfactual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15647)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedDiME，一种潜在空间分类器引导的扩散框架，用于医学反事实生成，实现空间精确编辑并大幅降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学反事实生成通过修改图像改变模型预测以提升可解释性。但现有基于扩散的方法速度慢、内存消耗大，难以应用于高分辨率场景；且现有掩码策略与像素空间表示紧密耦合，无法兼容潜在空间扩散编辑。

Method: MedDiME在潜在空间中进行分类器引导的扩散，引入与潜在空间兼容、由梯度驱动的自适应掩码机制，实现空间精确的医学反事实生成。

Result: 在大量实验中，MedDiME实现了高质量反事实生成，相比此前的分类器引导扩散基线，推理速度最高提升40倍，峰值GPU内存降低最多13倍。

Conclusion: MedDiME兼顾高质量反事实生成与显著效率提升，为高分辨率医学反事实生成提供可行方案。

Abstract: Medical counterfactual generation modifies images to change model predictions for interpretability. However, existing diffusion-based approaches are often prohibitively slow and memory-intensive, making them difficult to apply in high-resolution settings. Moreover, existing masking strategies are tightly coupled with pixel-space representations, making them incompatible with latent-space diffusion editing. To address these challenges, we propose MedDiME, a latent-space classifier-guided diffusion framework that reduces computational and memory overhead while introducing a latent-compatible, gradient-driven adaptive masking mechanism for spatially precise medical counterfactual generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedDiME achieves high-quality counterfactual generation with significant efficiency gains compared to prior classifier-guided diffusion baselines, achieving up to 40 times faster inference and 13 times lower peak GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [182] [From Model Patterns to Abstract Semantics in Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15649)
*Weize Li,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，将组合零样本学习中的基元变化视为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活，通过完形填空式推理与重排序提升未见组合的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖视觉语言模型并通过多重表示显式建模基元的上下文变化，但受限于固定的变体容量以及抽象语义与具体语义之间的竞争问题。

Method: CLEAR采用由粗到细的方式从基元候选集中提取条件变体，进行完形填空式推理以推断高层语义，并通过重排序预测来纠正对显著具体基元的偏向。

Result: 大量实验表明CLEAR稳定提升基础模型性能，并在具有挑战性的C-GQA和MIT-States数据集上超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将基元变化理解为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活是一种有效的新视角，基于该视角的重排序推理框架能显著改进组合零样本学习。

Abstract: Compositional Zero Shot Learning aims to recognize unseen compositions by recombining learned primitives. Recent methods rely on vision language models and attempt to explicitly model contextual variations of primitives through multiple representations. However, such approaches are limited by fixed variant capacity and competition between abstract and concrete semantics. In this work, we present a new perspective that views primitive variations as the context-driven activation of concrete visual cues rather than independent entities. Based on it, we propose CLEAR, a CLoze-style rEAsoning-based Re-ranking framework inspired by human perceptual processes. CLEAR extracts conditional variants from the primitive candidate set in a coarse-to-fine manner, performs cloze-style reasoning to infer high-level semantics, and re-ranks predictions to correct biases toward salient concrete primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CLEAR consistently improves the Base Model and outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the challenging C-GQA and MIT-States datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/buptLwz/CLEAR.

</details>


### [183] [Benchmarking Intra-Patient 3D Deformable Multimodal Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15669)
*Matteo Barbieri,Giammarco La Barbera,Juan Pablo De La Plata,Sabine Sarnacki,Isabelle Bloch,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对患者内部3D多模态可变形配准方法进行全面基准评测，覆盖三个不同解剖区域与难度的数据集，比较经典优化方法、深度学习及基础模型，发现几何指标与图像相似度指标存在系统性分歧，且没有方法能在各解剖与模态上稳健表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准是临床工作流的关键环节，但对应解剖结构在不同模态下强度差异显著，导致配准困难。现有方法缺乏跨解剖区域、跨难度场景的系统性比较，尤其对学习型方法与基础模型在真实临床场景中的表现认识不足，因此需要建立综合性基准评测。

Method: 构建患者内部3D多模态可变形配准基准，覆盖三个数据集、不同解剖区域与难度，包含合成形变恢复与真实临床场景。评测对象包括经典优化方法与现代学习方法（含近期深度学习和基础模型）。评价指标为三类互补指标：平均Dice相似系数（DSC）、平均95百分位Hausdorff距离（HD95），以及基于MIND自相似性上下文的模态无关结构相似度（MIND-SSC）。

Result: 结果在不同数据集间波动较大：学习型方法在大规模合成基准上表现更优，但在真实盆腔配准中仅观察到有限提升。关键发现是几何指标（DSC、HD95）与图像相似度指标（MIND-SSC）之间持续存在不一致，说明重叠度提升并不必然意味着更好的全局多模态对应。此外，解剖引导方法获得最高重叠分数，但在分割区域外性能下降，揭示标签驱动对齐与全局结构一致性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 目前没有方法能在所有解剖结构和模态上实现稳健性能。患者内部3D多模态配准需要多准则评估，应包括基于形变的指标，该问题仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Multimodal image registration is a key component of many clinical workflows, yet it remains challenging because corresponding anatomical structures often exhibit substantially different image intensities across modalities. In this work, we present a comprehensive benchmark of intra-patient 3D multimodal deformable registration methods across three datasets covering different anatomical regions and difficulty levels, including both synthetic deformation recovery and real clinical scenarios. We evaluate classical optimization-based approaches and modern learning-based methods, including recent deep learning and foundation models, using complementary metrics: Average Dice similarity coefficient (DSC), average 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95), and a modality-independent structural similarity measure based on the MIND self-similarity context (MIND-SSC). Results show high variability across datasets, with learning-based methods demonstrating superior performance on large synthetic benchmarks, while only limited improvements are observed in real pelvic registration. A key finding of this study is the consistent disagreement between geometric metrics (DSC, HD95) and image-based similarity metrics (MIND-SSC), highlighting that improved overlap does not necessarily imply better global multimodal correspondence. Furthermore, anatomy-guided approaches achieve the highest overlap scores but exhibit degraded performance outside of segmented regions, revealing a trade-off between label-driven alignment and global structural coherence. Overall, our results indicate that no current method achieves robust performance across anatomies and modalities. We demonstrate that intra-patient 3D multimodal registration requires multi-criteria evaluation, including deformation-based metrics, and remains an open problem.

</details>


### [184] [V-ICAL Bench: Evaluating Video In-Context Learning for Multimodal Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15683)
*Ziqian Fan,Shibo Xu,Junjie Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Shengyuan Ding,Yifan Yang,Zhenjie Yang,Haodong Duan,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 V-ICAL 基准，用于评估多模态智能体基于视频演示的上下文学习能力，涵盖 37 个环境的 342 个交互任务；19 个先进模型中最优仅得 54.4/100，远低于人类 83.6 分，揭示该方向存在明显能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态上下文学习研究不足，尤其缺乏对交互环境中视频演示的探索。多模态智能体从视频中学习面临三重挑战：需将演示转化为可执行策略、在新视觉状态下进行落地、并依据环境反馈迭代改进动作。现有基准无法系统评估这些能力，因此需要新基准来连接上下文知识归纳与智能体核心能力（状态落地、时序记忆、规划、动态适应）。

Method: 构建 V-ICAL 基准：包含 37 个环境下的 342 个交互任务，使用人工挑选的演示视频作为任务相关的行为范例，并从指定目标初始状态出发，通过持续交互过程对智能体进行评估。

Result: 对 19 个先进多模态智能体的评测显示能力局限明显：最佳模型 Seed-2.1-Pro 仅得 54.4/100，Gemini-3.1-Pro、GPT-5.6 等领先模型均未超过 50 分，远低于人类基线 83.6。控制对比实验进一步表明，当前智能体难以稳定地将视频范例转化为有效策略，未带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: V-ICAL 揭示了多模态智能体在上下文学习能力上的关键缺口，说明该方向仍需大量后续研究。

Abstract: While In-Context Learning (ICL) enables models to adapt from exemplars without parameter updates, multimodal ICL remains largely underexplored, particularly regarding video demonstrations in interactive environments. For multimodal agents, learning from videos presents unique challenges: they must translate in-context demonstrations into executable policies, ground these policies in novel visual states, and iteratively refine actions based on environmental feedback. We introduce V-ICAL, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate video-based ICL for multimodal agents. Comprising 342 interactive tasks across 37 environments, V-ICAL utilizes human-curated demonstration videos as task-specific behavioral exemplars, evaluating agents through sustained interaction from a target initialization. The benchmark seamlessly connects in-context knowledge induction with core agentic capabilities, including state grounding, temporal memory, planning, and adaptation in dynamic environments. Extensive evaluations across 19 state-of-the-art multimodal agents reveal significant limitations: the best-performing model, Seed-2.1-Pro, achieves a score of only 54.4/100, while other leading models (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro, GPT-5.6) fail to surpass 50, far below the human baseline of 83.6. Controlled comparisons further demonstrate that current agents struggle to reliably translate video exemplars into effective policies, failing to yield consistent performance gains. Ultimately, V-ICAL exposes a critical gap in the ICL capabilities of multimodal agents, underscoring an urgent need for future research.

</details>


### [185] [VC-Attention: Value Smoothing and Softmax Casting for Low-bit Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15810)
*Xingyang Li,Dongyun Zou,Shining Zhang,Jiacheng Chen,Haocheng Xi,Lvmin Zhang,Jun-Yan Zhu,Song Han,Zhekai Zhang,Yujun Lin,Muyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练低比特注意力框架 VC-Attention，通过 V-Smooth 值平滑与 ExpCast-FP8 融合概率转换，同时解决离群值导致的精度问题和 softmax 指数导致的延迟瓶颈，在多款 GPU 与视频生成模型上实现精度与速度双提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 用于视频生成时，长时空序列使注意力成为部署主要开销，且低比特内核须兼顾精度与速度。精度受离群值限制：块的量化尺度由最大元素决定，典型元素可用表示范围被压缩；已有工作只平滑 query 和 key，而 value 离群值无固定通道或时空结构，是输出误差的主要来源。速度受 softmax 限制：低比特 Tensor Core 只加速两次矩阵乘法，中间的高精度指数运算成为数据中心 GPU 上最长的流水线阶段。

Method: VC-Attention 为免训练低比特注意力框架，包含两个组件：V-Smooth 通过轻量在线聚类重排 value token，使同一硬件块内的 token 可协同量化；仅量化减去块均值后的残差，并利用在线 softmax 已维护的行和恢复该均值。ExpCast-FP8 将 log 域分数以一次融合乘加直接映射为 E4M3 概率编码，消除 FP32 指数运算与格式转换。已实现在 B200、B300、H200、RTX PRO 6000 和 RTX 5090 上。

Result: 在 Wan2.2、LongCat-Video、HunyuanVideo-1.5 和 MiniMax-H3 上，VC-Attention 保真度优于低比特基线；注意力内核相比 BF16 FlashAttention-4 在数据中心 Blackwell 与 Hopper 上加速 1.46-1.59 倍，在工作站显卡上加速 2.3-3.6 倍；端到端生成片段分别加速 1.13-1.19 倍和 1.36-1.70 倍。

Conclusion: VC-Attention 通过值平滑与融合概率转换协同设计，在无需训练的前提下同时缓解低比特注意力的离群值精度损失与 softmax 高精度指数延迟瓶颈，在多种主流视频生成模型与多代 GPU 上验证了精度与速度的双重收益，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers deliver state-of-the-art video generation, but their long spatiotemporal sequences make attention the dominant deployment cost, and a deployable low-bit kernel must be accurate and fast. Accuracy is limited by outliers: a block's quantization scale is set by its largest entries, leaving typical entries confined to a narrow range of representable values. Prior work smooths queries and keys, but value outliers follow no fixed channel or spatiotemporal structure and remain the dominant source of output error. Speed is limited by softmax: low-bit Tensor Cores accelerate only the two matrix multiplications, so the high-precision exponential between them becomes the longest pipeline stage on datacenter GPUs. We propose VC-Attention, a training-free low-bit attention framework that addresses both by pairing Value smoothing with a fused probability Cast. V-Smooth reorders value tokens by lightweight online clustering, so the tokens in a hardware block quantize well together. It quantizes only the residual after subtracting the block mean, and restores that mean from the row sum the online softmax already maintains. ExpCast-FP8 maps log-domain scores directly to E4M3 probability codes with one fused multiply-add, eliminating the FP32 exponential and the format conversion. We implement VC-Attention for B200, B300, H200, RTX PRO 6000, and RTX 5090. Across Wan2.2, LongCat-Video, HunyuanVideo-1.5, and MiniMax-H3, VC-Attention improves fidelity over low-bit baselines, speeds up the attention kernel over BF16 FlashAttention-4 by 1.46-1.59x on datacenter Blackwell and Hopper and by 2.3-3.6x on workstation cards, and generates a clip 1.13-1.19x and 1.36-1.70x faster end to end.

</details>


### [186] [Integrating Multi-view Multi-light Surface Reconstruction into Cultural Heritage Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15833)
*Baptiste Brument,Robin Bruneau,Benjamin Coupry,Vincent Demoulin,Jean Mélou,Antoine Laurent,Fabien Castan,Jean-Denis Durou,Lilian Calvet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将多视角、多光照表面重建的计算机视觉方法集成到开源摄影测量框架Meshroom中，构建了面向文化遗产的可用工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产文档记录日益依赖基于图像的三维表面重建，摄影测量软件已使这类工作流对考古学家、修复人员和遗产技术人员变得可及。但这些工具虽在常规多视角采集上表现良好，却未常规性地利用更丰富的多视角、多光照数据，尽管后者在精细表面重建方面有潜力。在遗产领域，RTI穹顶等可控光照采集设备已用于获取光照变化的图像集，因此挑战在于将现有采集实践与近期计算机视觉方法连接起来，并使其可用于实际遗产工作流。

Method: 作者并非提出新的重建算法，而是将计算机视觉中用于多视角、多光照表面重建的先进组件整合进开源摄影测量框架Meshroom，包括完整的光度立体生态（标定、自标定和通用型）、自动物体掩膜以及多视角法线与反射率积分，并在面向遗产的可用工作流中加以暴露。

Result: 所提系统在计算机视觉研究代码与实际文化遗产应用之间提供了一个中间软件层，使近期技术更易于使用和评估。

Conclusion: 通过整合与暴露已有先进方法，本研究弥合了现有遗产采集实践与最新计算机视觉方法之间的鸿沟，为文化遗产领域的多视角多光照表面重建提供了可操作的软件工作流。

Abstract: Cultural heritage documentation increasingly relies on image-based 3D surface reconstruction, with photogrammetry software making such workflows accessible to archaeologists, conservators, and heritage technicians. These tools have been successful for conventional multi-view acquisition, but they do not routinely exploit richer multi-view, multi-light data, despite its potential for improving fine-scale surface reconstruction. This limitation is particularly relevant in heritage contexts, where controlled-light acquisition devices such as RTI domes are already used to capture illumination-varying image sets. The challenge is therefore to connect these existing acquisition practices with recent computer vision methods in a form that can be used within operational heritage workflows. In this work, we address this need by integrating state-of-the-art components from computer vision for multi-view, multi-light surface reconstruction into Meshroom, an open-source photogrammetry framework. Rather than proposing a new reconstruction algorithm, our contribution is to assemble and expose existing advanced methods, namely a complete photometric stereo ecosystem (calibrated, self-calibrated and universal), automatic object masking, and multi-view normal-and-reflectance integration, within a usable heritage-oriented workflow. The proposed system thus provides an intermediate software layer between computer vision research code and practical cultural heritage applications, making recent techniques easier to use and evaluate.

</details>


### [187] [TRACE: Two-Stage Detector-Response Estimation With Angular Cosine Expansion for Ring Artifact Correction in Photon-Counting CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15834)
*Jigang Duan,Heran Wang,Ligen Shi,Zheng Sun,Ping Yang,Xing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种两阶段无监督正弦图分解方法，用于估计并校正PCD-CT中与探测器响应非均匀性相关的固定与动态条纹误差，在实测数据上有效抑制环形伪影且保留细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCD-CT中探测器响应非均匀性会引入系统性投影误差和环形伪影。实测数据中残余条纹幅度随投影角度缓慢变化，现有固定偏置模型无法充分刻画这种动态特性。

Method: TRACE将条纹建模为固定偏置加低阶DCT分量，用少量系数描述每个探测器元的角向变化；采用可学习的分析—合成架构表示理想投影，通过两阶段优化依次分离固定条纹与动态条纹；引入角向梯度软正交约束，抑制共享DCT梯度子空间内的相关变化，减少物体结构泄漏到伪影估计中。所有参数直接在实测正弦图上优化，无需配对训练数据。

Result: 在实测QRM小鼠体模和猪蹄数据上，TRACE抑制了环形伪影，提升了图像均匀性，同时保留了边缘锐度、软组织纹理和骨小梁细节。

Conclusion: TRACE通过无监督的两阶段正弦图分解，有效校正PCD-CT中随角度缓变的响应相关误差，为无需配对数据的伪影校正提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Detector response nonuniformity introduces systematic projection errors and ring artifacts in photon-counting detector computed tomography (PCD-CT). In measured PCD-CT data, residual stripe amplitudes vary slowly with projection angle, which fixed-bias models cannot adequately capture. We propose TRACE, a two-stage unsupervised sinogram decomposition method for estimating and correcting these response-related errors. TRACE represents stripes as a fixed bias plus low-order discrete cosine transform (DCT) components, using a small number of coefficients to describe angular variations at each detector element. A learnable analysis--synthesis architecture represents the ideal projections, while two-stage optimization separates them from fixed and then dynamic stripes. An angular-gradient soft orthogonality constraint suppresses correlated variations within the shared DCT gradient subspace, reducing the leakage of object structures into the artifact estimate. All parameters are optimized directly on the measured sinogram without paired training data. Experiments on measured QRM mouse phantom and porcine trotter data show that TRACE suppresses ring artifacts and improves image uniformity while preserving edge sharpness, soft-tissue texture, and trabecular detail.

</details>


### [188] [LynnReal-Omni: Native multi-modal Video Generation for Agentic Visual Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15863)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Peijia Lin,Shaohao Rui,Yibo Zhang,Haibin Wan,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LynnReal-Omni：基于32B共享多模态扩散Transformer的原生多模态视频生成框架，统一文本/图像/参考/结构控制、编辑、修复与长视频生成，并配27B Flash实时版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型随机性强、难以精确控制，重复采样不保证成功，长时程场景在表观、交互与时间一致性上漂移；而智能体视觉创作虽提供显式参考、可编辑3D场景或可执行游戏状态，却无法保证高保真度。二者结合可实现稳定高质量生成。

Method: 构建32B共享多模态扩散Transformer，接受外观参考、可编辑3D渲染、游戏录制等异构视觉输入，统一多种任务；另训练27B Flash版用于实时渲染；建立视频清洗、主体关联、多模态标注与对齐控制的数据流水线，构造多镜头音视频语料；提出MSAVP（100提示、20指标）评测；通过模型与解码加速（含轻量VAE解码器）降低推理成本。

Result: 单张H100上，22帧540p视频热生成与解码：LynnReal-Omni为843 ms，Flash为377 ms。MSAVP分离评估指令遵循、生成合理性、视觉质量、时间行为与音频协调。

Conclusion: 该工作为实时流式视频生成奠定基础，使LynnReal-Omni成为统一、可控、高效的智能体视觉创作基座。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are stochastic and hard to control: precise content often requires repeated sampling without guaranteed success, and long-horizon scenes drift in appearance, interactions, and temporal coherence. Agentic visual creation provides explicit references, editable 3D scenes, or executable game states for stable control, but does not by itself guarantee high object or character fidelity. Combining the two can enable stable, high-quality generation. To realize this combination, we present LynnReal-Omni, a native multimodal video generation framework built on a 32B shared multimodal diffusion transformer that unifies text-to-video, image-conditioned generation, reference-guided generation, structural control, editing, degraded video restoration, and long-video generation. It accepts heterogeneous visual inputs, including appearance references, editable 3D renders, and game recordings, allowing agents to compose visual conditions within a unified model. We also train a dedicated 27B Flash shared multimodal diffusion transformer for real-time rendering. We build a systematic data pipeline for video cleaning, subject association, multimodal annotation, and aligned control construction, yielding a curated corpus of multi-shot audiovisual segments, and introduce MSAVP, a 100-prompt, 20-metric evaluation design that separates instruction following, generating plausibility, visual quality, temporal behavior, and audio coordination. LynnReal-Omni-Flash further reduces inference cost through model and decoding acceleration, including a lightweight VAE decoder; on one H100, warm generation and decoding of a 22-frame 540p video take 843 ms with LynnReal-Omni and 377 ms with Flash. These results provide a foundation for real-time streaming video generation, making LynnReal-Omni a unified, controllable, and efficient basis for agentic visual creation.

</details>


### [189] [Anatomical Grounding and Leakage-Aware Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Alzheimer's Disease Classification from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15888)
*Paul-Gabriel Nicolae,Irina Georgiana Mocanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用轻量级切片编码器研究AD分期中的两个问题：模型关注解剖无关区域、多模态模型依赖标签泄漏变量，并提出解剖参考与对比学习对齐目标的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在结构MRI上做AD分期虽准确率尚可，但常关注解剖无关区域；加入临床表格的多模态模型又常依赖用于分配诊断标签的变量，存在标签泄漏，需要系统研究这两个问题。

Method: 使用ResNet18加单层Transformer的轻量切片编码器，在ADNI-1的1,075例基线T1扫描上分析。首先用FastSurfer分割作为解剖参考，训练YOLOv8定位AD相关结构，并用Grad-CAM比较注意力；其次采用CLIP式图像-表格对比框架，将ADNIMERGE变量按标签泄漏谱系组织，进行认知评分与区域体积融合；最后限制输入为内侧颞叶的受试者裁剪区域。

Result: YOLOv8定位AD相关结构mAP_50超0.96，Grad-CAM显示纯图像分类器常关注颅骨、眼眶和背景；融合认知评分得到87.3%三分类准确率（视为泄漏驱动的上界），融合区域体积为73.0%；MCI vs. CN中，对齐认知评分时纯图像头52.4%，对齐体积时73.8%；内侧颞叶裁剪将纯图像三分类准确率从58.7%提升至65.1%。

Conclusion: 结果来自小规模平衡测试集的单次运行，报告了置信区间及阻碍与已发表数字直接比较的协议差异；对比目标的选择会改变图像编码器所学内容，限定解剖区域可提升纯图像性能，融合结果需警惕标签泄漏。

Abstract: Deep networks trained on structural MRI for Alzheimer's disease (AD) staging often reach reasonable accuracy while attending to anatomically irrelevant regions, and multimodal models that add clinical tables frequently rely on variables that were used to assign the diagnostic label in the first place. We study both issues with a deliberately lightweight slice-based encoder (ResNet18 with a one-layer Transformer over slices) on 1,075 baseline T1-weighted scans from ADNI-1. First, we use FastSurfer segmentations as an anatomical reference: YOLOv8 models trained on segmentation-derived labels localize Alzheimer-relevant structures with mAP_50 above 0.96, and a Grad-CAM comparison shows that the image-only classifier frequently attends to the skull, orbits and background. Second, we adapt a CLIP-style image - tabular contrastive framework and organize ADNIMERGE variables along a label-leakage spectrum. Fusion with cognitive scores yields 87.3% three-way accuracy, which we treat as a leakage-driven upper bound rather than an imaging result; fusion with regional volumes yields 73.0%. We observe that the choice of contrastive target changes what the image encoder learns: on MCI vs. CN, the image-only head reaches 52.4% when the encoder is aligned to cognitive scores and 73.8\% when aligned to volumes, although no tabular input is used at inference. Third, restricting the input to a per-subject crop of the medial temporal lobe raises image-only three-way accuracy from 58.7% to 65.1%. All results come from single runs on a small balanced test set, and we report confidence intervals and the protocol differences that prevent direct comparison with published numbers.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Token Merging for Multilingual Speech Recognition: A Systematic Study Across Model Scale and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13151)
*Dylan Luke Holyoak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了token merging在Whisper多语言语音识别模型上的效果，覆盖16种语言、3种模型规模，并测试其与DoRA微调的交互作用，发现token merging能提升推理效率且几乎不损失转录准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper等领先的多语言语音识别模型能够无需语言特定训练即转录低资源语言，但部署时计算成本高昂。Token merging可通过动态合并冗余特征来缩短推理时的序列长度且无需重训练，然而其在不同语言和模型规模下的系统效果尚不明确。

Method: 在Whisper模型家族上系统评估token merging，覆盖十六种不同语言和三种模型规模，并测试token merging与低资源语言上的DoRA微调的交互作用。

Result: 合并token提升了计算效率，在大多数低资源语言和模型规模下转录准确率几乎无损失，且在模型经过微调后依然有效。

Conclusion: Token merging是一种非常实用的方法，可使多语言语音识别更快、部署成本更低。

Abstract: Leading multilingual speech recognition models like Whisper transcribe diverse, low-resource languages without language-specific training but are computationally expensive to deploy. Token merging mitigates this inefficiency by dynamically combining redundant features, shortening the sequence length during inference without requiring retraining. In this paper, we systematically evaluate token merging on the Whisper model family across sixteen diverse languages and three different model sizes. We also test how token merging interacts with fine-tuning (DoRA) on low-resource languages. Our findings show that merging tokens increases computational efficiency with almost no loss in transcription accuracy across most low-resource languages and model sizes, and it works even after the model has been fine-tuned. Our results demonstrate that token merging is a highly practical method for making multilingual speech recognition faster and cheaper to deploy.

</details>


### [191] [PhysMent: An Interactive Approach For LLM Reasoning In Physics Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13152)
*Joseph Chan,Utkarsh Jha,Xiyin Yang,Abhinav Jarajapu,Anik Sahai,Eddie Hu,Robin Jeshua Deepak,Stefano Saravalle,Aditya Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysMent是一个基于MuJoCo模拟器、通过迭代工具交互评估大模型物理推理能力的基准，包含105个经典力学场景；结果显示模型在定性单概念任务上表现尚可（最高80%），但在需要精确多步实验的定量任务上大幅退化（多数低于30%），瓶颈在于程序性的多步工具使用而非概念理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在静态科学基准上表现优异，但其通过主动实验来推理物理世界的能力仍缺乏了解。现有静态基准直接提供所有量值，无法评估模型主动探索和实验的能力，因此需要构建一个要求模型主动获取信息的交互式基准。

Method: 提出PhysMent基准，通过工具中介的方式与MuJoCo物理模拟器进行迭代交互。模型需通过施力、查询物体状态、推进时间、修改场景几何等方式主动发现信息后再作答。基准包含105个经典力学场景，覆盖四种难度体系（简单/困难 × 单概念/多概念）、三种场景模态（标准、物体创建、隐藏物体）及一个场景操纵类别，并采用六维评分框架评估。

Result: 当前模型在定性单概念任务上表现尚可（最高80%准确率），但在需要精确多步实验流程的定量任务上显著退化：多数模型在最难的单概念类别上低于30%。七个模型的准确率范围为25%至67%。失败原因主要是过早提交答案、探索效率低以及对模拟器反馈的grounding不一致，而非概念性缺陷。

Conclusion: LLM主动进行物理实验推理的主要瓶颈是程序性的（自适应多步工具使用），而非概念负荷。该基准揭示了当前模型在主动实验物理推理方面的不足，为未来提升模型工具使用和探索策略提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform strongly on static science benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about the physical world through active experimentation remains poorly understood. We introduce PhysMent, a benchmark that evaluates LLM physical reasoning via iterative, toolmediated interaction with a MuJoCo physics simulator. Unlike static benchmarks that supply all quantities upfront, PhysMent requires models to discover information by applying forces, querying object states, advancing time, and modifying scene geometry before answering. The benchmark comprises 105 scenes of classical mechanics, organized across four difficulty regimes (Easy/Hard and Single/Multi), three scene modalities (standard, object creation, hidden objects), and a scene-manipulation category, evaluated with a six-dimensional scoring framework. Results show that current models perform reasonably well on qualitative single-concept tasks (up to 80% accuracy) but degrade substantially on quantitative tasks that demand precise, multi-step experimental procedures: most models fall below 30% on the hardest single-concept category, where the bottleneck is procedural (adaptive multi-step tool use) rather than conceptual load. Across the seven models, accuracy ranges from 25% to 67%, with failures due to premature answer submission, inefficient exploration, and inconsistent grounding in simulator feedback rather than conceptual gaps.

</details>


### [192] [RFCLLM: Evaluating LLMs' Reasoning Ability of Network Protocol State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13389)
*Anqi Chen,Dan Goldwasser,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估大语言模型（LLM）在将协议的自然语言规范映射为形式化有限状态机模型时的理解能力，通过4类任务、1482个查询和16种协议，考察LLM隐式表示与人工构建的基准模型之间的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者与从业者常假设LLM生成的规范映射（用于网络安全或测试）能完美理解协议规范，但这一假设在现实中可能不成立，因此需要实证评估LLM对规范的正确理解程度。

Method: 设计了4类任务、覆盖16种协议的1482个任务查询，评测LLM在自然语言描述下对有限状态转移系统的隐式表示；分析了不同的评判偏见、任务间的固有难度差距、4种上下文类型的影响以及协议特征的作用。

Result: 通过多任务多协议的实证评估，揭示了LLM隐式FSM表示与人工基准模型之间的对齐程度，并识别出不同评判偏见、任务难度差异、上下文类型和协议特征对结果的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为验证LLM在协议规范的有限状态机推理中是否真正可信迈出了一步。

Abstract: Mapping textual specifications into formal representations is essential for ensuring the correctness of protocol designs and implementations. LLM-generated mappings, used for networking security or testing, are assumed to capture a perfect understanding of the specification, which may not hold in practice. The goal of this paper is to assess the extent to which LLMs can interpret the specification correctly. We examine the degree to which an LLM's implicit representation of a finite-state transition system-defined via natural language descriptions-aligns with a manually generated ground-truth model. We designed 4 tasks and 1482 task queries for 16 protocols. We evaluated different judge biases, observed the inherent difficulty gaps between tasks, looked into the effect of 4 context types, and the influence of protocol characteristics. Our work contributes to a step toward verifying whether LLMs can really be trusted in FSM (Finite State Machine) reasoning of protocol specifications.

</details>


### [193] [Hindsight Bias in Clinical Temporal Reasoning: How Future Data Exposure Affects Large Language Model Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13454)
*Misaki Matsuura,Sayantan Kumar,Ojas Kadam,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个配对基准，用于测量临床时间推理中与后见之明偏差一致的结果条件性偏移，发现完整时间线暴露会稳定诱发偏差，而时间遮蔽可在不降低准确率的情况下减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策本质上是前瞻性的，但临床语言模型常在包含最终诊断、治疗反应和结局的回顾性病历上评估。这种评估可能奖励模型利用未来信息，而非在决策时点的不确定性下进行推理，从而掩盖真实推理能力并夸大性能。

Method: 构建包含171份病例报告（40例脓毒症、131例GLP-1/糖尿病）的配对基准，数据来自PubMed Central开放获取子集，每例同时以文本叙述和人工标注及LLM生成的时间序列文本（TTS）表示。每个问题绑定临床上有意义的截断点，并配对前瞻性参考答案与结果一致的后见之明陷阱。模型使用截断TTS或完整时间线作答，并变化叙述来源与TTS标注来源。采用准确率、后见之明陷阱率、答案不稳定率与后见之明偏差率四项指标评估。

Result: 在GPT 5.6 Sol、Gemma 4、GLM 5.2和Opus 5上，完整时间线暴露均产生一致的后见之明敏感性偏移；时间遮蔽可降低偏差且不降低准确率。

Conclusion: 回顾性评估会系统性地诱发临床语言模型的后见之明偏差，需采用前瞻性、时间遮蔽的评测方式来更真实地衡量模型在决策时点的推理能力。

Abstract: Clinical decisions are prospective, but clinical language models are often evaluated on retrospective records that reveal the final diagnosis, treatment response, and outcome. Such evaluations may reward the use of future information rather than reasoning under the uncertainty present at the decision point. We introduce a paired benchmark for measuring outcome-conditioned shifts consistent with hindsight bias in clinical temporal reasoning. It contains 171 case reports from the PubMed Central Open Access Subset---40 sepsis and 131 GLP-1/diabetes cases---represented as both textual narratives and human-annotated and LLM-generated textual time series (TTS). For each case, questions are tied to a clinically meaningful cutoff and paired with a prospective reference answer and an outcome-consistent \emph{hindsight trap}. Models answer each question using either a TTS truncated at the cutoff or the complete timeline; additional conditions vary the narrative source (original or synthetic) and TTS annotation source (human or LLM). We evaluate accuracy (Acc), hindsight trap rate (HTR), answer instability rate (AIR), and hindsight bias rate (HBR), each of which captures different signals of hindsight bias. Across GPT 5.6 Sol, Gemma 4, GLM 5.2, and Opus 5, full timeline exposure produces consistent hindsight-sensitive shifts, while temporal masking reduces bias without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [194] [A Hybrid Hierarchical 1D-CNN-BiLSTM Framework for Extractive Summarization of Biomedical and Clinical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13481)
*Saad Bin Ather,Muhammad Saif,Ali Hassan Khan,Manzer Abbas,Hajra Waheed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合分层CNN-LSTM抽取式摘要器，通过直接从源文选取句子来避免生成式摘要的幻觉问题，在生物医学领域实现了可靠的事实性摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的摘要虽然流畅，但会捏造事实，在生物医学和临床领域带来严重风险。作者希望通过从流程中移除生成步骤、将摘要任务重构为抽取式句子选择，从设计上保证事实可靠性。

Method: 采用堆叠多核卷积将句子级嵌入组合为更丰富的句间表示，再用双向LSTM建模文档长程依赖；轻量级评分头为每句分配重要性分数，并以二元交叉熵针对抽取式oracle标签进行端到端训练。推理时使用动态均值加标准差阈值配合top-3回退策略选句，并按时间顺序重排为最终摘要。

Result: 在PubMed上，该架构优于单独的CNN和LSTM基线，消融实验显示更宽的卷积感受野能提升句子评分效果。在MIMIC-CXR和MIMIC-IV BHC上，模型对非结构化叙述表现良好，但在高度模板化的报告上倾向于退化为位置基线。

Conclusion: 由于每个输出句子都直接从输入复制，模型避免了生成导致的事实漂移。结果表明，结构约束可以为事实可溯的摘要系统提供可靠路径，使系统在设计上可信而非依赖事后纠正。

Abstract: Large language models have made abstractive summarization remarkably fluent, but generated summaries can hallucinate facts, posing serious risks in biomedical and clinical domains. We address this by removing generation from the pipeline and framing summarization as extractive sentence selection. Our Hybrid Hierarchical CNN-LSTM Summarizer uses stacked multi-kernel convolutions to compose sentence-level embeddings into richer inter-sentence representations, followed by a bidirectional LSTM to model long-range dependencies across the document. A lightweight scoring head assigns per-sentence importance scores and is trained end-to-end with binary cross-entropy against oracle extractive labels. At inference, a dynamic mean-plus-standard-deviation threshold with a top-3 fallback selects sentences directly from the source and chronologically reorders them into the final summary. Since every output sentence is copied from the input, the model avoids generation-induced factual drift. On PubMed, our architecture outperforms isolated CNN and LSTM baselines, while ablations show that wider convolutional receptive fields improve sentence scoring. On MIMIC-CXR and MIMIC-IV BHC, the model performs well on unstructured narratives but defaults toward positional baselines on highly templated reports. These results suggest that structural constraints can provide a reliable path toward factually grounded summarization systems that are trustworthy by design rather than by correction.

</details>


### [195] [From Token Probabilities to Semantic Constraints: Towards Declarative Probabilistic Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13520)
*Kyle Richardson,Cullen Anderson,Pranav Balakrishnan,Takuto Ban,Daksha Ladia,Ankita Gupta,Marisa Hudspeth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ModelLog，一个用于预训练评估的声明式概率框架，将评估目标表示为对 token 级预测的符号约束，并度量模型分布满足这些约束的强度，从而把评估与学习信号通过共享语义联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型发展迅速，但如何评估其获得的知识与推理能力，以及这些评估与预训练学习信号之间的关系，仍缺乏清晰的形式化理解。仅依靠 token 似然或答案准确率难以刻画模型行为的语义结构。

Method: ModelLog 将评估目标指定为对 token 级预测的符号约束，衡量模型分布对这些约束的满足程度；作者设计了针对否定、互斥性和一致性的新任务套件进行探索，并进一步将评估分数解释为损失，分析其梯度所反映的逻辑强度、信息量和变量级敏感性。

Result: 实验发现模型在这些任务上存在系统性失败，而这些问题仅凭 token 似然或答案准确率难以表征。同时，评估分数作为损失时，其梯度能够体现逻辑强度、信息量和变量级敏感性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过共享语义将评估与学习联系起来，提出的评估方法既能诊断模型行为，也有助于澄清学习的语义结构，为预训练评估提供了新的形式化工具。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have improved rapidly, many fundamental questions remain about how to evaluate the knowledge and reasoning abilities they acquire, and how such evaluations relate to the learning signals used in pre-training. In this paper, we propose ModelLog, a declarative probabilistic framework for pre-training evaluation that makes the semantic structure of model behavior explicit and provides new formal tools for relating evaluation to learning. ModelLog specifies evaluation targets as symbolic constraints over token-level predictions and measures how strongly a model's distribution satisfies those constraints. We explore the framework through a new suite of tasks targeting negation, mutual exclusivity, and consistency, finding systematic failures that are difficult to characterize through token likelihood or answer accuracy alone. We further show that these evaluation scores can also be interpreted as losses, whose gradients reflect logical strength, informativeness, and variable-level sensitivity. This links evaluation and learning through a shared semantics, suggesting evaluation methods that diagnose model behavior while also helping to clarify the semantic structure of learning.

</details>


### [196] [Domain-Specific Jargon in Large Language Models: A Comparative Analysis between General-Purpose and Specialist Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13556)
*Darin Keng,Zhewei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建两个医学术语评测基准，发现通用Llama-3.1在术语理解上竟优于医学微调版本，并通过机制可解释性揭示微调模型存在系统性误校准，可用组件重加权缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用任务上表现优异，但在高度专业的技术领域性能常下降；同时，模型如何以参数化形式编码领域术语知识尚不清楚，缺乏针对专业术语的评测基准与机制层面的理解。

Method: 提出两个新的医学术语评测基准；对比评测通用Llama-3.1与其医学领域数据微调变体；借助机制可解释性工具分析模型内部组件（如对术语偏好预测的组件）的作用；对相关组件施加重加权策略；并测试这些组件在材料科学术语任务上的迁移性。

Result: 通用模型在两个基准任务上均优于医学微调模型；微调模型未重组参数化知识，而是过度依赖一小部分与"偏好术语预测"相关的组件，呈现系统性误校准；组件重加权能有效抑制这些组件并弥合与通用基线的差距；部分术语敏感组件可迁移到材料科学术语任务，暗示其编码了部分领域无关的专业术语概念。

Conclusion: 医学微调检查点并未提升术语理解，反而弱于通用版本，说明领域适配不必然带来专业术语性能提升；研究为专业术语的机制分析与组件级干预提供了案例与方法参考。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable proficiency on general-purpose tasks, yet their performance often degrades in highly-specialized technical domains. Moreover, little is known about how parametric knowledge of domain-specific terms is encoded within these models. We address this gap by contributing two novel medical jargon evaluation benchmarks and evaluate a general-purpose Llama-3.1 model against a variant fine-tuned on medical-domain data. Surprisingly, the general-purpose model outperforms the medically fine-tuned model on both tasks. Using mechanistic interpretability tools, we find systematic patterns of miscalibration for the medically fine-tuned model. Instead of reorganizing parametric knowledge, the fine-tuned model places greater emphasis on a small subset of model components associated with jargon-favoring predictions. We find that applying component reweighting strategies against the benchmark tasks successfully suppresses these components and closes the gap with the general-purpose baseline. We also observe that some jargon-sensitive components transfer knowledge to the same tasks involving materials science jargon, suggesting they encode a partially domain-agnostic notion of specialized terminology. Our results provide a case study in which a medically fine-tuned checkpoint does not improve jargon comprehension over its general-purpose counterpart, highlighting that domain adaptation should not be assumed to yield better performance on specialized terminology.

</details>


### [197] [Toward Complete Hospital Discharge Summarization with Abstract Meaning Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13581)
*Paul Landes,Sitara Rao,Aaron Jeremy Chaise,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种证据驱动的对齐框架，用于临床就诊级别的出院小结生成，将"来源溯源"作为一等约束，通过语义图与深度学习模型为每句摘要选择、组织内容并附上显式证据链接，在MIMIC-III和UIC Health两个语料上验证，并公开代码与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院小结篇幅冗长，自动生成可减轻文书负担、把医生时间还给患者。但大语言模型存在幻觉这一"致命弱点"，在临床文档中后果严重，因此需要一种能确保内容有据可查、可溯源的生成方法。

Method: 提出证据驱动的对齐框架，在临床就诊级别处理出院小结生成；将溯源作为一等约束，采用语义图与深度学习模型，通过跨文档语义对齐来选择和组织每一句摘要，并为其附带指向来源文本片段的显式证据链接。

Result: 在公开语料MIMIC-III和伊利诺伊大学医院（UIC Health）医生撰写的临床笔记两个语料上展示了实验结果，同时公开了源代码与训练好的模型。

Conclusion: 将溯源作为核心约束、以证据链接支撑每句输出的框架，可在临床文档摘要中有效扼制大语言模型的幻觉问题，具有良好的通用性与可复现性。

Abstract: Discharge summaries are lengthy medical documents that summarize a hospital in-patient visit. Automatically generating them can reduce documentation burden and return clinician time to patient care. Whereas Large Language Model (LLMs) could be used for this task, their Achilles heel is hallucinations, which can have drastic consequences for clinical documentation. We present an evidence-driven alignment framework for discharge summarization at the clinical encounter level, that treats provenance as a first-class constraint, using semantic graphs and deep learning models. Each summary sentence is selected and organized via cross-document semantic alignment and is accompanied by explicit evidence links to its source spans. We show our results on two corpora: a publicly available corpus (MIMIC-III) and clinical notes written by physicians at the University of Illinois Hospital (UIC Health). Additionally, we make source code and trained models available.

</details>


### [198] [Same Patient, Different Order: Action-Level Reliability of Clinical LLM Agents Under Repeated Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13582)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Manpreet Singh,Zhoutian Han,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出临床智能体基准仅在单次运行下评分，无法发现"相同输入产生不同医嘱"的动作级分歧，为此提出"同输入重跑"评估协议与六项可靠性指标，并在 1000 次 MedAgentBench 运行中验证了该分歧的普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床智能体会开检查、开药和转诊，但现有基准（如 MedAgentBench）对每个任务只跑一次并只比较得分，不检验相同输入是否产生相同动作。这导致"分数一致而实际医嘱不同"的分歧被完全掩盖，缺少对动作级可靠性的度量。

Method: 提出"same-input rerun"（同输入重跑）：固定全部输入多次重放同一任务，比较生成的医嘱而非分数，并设计六项可靠性指标。实验覆盖 MedAgentBench 的 50 个任务（来自其五个可写操作族）、两个参数量低于 100 亿且量化到 4 bit 的开源权重模型、两个温度设置，共 1000 次运行。

Result: 在 8B 模型、温度 0.7 下：43 个开单组全部在五次相同运行中产生不同医嘱集合；26 组只在部分运行中开出该医嘱；28 组出现不同的编码值、剂量或检验项。其中 22 组的基准得分相同（均为失败判定）但行为实质不同，4B 模型在 0.7 下的 10 个分歧组也全部如此。此外，多次运行的医嘱会到达不同执行端点，其中一个被记录服务器拒绝，而智能体却收到成功反馈。

Conclusion: 研究证明动作级分歧确实存在且能被分数掩盖（而非给出可推广的比率）。作者呼吁临床智能体基准采用重复运行评估、报告动作级稳定性，并提供忠实反映执行结果的运行环境反馈。

Abstract: A clinical agent benchmark can report the same verdict on identical inputs while the agent files a materially different order on each run. Such agents order tests, request medications and place referrals, yet benchmarks typically score one run per task and rarely ask whether identical inputs produce identical actions; MedAgentBench, the benchmark we use, scores a single attempt and says so. To measure this gap we introduce "same-input rerun", which replays a task with every input held fixed and compares the orders rather than the score, with six reliability metrics, and apply it to 1000 MedAgentBench runs across 50 tasks from its five write-capable families, two open-weight models below ten billion parameters quantised to four bits, and two temperatures. The study establishes that action-level divergence exists and can pass unrecorded by the score, not that any rate generalises. Under the 8B model at temperature 0.7, all 43 ordering groups emit a different set of orders across five identical runs, 26 emit the order on some runs and not others, and 28 record a different coded value, dose or analyte. In 22 of those 43 the benchmark reports the same failing verdict for materially different behaviour, as it does for all 10 divergent groups of the 4B model at 0.7. Orders also reach different endpoints across runs, one of which the record server rejects while the agent is told it succeeded. These findings motivate repeated-run evaluation, action-level stability reporting and execution-faithful environment feedback in clinical-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [In the Blind: Building Pseudo-References for MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13611)
*Diptesh Kanojia,Chi-kiu Lo,Archchana Sindhujan,Samuel Larkin,Greg Hanneman,Alon Lavie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了WMT26通用机器翻译任务中为10个无人工参考的语向构建伪参考译文的方法，利用多模型翻译、无参考质量估计（QE）与逐文档选择器，并加入语言识别惩罚项消除"错误语言"失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WMT26通用机器翻译任务的10个语向完全没有人工参考译文（既无人工翻译也无人工后编辑），因此无法用常规方式评估或构建参考译文。作者需要在这些语向上构建可用的伪参考，并解决无参考条件下的选择难题。

Method: 对10个无参考语向及另外6个部分有人工参考的语向，使用7个模型在最多5种提示条件下翻译3,277份官方文档，共产生26种系统-提示组合；用3个无参考质量估计（QE）模型对每个候选译文打分；由逐文档选择器选出一个译文，再用GPT-5.5在必要时进行后编辑。选择流程在WMT25人工评判数据上校准，并在分数融合中加入按置信度缩放的语言识别惩罚项。

Result: 无参考条件下的QE引导选择暴露出一种失败模式：指标会把用错误语言写出的流畅文本排在正确译文之上。加入置信度缩放的语言识别惩罚后，错误语言数量降至零，且新选择器在MetricX上仍优于其所替代的排名融合基线。在构建后发布的人工评估中，当选择器保留了前沿模型候选时，其参考译文与最强参赛系统相当；未保留时最多低17个ESA分。

Conclusion: 无参考环境下的选择质量对最终参考质量影响显著，语言识别惩罚能有效消除错误语言这一关键失败模式。作者公开了选择方法及每条参考译文的来源信息（https://github.com/surrey-nlp/PseudoRef）。

Abstract: The WMT26 General MT task evaluates systems on 10 language pairs that have no human references (neither translated from scratch nor post-edited from MT output by humans). We describe how we built the pseudo-references for these pairs and six other language pairs (in which some forms of human references are available): seven models translate the 3,277 official documents under up to five prompt conditions, giving a total of 26 system-prompt combinations; then three reference-free quality estimation (QE) models score every candidate; and a per-document selector picks one translation, which GPT-5.5 post-edits where needed. Working without references exposed a failure mode of QE-guided selection: the metrics rank fluent output in the wrong language above correct translations. Adding a confidence-scaled language identification penalty to the score fusion drives the wrong-language count to zero, and the resulting selector still scores better on MetricX than the rank-fusion baseline it replaces. Since no references were available for these pairs while we were building them, we calibrate every selection decision on last year's WMT25 human judgments. The human evaluation, released after construction, shows the cost of getting selection wrong: our references stand with the strongest participating systems when the selector kept a frontier-model candidate, and fall up to 17 ESA points below them when it did not. We release the selection method and the provenance of every reference (https://github.com/surrey-nlp/PseudoRef)

</details>


### [200] [The University of Melbourne WMT 2026 CreoleMT Submission: A Domain-Balanced Approach to Low-Resource Pacific Creole Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13615)
*Raphaël Merx,Nick Thieberger,Ekaterina Vylomova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍针对WMT26克里奥尔语翻译任务的太平洋克里奥尔语（Tok Pisin、Bislama、Solomon Pijin）机器翻译系统，通过域不平衡预训练加域平衡微调及多种数据增强技术，在多个测试集上超越开源基线3+ chrF++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太平洋克里奥尔语（Tok Pisin、Bislama、Solomon Pijin）在机器翻译中资源匮乏，且现有模型在广泛领域上的表现不足，需要针对多领域覆盖的翻译模型。

Method: 先在大规模域不平衡数据上预训练，再在多样化域平衡数据上继续微调；采用LLM辅助的重拼写与对齐、回译，以及从Gemini蒸馏补充训练数据中原先缺失的领域。

Result: 在Bouquet及一个由口语转写构成的新测试集上，使用人工原文参考时，模型在所有翻译方向上比开源模型基线高出3个以上chrF++点。

Conclusion: 该方案有效提升了太平洋克里奥尔语的多领域翻译性能；未来计划为Solomon Pijin和Bislama构建人工翻译测试集，并将最佳模型蒸馏为更小且保持广泛领域覆盖的模型。

Abstract: For our submission to the WMT26 Creole Language Translation Shared Task, we focus on machine translation (MT) models for Pacific creoles: Tok Pisin, Bislama, and Solomon Pijin, with particular attention to broad domain performance. After pre-training on a large collection of domain-imbalanced data, we continue fine-tuning on a diverse mix of domain-balanced data. We rely on a number of data collection and preparation techniques, including LLM-assisted respelling and alignment, back-translation, and distillation from Gemini for domains originally not present in training data. Evaluated on Bouquet and a novel test set made of spoken language transcripts, our models beat open model baselines by 3+ chrF++ points in all directions with human-original references. Looking ahead, we plan to develop human-translated test sets for Solomon Pijin and Bislama, and to distil our best models into much smaller ones that retain broad domain coverage.

</details>


### [201] [LayerRoute: Adaptive Layer-Skipping with LoRA-Preserved Quality for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13682)
*Prateek Kumar Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LayerRoute：结合逐层硬门控路由（直通估计器训练）与LoRA联合微调的参数高效自适应层跳过方法，在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct上实现稳定收敛的跳过模式、真实加速与质量保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升Transformer推理效率，同时保持甚至改善模型质量，并解决层跳过中路由是否真实依输入计算、结果是否可复现的问题。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct的24个transformer块上各加一个轻量逐层路由器（约21.5K参数）与LoRA适配器（rank 8，约1.08M参数），以门控正则化的语言建模目标联合训练，采用直通估计器实现硬门控路由。

Result: 10个独立随机种子均收敛到相同跳过结构（第8-16层共9个中间层可跳过）；每次运行均获真实墙钟加速（1.02x-1.06x，均值1.04x）；困惑度在两评估划分上均改善（平均delta = -1.16与-1.11）；87-100%留出样本的门控决策会改变实际跳过/执行结果，证实输入依赖路由；单张A100训练不足7分钟。

Conclusion: LayerRoute是一种参数高效、可复现、具真实加速且质量不降的自适应层跳过方案，路由器执行非平凡的逐输入计算，附加开销可忽略，并附完整可复现方法与诊断分析。

Abstract: We introduce LayerRoute, a parameter-efficient method for adaptive transformer layer-skipping that combines per-layer hard-gated routing (trained via a straight-through estimator) with joint LoRA fine-tuning. LayerRoute augments each of the 24 transformer blocks in Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct with a lightweight per-layer router (~21.5K parameters) and LoRA adapters (rank 8, ~1.08M parameters), training both jointly under a gate-regularized language-modeling objective. Across 10 independently-seeded training runs, LayerRoute converges to an identical skip-pattern structure in every run - a consistent set of 9 middle layers (8-16) becomes skip-eligible in all 10 seeds - and delivers genuine, verified wallclock speedup in every run (1.02x-1.06x, mean 1.04x). Quality is preserved or improved in every configuration tested: joint LoRA adaptation yields a perplexity improvement over the unmodified backbone in all 10 seeds (mean delta = -1.16 and -1.11 across the two evaluation splits used). We further verify the router performs genuine, non-trivial per-input computation: gate decisions in skip-eligible layers change the actual skip/run outcome for 87-100% of held-out samples, confirming real input-dependent routing rather than a fixed pruning pattern. LayerRoute trains in under 7 minutes on a single A100 and adds negligible overhead beyond the routing decision itself. We report our full reproducibility methodology, including a systematic diagnostic investigation into what determines the router's per-input decisions, as part of this work.

</details>


### [202] [Not all Negation Cues are Equal: Affixal Negations Yield Better Negation Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13685)
*Tian Tan,Eduardo Blanco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建包含180万样本、200多种否定线索的大规模数据集NegCue，覆盖单词、多词和词缀否定，发现不同否定类型对性能提升贡献不均，词缀否定提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定理解一直是语言模型和大语言模型的长期挑战。已有研究主要关注not、never等少数高频单词否定线索，对更广泛的否定类型及现代大模型探索不足。

Method: 构建NegCue数据集，包含超180万样本，覆盖单词、多词和词缀三类否定，涉及200多种独特线索；在NegCue上对仅编码器语言模型和大语言模型进行进一步预训练，并在五个下游基准上评估不同否定类型的影响。

Result: 在相同训练规模下，不同否定类型对性能提升贡献不均：词缀否定带来最大提升，而常被研究的单词否定提升有限；进一步预训练能同时改善语言模型和大语言模型的否定理解能力。

Conclusion: 应重视更广泛的否定类型（尤其是词缀否定），进一步预训练是提升语言模型与大语言模型否定理解的有效途径。

Abstract: Negation remains a longstanding challenge for both language models (LMs) and large language models (LLMs). Prior work mainly focuses on a small set of high-frequency single-word negation cues, such as not and never, with limited exploration of broader negation types and modern LLMs. To address this gap, we construct NegCue, a large-scale dataset containing over 1.8M samples spanning single-word, multi-word, and affixal negation with more than 200 unique cues. We further pre-train both encoder-only LMs and LLMs on NegCue to investigate how different negation types affect negation understanding. Experiments on five downstream benchmarks show that negation types contribute unevenly to performance gains under the same training scale. In particular, affixal negation yields the largest improvements, while the gains from the commonly studied single-word negation remain modest. Moreover, our results demonstrate that further pre-training improves negation understanding for both LMs and LLMs.

</details>


### [203] [When Edit Localization Amplifies Relative Selection Bias: Gradient Geometry, Target Mismatch, and Importance Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13709)
*Shengwei Zhang,Haoda Dai,Yifei Li,Yuheng Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文分析人类修正中的选择性反馈问题，将局部梯度分解为被编辑与未触及分量，推导相对选择偏差的解析性质及估计误差，并在机器翻译实验中验证硬定位虽相对偏差更高但绝对偏差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类修正虽能标识可编辑片段，但收到修正的样本可能来自选择性反馈渠道，导致偏差。论文希望刻画这种局部化（localization）如何影响选择偏差这一诊断指标。

Method: 在固定模型检查点，将局部化梯度分解为被编辑分量与保留的未触及分量。将平方相对选择偏差表示为二次型之比，其导数符号由显式二次多项式决定。推导面向统一全梯度目标的有限样本均方误差、解析最优保留系数及固定裁剪扩展。用精确有限总体计算与每样本量10,000次蒙特卡洛重复验证。

Result: 定位可能增加、减少或非单调改变该诊断，方向取决于分量偏差与几何结构。在三个声明设置（两个翻译方向、英德扩展涉及7389万原生参数）中，硬定位相对偏差更高但绝对偏差低于完全保留；未触及分量偏差非零，被选分量均值内积为负。输出偏差诊断显示两方向相对偏差的端点排序一致，且存在一个内部极大值。

Conclusion: 证据将相对放大与绝对梯度误差区分开，并确立了估计性质，但未推断翻译质量提升，也未识别真实投诉倾向。各机制复用同语言记录并含混合成分，不构成独立重复。

Abstract: Human corrections identify editable spans, but the examples receiving corrections may come from a selective feedback channel. We analyze this interaction at a fixed model checkpoint by decomposing a localized gradient into edited and retained untouched components. Squared relative selection bias is a ratio of quadratics whose derivative has the sign of an explicit quadratic polynomial. Localization can increase, decrease, or nonmonotonically change this diagnostic; its direction depends on component biases and geometry. Oracle importance weighting recovers the population mean for each fixed localization objective, but these objectives have different targets. Against one common full-gradient target, we derive the finite-sample mean-squared error, an analytic optimal retention coefficient, and a fixed-clipping extension. Exact finite-population calculations and 10,000 Monte Carlo repetitions per sample size verify the identities and counterexamples. Public human-post-edit experiments use two translation directions and pretrained models, with declared synthetic selection. An English-German extension differentiates 73.89 million native parameters. In all three declared settings, hard localization has higher relative bias but lower absolute bias than full retention. Untouched-component biases are nonzero and selected component means have negative inner products, so the general criterion applies where the simple unbiased/aligned explanation fails. Output-bias diagnostics show the same endpoint ordering of relative bias across both directions, with one interior maximum. Mechanisms reuse each language's records and include a mixture; they are not independent replications. The evidence separates relative amplification from absolute gradient error and establishes estimation properties, without inferring translation-quality gains or identifying actual complaint propensities.

</details>


### [204] [Scaling Hindi Quantum Natural Language Processing through Automatic Pregroup Supertagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13721)
*Gautami Sanjay Naik,Krishna Bhatia,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain,H Aswath Babu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将印地语前置组语法的自动超标注建模为词元级分类任务，比较了多种方法，发现低资源下简单方法效果最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印地语QNLP依赖前置组语法将语法结构转化为图解表示和量子电路，但语法类型标注仍主要靠人工，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 构建380句人工标注语料，将印地语前置组超标注形式化为词元级分类任务，评估词汇、上下文、提示、词汇修复及后缀/形态感知方法。

Result: 上下文回退完成准确率最佳达64.56%，原始Qwen2.5提示仅11.65%；词汇修复将LLM辅助预测提升至64.08%；已见和无歧义词更易预测，后缀/形态特征提升karaka词元准确率但未改善整体性能。

Conclusion: 自动印地语前置组标注可行，可减少未来多语言QNLP流程对人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Quantum Natural Language Processing (QNLP) uses pregroup grammars to translate grammatical structure into diagrammatic representations and quantum circuits. Recent Hindi QNLP work has shown that Hindi-specific pregroup grammars can support grammar-sensitive compositional models, but grammatical type assignment is still largely manual, limiting scalability. This paper formulates automatic Hindi pregroup supertagging as a token-level classification task. Using a manually annotated corpus of 380 Hindi sentences, we evaluate lexical, contextual, prompting-based, lexical-repair, and suffix/morphology-aware methods. Results show that simple lexical and contextual models are strong in this low-resource setting: contextual backoff achieves the best completed accuracy of 64.56\%, while raw Qwen2.5 prompting reaches only 11.65\%. Lexical repair raises LLM-assisted prediction to 64.08\%, demonstrating the value of constraining generative outputs with symbolic grammar knowledge. Diagnostic analysis further shows that seen and unambiguous tokens are much easier than unseen tokens, and suffix/morphology features improve karaka-token accuracy but not overall performance. These results show that automatic Hindi pregroup assignment is feasible and can reduce reliance on manual annotation in future multilingual QNLP pipelines.

</details>


### [205] [ForeSight: Enhancing Risk Monitoring via Early Safety Signal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13737)
*Hanling Wang,Chenlong Wei,Ling Xu,Hanyan Niu,Qi Cao,Shizhou Huang,Yang Yang,Xiaohui Zhu,Yao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ForeSight 框架，仅利用首 token 隐藏状态即可高效预测最终响应的有害性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型有害内容生成是重要安全问题。现有防护多作用于输入、输出或流式生成阶段；早期风险方法依赖浅层 token 或输出 logits，初期信号弱；基于内部表征的检测器又保留大量纠缠冗余的安全无关信息。因此尚不清楚生成后最早的隐藏状态是否已包含可靠的最终有害性信号。

Method: 提出 ForeSight，首个 token 输出风险预测框架，将微弱且冗余的早期安全信号蒸馏为紧凑的、层感知的风险表征。

Result: 在五个安全基准和两个目标模型上，仅依赖首 token 隐藏状态即实现更优且高效的早期风险预测。

Conclusion: 首 token 隐藏状态已含有可用的安全性信号，经蒸馏压缩后可实现高效早期风险预警。代码已开源。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, the generation of harmful content has become a critical safety concern. Existing safeguards operate at the input, output, or streaming-generation stages, while early-risk methods that rely on surface tokens or output logits may suffer from weak initial signals, and internals-based detectors using dense representations may retain highly entangled and redundant safety-irrelevant information. It therefore remains unclear whether the earliest post-generation hidden states already contain reliable signals about final-response harmfulness. To address this gap, we propose ForeSight, a first-token output-risk forecasting framework that distills weak and redundant early safety signals into compact, layer-aware risk representations. Experiments on five safety benchmarks and two target models demonstrate that ForeSight achieves superior and efficient early-risk forecasting while relying solely on first-token hidden states. The code is available at: https://github.com/Scabbards1500/Foresight

</details>


### [206] [Inside VLM Chart Reading: Tracing Value Reading from Vertical Bar Charts Across Space and Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13745)
*Tianhao Niu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过对Qwen2.5VL-7B-Instruct与InternVL3.5-8B进行受控反事实激活替换,初步定位了视觉语言模型精确读取柱状图数值所依赖的内部计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型虽能较准确地回答图表问题,但输出准确率无法揭示它们如何组合证据以恢复精确数值,因此需要因果层面的内部机制分析。

Method: 采用受控反事实激活替换(counterfactual activation patching)研究垂直柱状图的数值读取,并结合三类分析:单因素分析、状态交接(handoff)分析与析因分析。

Result: 单因素结果显示,被改变的柱顶区域虽视觉token更少,却能比未变的柱体恢复更多答案偏好;图例与系列相关状态比柱几何与坐标轴刻度状态更早失去局部可恢复性。交接分析中,恢复作用从早期层的视觉图例区域转移到中间层的提示词系列位置,重置该提示词系列状态会选择性降低图例来源的挽救效果,支持其作为部分中介。析因分析中,两个模型均可从不同供体获取几何与刻度状态以支持组合目标;InternVL在状态来自不同供体或同一图像时表现相近,而Qwen在分离供体下恢复更低,提示其情境敏感性更高。

Conclusion: 这些结果为定位支撑精确柱值读取的内部计算提供了初步的因果证据。

Abstract: Vision--language models (VLMs) can answer chart questions accurately, but output accuracy does not show how they combine the evidence needed to recover an exact value. We study vertical-bar value reading with controlled counterfactual activation patching in Qwen2.5VL-7B-Instruct and InternVL3.5-8B. The study connects three analyses: (1) The single-factor results show that the changed bar-top region restores much more answer preference than the unchanged bar body, despite containing fewer visual tokens. Legend- and series-related states also lose local recoverability earlier than bar-geometry and axis-scale states. (2) In the handoff analysis, restoration shifts from visual legend regions in early layers to prompt-series positions in middle layers. Resetting the prompt-series state selectively reduces legend-source rescue, supporting its role as a partial mediator. (3) In the factorial analysis, both models can use geometry and scale states from separate donors to favor the combined target. InternVL performs similarly when the states come from separate donors or one image, while Qwen shows lower restoration for separate donors which suggests higher context sensitivity. Together, these results provide preliminary causal evidence for localizing the internal computations that support exact bar-value reading.

</details>


### [207] [HyperProve: Answer-Guided Hypergraph Expansion for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13768)
