*An Nguyen Phu,Dung Nguyen Quang,Luu Hieu An,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperProve，一种将问题分解与基于原子事实超图的答案条件扩展相耦合的检索增强多跳问答框架，通过将中间答案和支持超边作为检索状态来引导下一轮局部扩展，构建连贯证据链，在多跳问答基准上取得最佳总体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中，检索若仅将证据视为对原始问题的孤立匹配往往失败，因为回答复杂问题所需事实通常通过中间实体、关系和约束相互连接，需要捕捉这种结构性关联。

Method: HyperProve 将问题分解与答案条件扩展相结合，在原子事实构成的超图上进行检索；它不孤立使用原子事实、超图或迭代检索，而是把中间答案与支持超边作为检索状态，并用该状态偏置下一轮局部超图扩展，使检索过程具有状态性且以事实为中心。

Result: 在多跳问答基准评测中，HyperProve 取得最佳总体表现，相较最强基线在答案准确率上平均相对提升 6.2%，F1 相对提升 4.9%。

Conclusion: 通过将分解、答案条件扩展与超图上的状态化检索耦合，HyperProve 能为最终答案生成构建连贯的证据链，显著提升多跳问答性能。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering often fails when retrieval treats evidence as isolated matches to the original question, since the facts needed to answer a complex question are usually connected through intermediate entities, relations, and constraints. We propose HyperProve, a retrieval-augmented QA framework that addresses this challenge by coupling question decomposition with answer-conditioned expansion over a hypergraph of atomic facts. HyperProve does not use atomic facts, hypergraphs, or iterative retrieval in isolation; instead, it carries intermediate answers and supporting hyperedges as retrieval state, then uses that state to bias the next local hypergraph expansion. This design enables HyperProve to construct coherent evidence chains for final answer generation while making the retrieval process stateful and fact-centered. Across multi-hop QA benchmarks, HyperProve achieves the best overall performance in our evaluation, outperforming the strongest baselines by an average relative improvement of 6.2% in answer accuracy and 4.9% in F1.

</details>


### [208] [SyRHM: Symbolic-Language-Enhanced Reasoning with Associative Retrieval for Zero-shot Harmful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13794)
*Hanling Wang,Chenlong Wei,Yingjuan Li,Di Wu,Yuchao Zhang,Xiaohui Zhu,Yao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SyRHM 框架，将有害网络迷因（meme）检测分解为"意义接地的检索"与"符号语言增强的多阶段推理"两大部分，通过检索语义相关迷因提供接地语境，再经翻译器转为符号表示并进行规划-求解多阶段推理，在 FHM、HarM、MultiOff 数据集上多数评估设置中优于多模态与推理基线，并能输出推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害迷因的恶意意图往往隐含于图文不一致和文化刻板印象之中，现有的多模态检测器难以捕捉这类隐式语义，因此需要引入外部接地语境与可解释的推理机制来提升检测效果。

Method: SyRHM 将检测任务拆解为两个环节：(1) 意义接地的检索——将多模态内容解析为文本元素与描述，据此检索语义相关的迷因，获得超越表层相似度的接地上下文；(2) 符号语言增强的多阶段推理——先由 translator 阶段把多模态输入转换为符号化中间表示，再由 planner 与 solver 阶段执行多阶段推理，从而实现对有害意图的表达性强且可解释的分析。

Result: 在 FHM、HarM、MultiOff 三个数据集上的实验表明，SyRHM 在多数评估设置中相较现有多模态基线与基于推理的基线取得更优性能，同时能够为有害内容提供推理轨迹。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SyRHM 通过检索增强的接地上下文与符号化多阶段推理，有效缓解了图文不一致与文化刻板印象带来的隐式恶意检测难题，兼顾了检测性能与可解释性，为有害迷因识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Detecting harmful memes is critical for maintaining safe online communities. However, harmful intent is often implicit, arising from visual-textual incongruity and cultural stereotypes, which challenges existing multimodal detectors. We propose SyRHM, a framework that decomposes harmful meme detection into meaning-grounded retrieval and symbolic-language-enhanced multi-stage reasoning. SyRHM retrieves semantically related memes by parsing multimodal content into textual elements and descriptions, providing grounded context beyond surface-level similarity. Building on the retrieved context, SyRHM uses a translator stage to convert multimodal inputs into symbolic intermediate representations, and then performs multi-stage reasoning via planner and solver stages, enabling expressive and interpretable analysis of harmful intent. Experiments on FHM, HarM, and MultiOff demonstrate the effectiveness of SyRHM, achieving superior performance on most evaluation settings against multimodal and reasoning-based baselines, while providing reasoning traces for harmful content. The code is available at: https://github.com/Scabbards1500/SyRHM

</details>


### [209] [Understanding the Limits of Agentic ICD Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13806)
*Chong Yock Eng,Yushi Cao,Yiming Chen,Kezhi Mao,Hongchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在稀有性分层的MIMIC-IV出院小结上评估神经、工作流和智能体系统用于ICD-10-CM编码，发现两类正交失败模式，工具增强智能体在复杂编码子集上表现最优但无单一系统全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ICD-10-CM基准仅报告聚合指标，掩盖了系统在复杂编码场景中的表现差异，需要揭示不同系统在稀有码和复杂编码任务上的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建稀有性分层的MIMIC-IV出院小结评测集，对比神经分类器、工作流系统和智能体系统；智能体配置通过结构化访问官方ICD-10-CM参考材料进行工具增强。

Result: 神经分类器稀有码与常见码间micro-F1差距达0.43；工作流系统擅长稀有码但在需多步指南遵循的损伤与外因码上接近零分；工具增强智能体在该子集上恢复最高0.34 micro-F1。

Conclusion: 不同系统存在互补能力，无单一系统在所有条件下占优，需针对编码场景选择或组合方法。

Abstract: ICD-10-CM codes are alphanumeric codes used in the US to classify diagnoses and injuries for medical billing and epidemiological reporting. Standard ICD-10-CM benchmarks report aggregate metrics that obscure performance on complex coding scenarios. We evaluate neural, workflow, and agentic systems on a rarity-stratified set of MIMIC-IV discharge summaries and identify two orthogonal failure modes. Neural classifiers exhibit a 0.43 micro-F1 gap between rare and common codes. Workflow systems handle rare codes well but score near zero on injury and external cause codes that require multi-step guideline following. A tool-augmented agentic configuration with structured access to official ICD-10-CM reference materials recovers up to 0.34 micro-F1 on this subset. No single system dominates across all conditions.

</details>


### [210] [DARE: Dialectical Agentic Reasoning for Structured Knowledge Fact Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13808)
*Yifei Li,Xiaohan Zheng,Wentao Qian,Liansheng Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DARE（辩证智能体推理）多智能体框架，将结构化知识事实验证建模为迭代的"检索-推理-反思"过程，8B骨干模型达到88.12%准确率，可比肩或超越基于GPT-4o的程序生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于程序生成的方法虽利用大语言模型生成可执行图推理程序，但存在三大局限：无效关系生成、缺乏自我纠正的单路径推理、以及倾向高估支持信号的偏置证据评估。

Method: DARE整合三项机制：关系锚定的证据检索（将推理约束到有效结构）、辩证双向验证（从支持与反驳双视角评估证据）、置信度驱动的元反思（动态判断是否需要进一步证据探索）。

Result: 大量实验证明DARE在结构化知识事实验证上的有效性，8B骨干模型取得88.12%的准确率，达到或超过基于GPT-4o的程序生成基线。

Conclusion: 辩证智能体推理能有效激发大语言模型的潜在推理能力，在结构化知识事实验证任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Structured knowledge fact checking aims to determine the truthfulness of natural language claims by reasoning over structured evidence. Recent program-generation approaches leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate executable graph reasoning programs, achieving strong performance on structured knowledge fact checking benchmarks. However, these methods remain limited by invalid relation generation, single-path reasoning that lacks self-correction, and biased evidence assessment that tends to overestimate supporting signals. We propose Dialectical Agentic Reasoning (DARE), a multi-agent framework that formulates structured knowledge fact checking as an iterative retrieve-reason-reflect process. DARE integrates relation-grounded evidence retrieval to constrain reasoning to valid structures, dialectical bidirectional verification to evaluate evidence from both supporting and refuting perspectives, and confidence-driven meta-reflection to dynamically determine whether additional evidence exploration is necessary. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DARE in structured knowledge fact checking, with an 8B backbone achieving 88.12% accuracy and matching or surpassing GPT-4o-based program-generation baselines, which attests to the efficacy of dialectical agentic reasoning in eliciting the latent reasoning capabilities of LLMs.

</details>


### [211] [When Consistency Does Not Mean Reliability: Evaluating Local LLM Judges Against Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13824)
*Aakash Kumar Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究两个开源本地LLM评判者（LLaMA-3-8B与Qwen2.5-7B）的自我一致性与人类评分一致性之间的关系，发现高自我一致性并不代表与人类判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge被广泛用于替代人工评估，速度快、成本低。但评判者可能给出稳定一致的分数，却未必与人类评估者真正一致。作者希望探究这一潜在问题。

Method: 使用两个本地开源权重LLM评判者（LLaMA-3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B），对指令微调GPT-2（124M）针对100个问题生成的300条回答进行评分。问题涵盖事实知识、指令遵循、数学、推理、写作五类。每条回答由9位人工标注者评分，并由每个LLM评判者使用相同评分标准评估三次。采用Pearson相关、Spearman相关、平均绝对误差（MAE）、符号偏差与自我一致性进行比较。

Result: LLaMA-3-8B与人类分数的Pearson相关为0.275，Qwen2.5-7B为0.340；二者MAE分别为27.71与18.64。在一致性方面，两者自我一致性较高，完全一致率分别为97.3%（LLaMA-3-8B）与92.3%（Qwen2.5-7B）。

Conclusion: 高自我一致性并不必然意味着与人类判断高度一致。使用本地LLM作为自动评判者时，需同时评估其一致性与人类对齐程度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate the responses of other language models. This approach, known as LLM-as-a-Judge, is faster and cheaper than human evaluation. However, a judge may produce consistent scores without necessarily agreeing with human evaluators. In this work, we study this issue using two local open-weight LLM judges, LLaMA-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B. We evaluate 300 responses generated by an instruction-tuned GPT-2 (124M) model for 100 questions covering five categories: factual knowledge, instruction following, mathematics, reasoning, and writing. Each response is scored by nine human annotators and is evaluated three times by each LLM judge using the same rubric. We compare the judge scores with the average human scores using Pearson correlation, Spearman correlation, mean absolute error (MAE), signed bias, and self-consistency. LLaMA-3-8B shows a Pearson correlation of 0.275 with human scores, while Qwen2.5-7B achieves 0.340. Their MAEs are 27.71 and 18.64, respectively. Despite this limited agreement, both judges show high self-consistency, with exact consistency rates of 97.3\% for LLaMA-3-8B and 92.3\% for Qwen2.5-7B. These results show that high self-consistency does not necessarily indicate high agreement with human judgments. Our findings highlight the need to evaluate both consistency and human alignment when using local LLMs as automatic judges.

</details>


### [212] [Sweet Talkers: How Query Formulation Shapes Sycophancy in Romantic Relationship Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13841)
*Helena Choi,Edric Castel Hao,Karl Bautista,Francis Gabriel Magleo,Renzo Panti,Danielle Beatrice Olalia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了 RRASP 数据集（2400 条关于恋爱关系建议的提示），基于 ELEPHANT 框架评估 GPT-5 Mini 与 Gemini 3 Flash 的"社会谄媚"倾向，发现语法语气影响有限，而视角框架和对话轮次会显著加剧谄媚行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多被用于情感支持和关系建议，其"保全面子"的倾向可能无意中强化用户有害的人际行为，因此需要系统考察这一风险。

Method: 构建 Romantic Relationship Advice-Seeking Prompts（RRASP）数据集，包含 5 个关系主题的 2400 条提示；采用 ELEPHANT 框架，对 GPT-5 Mini 和 Gemini 3 Flash 两个消费级模型进行社会谄媚评估，并比较语法语气、视角框架翻转及多轮对话的影响。

Result: 与初始假设相反，语法语气本身并未产生系统性的谄媚差异，说明用户隐含之意比表达方式更重要；视角框架影响更大，原始提示与翻转提示之间的差距在后续回应中扩大；随对话推进，框架谄媚和道德谄媚持续上升；Gemini 3 Flash 的道德谄媚增幅明显小于 GPT-5 Mini。

Conclusion: 模型在对话推进中更易接受用户既定前提并认同其道德立场；Gemini 3 Flash 相比 GPT-5 Mini 更能抵抗在多轮对话中强化伦理上存在问题的立场。该研究提示情感支持类应用中需关注多轮交互下的谄媚风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for emotional support and relationship advice, where a model's tendency to preserve a user's face can inadvertently reinforce harmful interpersonal behaviors. To systematically examine this risk, we developed the Romantic Relationship Advice-Seeking Prompts (RRASP) dataset of 2,400 prompts across five relationship themes and evaluated social sycophancy using the ELEPHANT framework on two consumer-facing models, GPT-5 Mini and Gemini 3 Flash. Contrary to our initial hypothesis, grammatical mood alone did not produce systematic differences in sycophantic behavior, suggesting that what a user implies matters more than how they phrase it. Instead, perspective-driven framing had a stronger influence, with gaps between original and flipped prompts widening in follow-up responses. Consistent increases in framing and moral sycophancy across turns indicate that models become more likely to accept a user's stated premises and affirm their ethical stance as a dialogue progresses. Notably, Gemini 3 Flash exhibited substantially smaller increases in moral sycophancy than GPT-5 Mini, suggesting it is more resistant to reinforcing ethically problematic positions across turns.

</details>


### [213] [Measuring the Cost of Variety Conflation in Multilingual MT Evaluation: Adding Mozambican Xichangana, Nyanja and Sena to FLORES+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13847)
*Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Delfina Lázaro Mateus,Manuel Valente Mangue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将FLORES+扩展至三种莫桑比克班图语变体（Xichangana、Mozambican Nyanja、Sena），评估多系统翻译质量，发现参考译文对评测结果影响巨大，变体感知微调可有效提升目标变体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨边界语言或方言/变体在现有评测基准中缺乏对应参考译文，直接使用相近语言（如Tsonga、Chichewa）的参考会掩盖真实性能差异，因此需要为莫桑比克本地变体构建专门的评测集并研究参考敏感性。

Method: 为Xichangana、Mozambican Nyanja和Sena三种莫桑比克班图语变体扩展FLORES+葡萄牙语源评测集；将Xichangana与现有Tsonga参考对比、Mozambican Nyanja与Chichewa对比；在系统输出固定的条件下评估NLLB-200、Google Translate、GPT以及一个变体感知的NLLB模型，并进行变体感知微调实验。

Result: 在devtest上，仅将参考从Tsonga换成Xichangana，NLLB-200的spBLEU下降13.10分，Google下降15.30分；在匹配的Nyanja子集上，将Chichewa换成Mozambican Nyanja分别带来3.03和6.10 spBLEU的小幅但一致的下降。变体感知微调在目标变体上逆转该趋势：相对NLLB-200，Xichangana提升7.04 spBLEU，Mozambican Nyanja提升5.33；但在同族参考上性能下降。GPT在Tsonga和Chichewa上具竞争力，但在莫桑比克变体上明显较弱。Sena上微调模型在devtest达到12.64 spBLEU和36.21 chrF++。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调对于跨边界语言或方言/变体，需要采用变体感知的语言标识符、参考译文和报告方式；相关数据已公开于Hugging Face。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend FLORES+ with Portuguese-source evaluation sets for three Mozambican Bantu varieties: Xichangana, Mozambican Nyanja, and Sena. We compare Xichangana with the existing Tsonga reference and Mozambican Nyanja with Chichewa, and evaluate NLLB-200, Google Translate, GPT, and a variant-aware NLLB model. Holding system output fixed reveals substantial reference sensitivity. On \textit{devtest}, changing only the reference from Tsonga to Xichangana reduces spBLEU by 13.10 points for NLLB-200 and 15.30 for Google. On matched Nyanja subsets, replacing Chichewa with Mozambican Nyanja produces smaller but consistent reductions of 3.03 and 6.10 spBLEU, respectively. Variant-aware fine-tuning reverses this pattern on the intended targets: relative to NLLB-200, it improves Xichangana by 7.04 spBLEU and Mozambican Nyanja by 5.33 on \textit{devtest}, while losing performance on the sibling references. GPT is competitive on Tsonga and Chichewa but substantially weaker on the Mozambican varieties. For Sena, the finetuned model reaches 12.64 spBLEU and 36.21 chrF++ on \textit{devtest}. These findings motivate variety-aware language identifiers, references, and reporting for cross-border languages or language dialects/variants. The data is publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/MOZNLP/FLORES_MOZ

</details>


### [214] [ShopEase: A Generative AI-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Intelligent Enterprise Customer Support Using Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13856)
*Aakash Kumar Tiwari,Somesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ShopEase，一个面向企业客户支持的多智能体生成式AI框架，结合意图识别、CRM、记忆、混合RAG、升级与监督六大组件，对比六种检索配置，发现纯稠密检索（FAISS）精度最高（85.37%），交叉编码器重排序无增益且增加延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客户支持系统需正确回答客户问题、检索正确政策信息、利用客户上下文，并在必要时将疑难案例转交人工。作者希望构建一个综合性的多智能体框架来满足这些需求，并探究混合检索与重排序策略在其中的实际效果。

Method: 系统包含Intent、CRM、Memory、Hybrid RAG、Escalation、Supervisor六个组件，使用本地Ollama运行的LLaMA 3.2生成回复。检索模块结合FAISS（稠密）与BM25（稀疏），评估六种配置：BM25-only、FAISS-only、Fair RRF、Weighted RRF、RRF+Cross-Encoder、Top-10 Hybrid+Cross-Encoder。政策类别直接从检索文档中判定，而非固定意图—政策映射。

Result: 在2632条留出客户查询（Refund、Return、Shipping、Cancellation、Damaged Product、Unknown六类）上评估。FAISS-only精度最高，达85.37%（2247条正确），Weighted RRF次之85.07%；BM25-only仅55.74%。加入交叉编码器重排序未提升效果（RRF+Cross-Encoder 83.24%，Top-10 Hybrid+Cross-Encoder 81.88%），且增加响应延迟。类别层面，Shipping、Cancellation、Return表现较强，Unknown是主要错误来源。McNemar检验显示FAISS-only与Weighted RRF无显著差异，但均显著优于Fair RRF和交叉编码器配置。

Conclusion: 在该数据集上，稠密检索取得最佳精度，额外的重排序只增加处理时间而不提升分类性能，因此推荐采用FAISS-only或Weighted RRF的简洁检索方案。

Abstract: Enterprise customer support systems must answer customer questions correctly, retrieve the right policy information, use customer context, and pass difficult cases to human agents when needed. This paper presents ShopEase, a Generative AI-based multi-agent framework for enterprise customer support. The system combines six components: Intent, CRM, Memory, Hybrid RAG, Escalation, and Supervisor, and uses LLaMA 3.2 running locally through Ollama for response generation. The retrieval module combines FAISS (dense retrieval) and BM25 (sparse retrieval), and six configurations are evaluated: BM25-only, FAISS-only, Fair RRF, Weighted RRF, RRF with Cross-Encoder, and Top-10 Hybrid with Cross-Encoder. Instead of using a fixed mapping between intent and policy, the policy category is decided directly from the retrieved documents. The system was evaluated on 2632 held-out customer queries across six categories: Refund, Return, Shipping, Cancellation, Damaged Product, and Unknown. FAISS-only achieved the highest accuracy of 85.37\% (2247 correct predictions), closely followed by Weighted RRF at 85.07\%. BM25-only achieved only 55.74\% accuracy. Adding cross-encoder reranking did not improve results: RRF with Cross-Encoder reached 83.24\%, and Top-10 Hybrid with Cross-Encoder reached 81.88\%, while also increasing response latency. Category-level analysis shows strong performance on Shipping, Cancellation, and Return, while Unknown queries remain the main source of errors. Statistical testing using McNemar's test shows no significant difference between FAISS-only and Weighted RRF, though both perform significantly better than Fair RRF and the cross-encoder configurations. Overall, dense retrieval gives the best accuracy on this dataset, and additional reranking adds processing time without improving classification performance.

</details>


### [215] [Bangla Sentence Function Classification: Corpus Development, Model Benchmarking, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13869)
*Swapnil Kundu Argha,Abdullah Al Shafi,Rowzatul Zannat,Shoumik Barman Polok,Abdul Muntakim,Jannatul Ferdousi,M. A. Moyeen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了1万条孟加拉语句子功能分类语料库（四类：陈述、疑问、祈使、感叹），标注一致性Kappa为0.82，并对比多种特征表示与分类器及异构集成模型，发现TF-IDF优于Word2Vec，DLE+TF-IDF达到0.95的准确率与宏F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句子功能识别对对话系统、语音合成、机器翻译等NLP下游任务很重要，但孟加拉语的句子功能分类基准资源仍然稀缺，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建1万条人工标注的孟加拉语句子语料库，覆盖陈述、疑问、祈使、感叹四类，类别近乎均衡。评估BoW、TF-IDF、Word2Vec等特征表示，配合多种经典机器学习分类器，并引入单层集成（SLE）与双层集成（DLE）两种异构集成模型；同时使用交叉验证与LIME进行稳健性检验和可解释性分析。

Result: TF-IDF表现稳定优于Word2Vec，原因可能是其能突出与句子功能相关的判别性词汇线索，而Word2Vec受限于语料规模较小。DLE结合TF-IDF取得最佳性能，准确率和宏F1均为0.95，交叉验证确认方法稳健性，LIME提供模型预测的可解释洞察。

Conclusion: 该研究提供的语料库与模型基准为孟加拉语句子功能分类建立了强有力的基线，验证了稀疏词汇表示与异构集成学习在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic sentence function identification is important for many downstream natural language processing (NLP) applications such as dialogue systems, text-to-speech synthesis, and machine translation. However, benchmark resources for Bangla sentence function classification remain limited. To mitigate this gap, this paper introduces a corpus of 10,000 Bangla sentences, manually annotated into four functional categories, namely declarative, interrogative, imperative, and exclamatory. The corpus is nearly balanced across the four classes, with high annotation reliability reflected by a Fleiss\' Kappa of 0.82. Furthermore, we evaluate multiple feature representations, including Bag-of-Words (BoW), TF-IDF, and Word2Vec, with several classical machine learning classifiers. In addition, two heterogeneous ensemble models, namely Single-Level Ensemble (SLE) and Double-Level Ensemble (DLE), are utilized to improve classification performance. Experimental results show that TF-IDF consistently outperforms Word2Vec, likely due to its ability to emphasize discriminative lexical cues associated with sentence functions, particularly given the relatively small corpus used to train Word2Vec. The DLE model with TF-IDF features achieves the best performance with accuracy and macro-F1 of 0.95, demonstrating the effectiveness of sparse lexical representations and heterogeneous ensemble learning for this task. Further cross-validation confirms the robustness of the approach, while LIME-based interpretability provides insights into model predictions. The developed corpus and model benchmarking establish strong baselines for Bangla sentence function classification.

</details>


### [216] [SHIFT-M3: Pre-fusion Alignment-based Consistency Screening for Multimodal ECG Record Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13874)
*Md Ashik Khan,Md Nahid Siddique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出轻量级文本预融合筛查模型 SHIFT-M3，用于检测多模态临床记录中跨患者错配，在 MEETI 心电数据集上取得高检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床 AI 通常假设同一记录内的波形、报告、元数据与下游预测属于同一患者，但实际中数据关联失败可能静默拼装出跨患者的"看似合理"记录，构成安全隐患，而标准预测模型无法检测此类问题。

Method: 将该问题定义为多模态记录完整性分诊：给定组装记录，判断各模态是否可信地属于同一患者。提出 SHIFT-M3，一种仅含 573,569 参数的轻量文本预融合筛查器，通过比较 ECG 的两种文本视图（LLM 生成解释与临床报告摘要）的一致性来度量对齐程度。另引入 CMST 冲突类型多模态压力测试评估分类体系，并进行三种子稳定性研究、损失消融、时间容忍度扫描及共享 token 掩码对照实验。

Result: 在 784,680 条 MEETI ECG 记录上，SHIFT-M3 对完全文本视图交换达到 97.6% TPR@5% FPR（AUROC 0.996），部分交换 90.3%（AUROC 0.974），标签匹配困难负样本 97.7%（AUROC 0.996）。相比同数据集词汇基线，在部分交换与困难负样本上提升最大，说明模型学到的不只是表层重叠。主要遗留失败模式是纵向歧义：默认工作点下，同患者跨就诊配对仍产生 87.0% 的 II 类假阳性。

Conclusion: SHIFT-M3 以极低参数量有效筛查多模态记录完整性问题，尤其擅长部分交换与困难负样本，但同患者跨就诊的纵向歧义仍是主要失效场景，需进一步解决。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical AI typically assumes that the waveform, report, metadata, and downstream predictions attached to a record belong to the same patient. In practice, linkage failures can silently assemble individually plausible but cross-patient components, creating a safety problem that standard predictive models are not designed to detect. We study this problem as multimodal record integrity triage: given an assembled record, should its modalities be trusted to belong together? We introduce SHIFT-M3, a lightweight text-based pre-fusion screen that measures alignment-based consistency between two separately produced ECG text views: an LLM-generated interpretation and a clinical report summary. On 784,680 MEETI ECG records, SHIFT-M3 achieves 97.6% TPR@5% FPR for full text-view swaps (AUROC 0.996), 90.3% for partial swaps (AUROC 0.974), and 97.7% for label-matched hard negatives (AUROC 0.996) with only 573,569 parameters. Compared with same-dataset lexical baselines, the gains are largest on partial swaps and hard negatives, suggesting that the model is learning more than surface overlap. We also introduce the CMST (Conflict-type Multimodal Stress Test) evaluation taxonomy, a three-seed stability study, a loss ablation, a temporal-tolerance sweep, and a shared-token masking control. The main remaining failure mode is longitudinal ambiguity: at the default operating point, same-patient cross-visit pairs still produce 87.0% Type-II false positives.

</details>


### [217] [Phorecaster365: A Human-Supervised Reference Architecture for Hybrid Pharmaceutical Sales Forecasting and Planning Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13907)
*Houman Kazemzadeh,Kamyar Naderi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Phorecaster365——一个人工监督的药品销售预测参考架构，将ERP数据与可审核预测连接，并给出验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药品销售预测依赖库存、交易语义、产品生命周期及预测发布时可用信息等条件，单一模型预测无法保留这些条件，也无法判断预测是否适合运营使用。

Method: 构建人类监督的参考架构，分离数据接入、产品-区域序列构建、时间有效特征生成、统计与机器学习建模、集成、不确定性评估、规划者审核及生命周期治理；以"预测上下文包"和"预测证据包"为核心中间表示；基于30个产品-区域序列、10,950条日度记录的合成面板进行开发，并规定滚动起始评估协议、基线与消融要求。

Result: 开发经验来自合成数据面板；历史实验摘要仅作为开发性描述证据，其评估不能确立独立预测效度。本文未确立真实世界预测准确性、比较优越性或运营收益。

Conclusion: 贡献为一项实现中立的系统设计与验证框架，并提出从合成测试到受治理规划应用的阶段性路径。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical sales forecasts inform planning across products, regions, and distribution channels, yet their interpretation depends on inventory availability, transaction semantics, product lifecycle, and the information available when each forecast is issued. A model prediction alone does not preserve these conditions or establish whether a forecast is suitable for operational use. We present Phorecaster365, a human-supervised reference architecture that connects enterprise resource planning data to reviewable pharmaceutical sales forecasts. The architecture separates source ingestion, product-region series construction, temporally valid feature generation, statistical and machine-learning modeling, ensemble formation, uncertainty assessment, planner review, and lifecycle governance. Its central intermediate representation is a forecast context package that preserves the source snapshot, forecast target, temporal cutoff, available covariates, data-quality state, and hierarchy version. A corresponding forecast evidence package links predictions to model and calibration versions, exceptions, human adjustments, and publication history. Development experience with a synthetic panel of 10,950 daily records across 30 product-region series informs the design. Historical experiment summaries are retained only as descriptive evidence of development because their evaluation does not establish independent predictive validity. We specify a rolling-origin evaluation protocol, baseline and ablation requirements, uncertainty and robustness assessments, and a staged pathway from synthetic testing to use in governed planning. The contribution is an implementation-neutral system design and validation framework; the report does not establish real-world forecasting accuracy, comparative superiority, or operational benefit.

</details>


### [218] [Inter-Rater Reliability of LLM and Rule-Based Annotation for Inferential Narrative Features: Three Studies on a Turkish Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13936)
*Levent Bulut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该报告检验了自动生成的叙事特征标注是否与人类判断一致，发现"具象隐喻"规则下五个机器标注者与人类几乎无一致性（κ接近0），但无法判定原因是特征本身难以自动检测还是规则定义不够可操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成的标注数据集很少被追问"人类是否会同意这些标签"，本文针对Objective Projection土耳其叙事语料库，检验其基于规则的检测器对六项写作特征（两项禁令、四项正向技巧）的标注是否与人类评判一致，尤其关注与理论核心最接近的"具象隐喻"规则。

Method: 开展三项研究：研究1（n=120）将检测器与方案作者本人的盲标注对比；研究2（n=100，不相交场景集）将检测器及Gemini 2.5 Flash、Grok与一位标签在任何机器运行前即锁定的独立非专家评分者对比；研究2b以Claude Fable 5（High）和ChatGPT 5.5重复同一协议。

Result: 在"具象隐喻"上，五个机器标注者在100个场景中的正例数分别为0、1、40、72、78，而人类为9。六个标注者中有五个的Cohen's κ接近或等同随机水平（0.004、0.015、0.000、0.019、0.027），覆盖两个人类参照和两个场景集。原始一致率为74.7%至84.5%，是类别不平衡造成的假象而非能力体现。

Conclusion: 作者刻意不将其归结为单一结论。两种解读均成立：该特征确实是推理性的、超出当前自动检测能力；或规则定义尚不够可操作，以致任何评分者（包括人类）都无法一致应用。区分二者需要第二位独立人类评分者，本报告不具备该条件，因此不作此声称。

Abstract: Datasets that ship automatically generated feature annotations invite a question rarely asked of them: would a human agree with those labels? This report answers that for the Objective Projection corpus, a Turkish narrative dataset whose scenes carry a per-scene applied_rules field from a rule-based detector over six craft features -- two prohibitions (emotion labelling, simile) and four positive techniques (materialized metaphor, micro-focus, temporal anchor, atmosphere contradiction).
  Three studies are reported. Study 1 ($n = 120$) scores the detector against blind labels from the scheme's own author. Study 2 ($n = 100$, a disjoint scene set) scores the detector plus Gemini 2.5 Flash and Grok against an independent non-expert rater whose labels were locked before any machine ran. Study 2b re-runs the identical protocol with Claude Fable 5 (High) and ChatGPT 5.5.
  The central result concerns one rule. On materialized metaphor -- closest to the methodology's theoretical core -- the five machine labellers returned positive rates of $0$, $1$, $40$, $72$ and $78$ out of $100$ scenes, against a human count of $9$. Cohen's $κ$ was at or indistinguishable from chance for five of six labellers, across both human references and both scene sets: $0.004$, $0.015$, $0.000$, $0.019$, $0.027$. Raw agreement ranged from $74.7\%$ to $84.5\%$, an artefact of class imbalance rather than a sign of competence.
  We deliberately do not resolve this into a single story. Two readings survive: the feature is genuinely inferential and beyond current automatic detection, or the rule's definition is not yet operational enough for any rater to apply consistently -- including the human. Distinguishing them needs a second independent human rater, which this report does not have and therefore does not claim.

</details>


### [219] [CRITICS - Critical Science Without Borders: Language Models to Promote Critical Thinking in Science Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13942)
*Rodrigo Agerri,Itziar Aldabe,Elena Cabrio,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu,Mariana Flores,Jurgita Kapociute-Dzikiene,Dovile Kuiziniene,Arantza Rico,Aritz Ruiz-Gonzalez,Aitor Soroa,Mantas Vaskevicius,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRITICS项目将基于大语言模型优化的科学领域机器翻译与教育技术结合，提升科学内容可及性与科学素养，促进科学知识民主化与批判性思维培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿科研与教育资料多仅以高资源语言呈现，造成语言壁垒，阻碍学生获取和理解科学知识，也影响科学教育公平与科学素养培养。

Method: 针对科学内容优化机器翻译系统，提供准确且文化相关的母语译文；在此基础上设计与评估与课程对齐的科学教学方案，研究科学论证、批判性思维实践，以及基于能力评估框架、对齐学习目标与评价标准的文本反馈。

Result: 摘要为项目规划性描述，未给出具体实验结果或量化数据，主要提出项目目标、研究路径与关键研究组成部分。

Conclusion: CRITICS有望打破语言障碍，使更多学生接触前沿研究与教育资源，推动科学知识民主化，并在科学教育中促进批判性思维发展。

Abstract: The CRITICS project addresses science accessibility and literacy by converging advanced Machine Translation (MT) based on Large Language Models (LLMs) with educational technology. By leveraging MT systems specifically optimized for scientific content, educational institutions can provide accurate, culturally relevant translations of scientific materials in students' native languages, ensuring that complex scientific concepts are comprehensible while maintaining technical accuracy. Building on these translations, the project explores the design and evaluation of innovative science teaching-learning proposals grounded in curriculum-aligned teaching-learning. Thus, CRITICS will investigate key components of scientific argumentation and critical thinking practices together with textual feedback aligned with learning objectives and assessment criteria inspired by competence-based evaluation frameworks. CRITICS aims to break down language barriers to accessing cutting-edge research and educational materials currently available only in high-resourced languages, thereby facilitating the democratization of scientific knowledge and fostering critical thinking in science education.

</details>


### [220] [Thought without systematicity? Evaluating reasoning models on rule induction tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13948)
*Simon Schug,Brenden M. Lake*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了推理模型是否具备系统性思维，通过规则归纳任务及其结构等价变体测试发现，模型即使能解决某一任务，也常在结构等价的变体上失败，表明其行为缺乏系统性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知的核心原则之一是系统性——理解一个概念应与理解其相近变体相关联。作者希望检验推理模型是否也稳健地表现出这种系统性，从而判断能否可靠地确立其认知能力。

Method: 将认知科学中已建立的规则归纳任务扩展到当前推理模型评估中，利用任务家族的组合结构，通过重组、替换等任务同构方式构造结构等价的变体任务，并比较模型在原任务与等价变体上的表现。

Result: 模型尽管能正确解决某一任务，但在该任务的结构等价变体上经常失败，表现出不一致的系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: 许多模型行为缺乏系统性，因此难以在这些评估的具体情境之外稳健地确立推理模型的认知能力。

Abstract: A central tenet of human cognition is systematicity, the principle that understanding one concept is inherently tied to understanding close variations of that concept. Do reasoning models robustly exhibit such systematicity? If so, we would expect consistent performance on structurally equivalent variants of the same task. Here, we extend established rule induction tasks from cognitive science to assess the systematicity of thought in current reasoning models. Each task family has compositional structure that we use to create structurally equivalent task variations through task isomorphisms such as recombination and substitution. We find that despite being able to correctly solve a task, models often fail on structurally equivalent variants of the same task. These findings suggest that many model behaviors lack systematicity, rendering it difficult to robustly establish the cognitive abilities of reasoning models beyond the particular contexts they were evaluated in.

</details>


### [221] [Mizan: A National Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on Iraqi Arabic and the Iraqi Civic Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13980)
*Nawar S. Alseelawi,Mustafa S. Aljumaily*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Mizan——伊拉克国家级的伊拉克阿拉伯语与国家语境LLM评测基准，包含MSA基线与伊拉克方言两条赛道、六大方轴、340道原创双审题目，并对27个系统做了试点评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语LLM评测已围绕现代标准阿拉伯语(MSA)成熟，OALL、HELM Arabic、BALSAM等榜单趋于饱和；而伊拉克人日常所说的方言阿拉伯语几乎被现有评测基础设施忽略。因此需要一个面向伊拉克阿拉伯语及伊拉克公共语境的评测基准。

Method: 构建Mizan基准：MSA基线赛道+伊拉克赛道，覆盖六轴（方言理解、方言生成、MSA-伊拉克语双向翻译、伊拉克专属知识、官方文档字段抽取、安全）；340道原创、双人复核题目；对答案位置做统计审计，并给出每个公开分数的Wilson置信区间。试点评测27个系统，含发布后三天内的闭源前沿模型、各规模档开源权重模型，以及商用、开源专用、主权三类阿拉伯语系统。平台实施不可变快照、验证证书、人工发布门与公开撤回的完整性协议。

Result: MSA赛道饱和而伊拉克赛道具备区分度，各模型稳定存在14-18分差距，领先者统计上并列；官方文档抽取将各系统限制在32-56分；阿拉伯语专用化实为MSA专用化——两个专用阿拉伯语模型在伊拉克赛道上低于同规模通用模型；最新模型家族的安全加固档会把无害的方言理解题判定为违规而确定性拒答，属MSA基准不可见的过度拒答模式。

Conclusion: 方言阿拉伯语评测揭示MSA基准无法发现的模型能力差距与失效模式，Mizan提供了可审计、可撤回的基准基础设施；代码与公开开发集随论文发布。

Abstract: Arabic large-language-model (LLM) evaluation has matured around Modern Standard Arabic (MSA): aggregated leaderboards such as the Open Arabic LLM Leaderboard (OALL), HELM Arabic, and BALSAM rank models across dozens of MSA tasks, and frontier systems increasingly saturate them. Dialectal Arabic, the language Iraqis actually speak, remains nearly invisible to this infrastructure. We introduce Mizan ("the balance"), Iraq's national benchmark for evaluating LLMs on Iraqi Arabic and the Iraqi civic context: an MSA baseline track paired with an Iraqi track across six axes (dialect comprehension, dialect generation, bidirectional MSA-Iraqi translation, Iraq-specific knowledge, official-document field extraction, and safety), built from 340 originally authored, dually reviewed items with statistically audited answer positions and Wilson intervals on every published score. A pilot evaluation of 27 systems, spanning closed frontier models three days after release, open weights across size tiers, and an Arabic trio of commercial, open-specialized, and sovereign systems, yields four findings. The MSA track saturates while the Iraqi track discriminates, with a consistent 14-18-point per-model gap and statistically tied leaders. Official-document extraction confines every system to 32-56. Arabic-focused specialization behaves as MSA specialization: two dedicated Arabic models score below a size-matched generalist on the Iraqi track. And the safety-hardened tier of the newest model family deterministically refuses innocuous dialect-comprehension items as policy violations, an over-refusal mode invisible to MSA benchmarks. The platform enforces an integrity protocol of immutable snapshots, verification certificates, a human publication gate, and public retraction, all exercised during this study. Code and the public development set accompany the paper.

</details>


### [222] [Unlocking the Unsolvable: Teacher-Guided Curriculum for Data-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13997)
*Yukang Zhu,Zhen Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过教师引导的课程学习"解锁"模型当前无法解决（rollout全部失败）的问题，用反向链式课程逐步撤去提示，仅用128个不可解问题即达到甚至超过用2000题全量数据训练GRPO的效果（约16倍数据效率），并提出单调前沿课程（MFC）缓解分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽显著提升大模型数学推理能力，但超出模型当前能力的问题（rollout全部失败、无学习信号）会被结构性浪费，而这类问题恰恰是最具信息量的训练前沿。

Method: 利用更强模型的"部分推理轨迹"作为教师引导，构建分级难度景观；采用反向链式课程，逐步撤销引导，直至模型能独立解题；针对不可解问题独有的分布偏移代价，提出单调前沿课程MFC，单调地推动训练走向无引导求解。

Result: 仅用128个不可解问题训练，在九个基准平均上达到或超过用完整2000题语料训练的GRPO（约16倍数据效率，两个基座模型均如此），同时通过大k下的pass@k显著扩展推理边界；MFC在现有课程方法中持续表现最优。

Conclusion: 不可解问题可通过教师引导的课程学习被有效利用，带来显著数据效率与推理边界提升；MFC能有效应对分布偏移代价，是更优的课程训练方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has shown remarkable success in improving the mathematical reasoning of large language models. Yet problems beyond the model's current capability, where rollouts uniformly fail and no learning signal is produced, are structurally wasted despite marking the most informative training frontier. We show that these otherwise-inert problems can be unlocked via teacher-guided curriculum learning: partial reasoning traces from a stronger model create a graded difficulty landscape, and a backward-chaining curriculum progressively withdraws guidance until the model solves problems unaided. Training on only 128 unsolvable problems matches or exceeds GRPO trained on a full 2,000-problem corpus (~16x data efficiency) on the nine-benchmark average for both base models, while substantially expanding the reasoning boundary measured by pass@k at large k. Furthermore, we identify a distribution-shift cost that is particularly acute in the unsolvable-only regime and propose Monotone Frontier Curriculum (MFC), a method that monotonically drives training toward unguided solving, consistently outperforming existing curriculum methods.

</details>


### [223] [Measuring the Creativity of Frontier LLMs in Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14057)
*Yiheng Zhao,Mengzhuo Chen,Chengming Hu,Pengyi Liao,Yiran Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套从"价值性"和"新颖性"两个维度评估前沿大模型自动化研究创造力的指标体系，并发现变量级P-Novelty是最能反映研究空间探索广度且与研究表现最相关的创造力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型已具备开展自动化研究的能力，但其在该场景下的创造力尚未被系统性评估，缺乏针对性的评测指标。

Method: 提出面向价值性与新颖性两维度的创造力评测指标：价值性衡量想法是否有用；新颖性从三个视角评估，包括Exact-Match P-Novelty（是否曾出现过相同想法）、Variable-level P-Novelty（是否探索了此前未被研究的变量或变量组合）、H-Novelty（想法是直接沿用检索到的外部知识还是偏离它）。

Result: 各模型在多数创造力指标上得分相近，但在反映研究空间探索广度的Variable-level P-Novelty上差异显著；进一步的相关性与想法级表现分析表明，Variable-level P-Novelty是与研究表现关联最强的创造力维度。

Conclusion: 该指标体系可用于系统评估自动化研究的创造力，其中Variable-level P-Novelty可作为衡量研究表现的关键创造力指标。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly capable of conducting automated research, yet their creativity in this setting has not been systematically evaluated. In this paper, we propose a set of metrics to evaluate creativity along the two dimensions of valueness and novelty. Valueness assesses whether each proposed idea is useful, while novelty is evaluated from three perspectives: whether the same idea has appeared before (Exact-Match P-Novelty), whether a previously unexplored variable or variable combination is explored (Variable-level P-Novelty), and whether the idea directly follows retrieved external knowledge or departs from it (H-Novelty). Our evaluation shows that the models achieve relatively similar scores on most creativity metrics, but differ substantially in Variable-level P-Novelty, which reflects the breadth of research-space exploration. Further correlation and idea-level performance analyses show that Variable-level P-Novelty is the creativity dimension most strongly associated with research performance.

</details>


### [224] [GraMRAG: Orchestrating Multi-Agent Multi-Step Reasoning via Graph Memory with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14066)
*Zhongyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GraMRAG，一个图记忆引导的多智能体RAG框架，通过动态多模态记忆图和视觉-文本桥接推理范式提升长程跨模态推理能力，并结合拓扑感知策略优化（TAPO）实现细粒度信用分配，在挑战性多模态基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体RAG系统在复杂多模态推理任务上虽有前景，但在推理深度和记忆结构上存在根本局限，回答知识密集型问题时会出现检索不充分和"状态盲区"（state blindness）问题。

Method: 1) 视觉-文本桥接推理范式：统一多尺度实体裁剪与ReAct风格视觉工具链；2) 多模态记忆图：将智能体推理形式化为动态有向无环图（DAG），显式建模动作-观察依赖关系，缓解状态盲区并抑制冗余检索；3) 拓扑感知策略优化（TAPO）：利用图拓扑进行关键路径识别与目标节点剪枝，实现多步推理轨迹的细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在具有挑战性的多模态基准上，该方法持续优于现有基线，并在复杂长程推理任务上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过融合图记忆结构与多智能体RAG、并结合拓扑感知的策略优化，可有效提升多模态长程推理的稳定性与深度，为知识密集型多模态问答提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Although existing multi-agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems have demonstrated promise on complex multimodal reasoning tasks, they remain fundamentally limited in reasoning depth and memory structure, suffering from inadequate retrieval and state blindness when answering knowledge-intensive questions. To address these limitations, we propose GraMRAG, a graph memory-guided multi-agent RAG framework that integrates a dynamic multimodal memory graph to enable stable, multi-step multimodal reasoning. We introduce a vision-text bridged reasoning paradigm that unifies multi-scale entity cropping with a ReAct-style visual toolchain, enhancing the long-horizon cross-modal reasoning capability. We further construct a multimodal memory graph that formalizes agent reasoning as a dynamic directed acyclic graph (DAG), explicitly modeling action-observation dependencies to mitigate state blindness and suppress redundant retrieval. Moreover, we propose Topology-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO) that leverages graph topology for critical path identification and targeted node pruning, enabling fine-grained credit assignment across multi-step reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments on challenging multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on complex long-horizon reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [225] [One Size Does Not Fit All: Setting Inference Depth from the Questions a Deployment Actually Asks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14144)
*Jerry Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在已知部署流量分布时，冻结语言模型通过"提前退出"能节省多少推理成本；发现节省幅度高度依赖任务类型，按部署定制退出阈值收益最大，且token级保真度这一主流评价指标在可验证答案的领域会严重高估实际正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个通用transformer语言模型被训练来响应任意提示，但每次部署实际只面对很窄的提示范围（客服助手只遇到配送投诉、编程工具只遇到Python）。然而每次部署都为每个token支付相同的计算成本。论文希望量化：当提示范围事先已知时，有多少成本是可以避免的。目标场景是个人设备上的小模型，其生成瓶颈是内存带宽而非计算。

Method: 考察的机制是提前退出（early exit）：在中间层附加一个小的、经过训练的成分（称为readout），它提出一个token，再由置信度测试决定是直接输出还是继续运行剩余层。模型保持冻结，唯一使用的监督信号是模型自身在普通流量上的输出。论文比较了三种利用部署流量知识的方式，并考察了不同语料下的退出率与评价指标。

Result: 三项发现：第一，可实现的节省强烈依赖流量类型——在15亿参数模型的半深度处，算术应用题有96%的token可提前输出，而中文解释仅8%（在与完整模型token级保真度匹配的条件下）。第二，在三种利用流量知识的方式中，只有定制退出阈值是值得的：按部署校准阈值使退出率最多提升59个百分点（跨三个模型），在多数测试语料上提升超过10个百分点。第三，token级保真度——提前退出文献的标准评价指标——在可用真值检验的领域会失效：在算术应用题上，三个模型完整运行时各答对60题，而在保真度得分最高的提前退出配置下只答对10到28题。

Conclusion: 提前退出的可节省程度取决于部署流量类型，定制阈值是唯一有效的流量利用方式，而token级保真度作为评价指标存在根本缺陷，会高估提前退出的实际任务正确率。研究面向内存带宽受限的个人设备小模型场景。

Abstract: A transformer language model is trained to respond to any prompt, but each deployment asks only a narrow range of questions: a support assistant sees delivery complaints, a coding tool sees Python. Every deployment nonetheless pays the same computation per token. This paper measures how much of that cost is avoidable when the range of prompts is known in advance.
  The mechanism examined is early exit: a small, trained component - called a readout - is attached to an intermediate layer and proposes a token, and a confidence test decides whether to emit it or to run the remaining layers. The models are frozen, and the only supervision used is the model's own output on ordinary traffic.
  Three findings are reported. First, achievable savings depend strongly on the kind of traffic: at half depth on a 1.5-billion-parameter model, 96 percent of tokens could be emitted early for arithmetic word problems and 8 percent for Chinese-language explanations, at matched token-level fidelity to the full model (a measure whose limits the third finding exposes). Second, of three ways a deployment might use knowledge of its traffic, only customizing the threshold for exiting early is worthwhile: calibrating it per deployment raised exit rates by up to 59 percentage points across three models, and by more than 10 points on most corpora tested. Third, token-level fidelity - the standard evaluation measure in the early-exit literature - fails in domains where tokens can be checked against ground truth: on arithmetic word problems, three models each answered sixty questions correctly when run in full, and between 10 and 28 correctly under early exit, in the configuration that scored highest on fidelity.
  The intended setting is small models on personal devices, where generation is limited by memory bandwidth rather than computation.

</details>


### [226] [When Tools Get in the Way: The Effect of Unnecessary Tool Availability on LLM Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14157)
*Saanvi Paturi,Arsen Kenzhebayev,Arham Sethi,Vyas Raina,Ivaxi Sheth,Vatsal Raina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究多余工具（无关但相关的工具）的存在是否会损害大语言模型依赖自身知识作答的能力，以及先前的工具交互是否会改变这一行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地与外部工具结合部署。以往研究多关注模型是否能恰当选择和使用工具，而"不必要的工具是否会改变答案正确性"这一问题受到的关注较少。作者希望探究：提供一个相关但不必要的工具，是否会削弱模型凭自身知识回答问题的能力，以及此前的工具交互是否会进一步影响该行为。

Method: 构建了跨10个知识领域的500个查询对，每对包含一个需要该领域工具的"工具查询"和一个不需要工具的"封闭域查询"。对6个LLM在三种条件下进行评估：工具不可用、工具可用、以及先前调用过工具后工具可用。

Result: 在3,000次基线试验中，汇总回答率为98.2%。当不必要的工具可用时，回答率降至63.5%，且模型之间差异很大。即使工具很少被调用，回答率也会下降，因此不能仅用"不必要地调用工具"来解释。一句范围感知的系统指令即可恢复大部分丢失的回答。

Conclusion: 无关但可用的工具会显著削弱LLM凭自身知识作答的能力，且该效应无法由工具调用行为单独解释；通过加入一句范围感知的系统指令可以缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed with external tools that extend what they can do beyond their own knowledge. Tools help on tasks that need external information, but their availability may also change how a model handles questions that do not need them. Prior work has mostly asked whether models select and use tools appropriately; whether an unnecessary tool changes the correctness of answers has received less attention. We ask whether making a related but unnecessary tool available affects a model's ability to answer from its own knowledge, and whether a preceding tool interaction changes this behaviour. We construct 500 query pairs across 10 knowledge domains. Each pair consists of a tool query, which needs the domain's tool, and a closed-domain query, which does not. Six LLMs are evaluated with the tool unavailable, available, and available after a prior tool call. Across 3,000 baseline trials the pooled answer rate is 98.2%. When an unnecessary tool is available it falls to 63.5%, with large differences between models. The decrease occurs even when the tool is rarely called, so it cannot be explained by unnecessary tool invocation alone. A one-sentence scope-aware system instruction recovers most of the lost answers.

</details>


### [227] [Towards Evolving Context Parameterization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14168)
*Xiaobing Shi,Zherui Li,Yiming Jiang,Kun Wang,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE任务与MUSE-bench，并设计免训练的PLUME方法，应对上下文持续更新场景下的记忆更新与信息保持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文参数化方法假设上下文静态，缺乏在持续更新下区分有效性状态的显式机制，难以处理真实世界中记忆需随序列演化而更新的场景。

Method: 形式化Memory Updating with Sequential Evolution（MUSE）任务并构建MUSE-bench；提出免训练方法PLUME，构建全局更新表示，激活记忆证据形成局部参数视图，并在解码时自适应融合两者预测。

Result: 在MUSE-bench上，PLUME在序列演化设置中有效，平均ROUGE-L Recall相对提升29.9%，LLM-as-a-Judge相对提升54.9%。

Conclusion: PLUME在上下文持续更新场景下能兼顾全局状态保持与记忆证据调整，验证了其有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Context parameterization enables large language models (LLMs) to internalize contexts into reusable model parameters, avoiding repeated processing across subsequent queries. However, existing methods typically assume static contexts and lack explicit mechanisms for distinguishing validity states under continual updates. To study this real-world scenario, we formalized the Memory Updating with Sequential Evolution (MUSE) task and constructed MUSE-bench to evaluate update incorporation and unaffected-information preservation. The resulting challenge requires preserving the global state while adjusting the contribution of memory evidence. Motivated by this, we proposed PLUME, a training-free method that constructs a global update representation, activates memory evidence to form a local parameter view, and adaptively integrates their predictions during decoding. Comprehensive evaluation on MUSE-bench demonstrated PLUME's effectiveness in sequential evolution settings, yielding relative improvements of 29.9% in average ROUGE-L Recall and 54.9% in LLM-as-a-Judge. Our codes are available at: https://github.com/xiaobingshi-LLM/PLUME.

</details>


### [228] [A Multi-Stage Agentic Framework for Effective Counter-Narrative Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14178)
*Carmel Kronfeld,Sharva Gogawale,Tetsuro Kobayashi,Irad Ben-Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段智能体框架，用于生成、精炼和评估反叙事（CNs），以应对网络仇恨言论与虚假信息，实验证明其生成的反叙事在说服力、情感参与和传播性上接近或优于专家撰写内容，并能削弱亲俄叙事的感知强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上仇恨言论与虚假信息快速扩散，直接压制可能加剧极化、公众不信任和极端叙事。LLM驱动的反叙事有望降低这些风险，但其效果取决于修辞与风格选择，而这一点理解不足。因此需要一个可系统生成、精炼并评估反叙事的框架。

Method: 提出了一个多阶段智能体（agent-based）框架，用于生成、精炼和评估反叙事。先通过人类评估者的小规模试点实验，识别有效的"技巧-风格"配对（如重复结合情感框架可增强说服力）；再基于这些洞见设计多智能体精炼流程，迭代提升反叙事的说服力、情感参与度与可分享性；随后进行人类验证，并开展自动化安全分析，最后通过模拟实验评估效果。

Result: 试点实验识别出有效的技巧-风格配对；多智能体精炼后经人类验证确认效果提升；自动化安全分析显示精炼后反叙事与专家撰写的反言论相当或更优；模拟实验表明精炼反叙事降低了亲俄叙事的感知强度，并持续优于原始LLM基线。

Conclusion: 该框架为可扩展、针对特定叙事的仇恨言论与虚假信息干预提供了一条可行路径，并具备跨领域迁移潜力。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: The rapid diffusion of hate speech and misinformation on social networks challenges democratic societies, since direct suppression efforts may deepen polarization, fuel public distrusts, and strengthen extremist narratives. LLM-driven counter-narratives (CNs) offer a promising way to reduce those risks, yet their effectiveness depends on rhetorical and stylistic choices that remain poorly understood. We present a multi-stage agent-based framework for generating, refining, and evaluating CNs, applied to pro-Russian hate and misinformation narratives on the war with Ukraine and adaptable to other domains. A pilot experiment with human evaluators identifies effective technique style pairings, such as repetition with emotional framing enhancing persuasiveness. Building on these insights, we introduce a multi-agent refinement process that iteratively improves CNs for persuasiveness, emotional engagement, and shareability. After human validation confirmed improvement, an automated safety analysis shows that our refined CNs match or improve on expert-written counterspeech. A simulated experiment then shows that they reduce the perceived strength of pro-Russian narratives and consistently outperform a vanilla LLM baseline, highlighting a pathway toward scalable, narrative-specific interventions against hate speech and misinformation. Code and data accompanying this work are publicly available at https://github.com/carmelkron/inlg2026-counter-narratives.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Refer from Estimated Listener Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14207)
*Téa Wright,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用人类注视扫视路径（gaze scanpaths）作为隐式学习信号，对视觉-语言模型进行微调，以生成更符合语用学的最优指称表达，显著缩短指称长度并提升指称成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型生成的指称表达往往不够符合语用最优性，且以往学习多依赖显式的交流成功信号，忽略了听者理解过程中隐含可得的信号。作者希望利用听者增量理解过程（以注视扫视路径形式呈现）作为更丰富的学习信号。

Method: 训练时从待优化说话者策略中采样指称表达（条件为图像与目标指称物）；用估计人类注视行为的神经听者模型将图像与采样表达映射为扫视路径（由注视点序列构成，每个注视点对应表达中的一个词）；尝试多种方法将注视序列与目标指称物转化为token级和序列级奖励，并用这些奖励优化策略参数。

Result: 通过人类听者评估，使用注视估计听者训练的说话者策略比基础模型产生显著更符合语用最优性的指称：序列长度从15.4词降至4.0词，同时指称成功率从75.2%提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过基于语言的交互来学习生成话语具有广阔前景——不仅可从交流成功的显式信号中学习，还可从听者理解过程中隐式可得的观察中学习。

Abstract: We propose to finetune vision-language models to generate more pragmatically optimal referring expressions by transforming observations of incremental listener comprehension, in the form of gaze scanpaths, into learning signals. During training, referring expressions are sampled from the speaker policy being optimized, conditioned on images and target referents; then, a neural listener estimating human gaze behavior maps from images and sampled referring expressions to scanpaths, each represented by a sequence of fixations, with each fixation corresponding to a word in the referring expression. We experiment with several approaches to convert fixation sequences and target referents into token- and sequence-level rewards, which are used to optimize policy parameters. Through evaluation with human listeners, we find that speaker policies trained with gaze-estimating listeners result in significantly more pragmatically-optimal references than base models, reducing sequence length from 15.4 down to 4.0 words while increasing referential success from 75.2 up to 80.0%. Our work demonstrates a promising opportunity for learning to generate utterances through language-based interaction, not only from the explicit signal of communicative success, but also from implicitly-available observations of a listener's process of comprehension.

</details>


### [230] [Corpus Characterization and Inverse Constitutional Fine-Tuning for Style-Aware Radiology Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14226)
*Sarah Y. Li,Elijah Renner,Rayan Ansari,Alaa Youssef*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过语料级风格刻画与"逆宪法AI"方法，使自动化放射报告在结构、用词与不确定性表达上更贴近放射科医生的真实写作风格，文本对齐指标大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动放射报告生成在诊断准确性上进步很快，但生成文本在结构、用词和不确定性语言上与真实放射科医生的写作习惯存在明显差异，这会影响临床医生的信任和使用体验，因此需要一种能对齐写作风格的生成策略。

Method: 使用Bio-ClinicalBERT嵌入、UMAP降维与HDBSCAN聚类，对CheXpert Plus的2,000份报告进行风格差异刻画，识别出5种在病理侧重、叙事结构与词汇偏好上不同的报告模式；随后改造"逆宪法AI"框架，从放射科医生撰写的报告配对中自动提炼出聚焦风格的"宪法"（涵盖语气、用词、不确定性校准与报告结构），并将其融入MedGemma-4B基座模型在25,245对CheXpert Plus训练数据上的监督微调。

Result: 宪法式微调相较未调优基线在文本对齐上有显著提升：BLEU-4从0.006升至0.308，ROUGE-L从0.171升至0.484；基线因格式不匹配而得分接近零，因此这一提升反映的是结构与词汇对齐上的质性转变，而非边际改善。

Conclusion: 语料级风格刻画结合宪法式建模，是一种有效且数据高效的方法，可生成符合真实放射科医生写作规范的报告。该方法无需正式偏好数据集，为风格对齐提供了可迁移的思路。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation has advanced rapidly in diagnostic accuracy, yet generated reports frequently diverge from the stylistic conventions of authentic radiologist writing in structure, diction, and uncertainty language, a gap which has direct implications for clinician trust and user experience. To address this, we characterize stylistic variation across 2,000 reports from the CheXpert Plus dataset using Bio-ClinicalBERT embeddings, UMAP dimensionality reduction, and HDBSCAN clustering, identifying five distinct reporting patterns differing in pathology focus, narrative structure, and lexical preference. Drawing on these findings, we adapt the inverse constitutional AI framework to derive a style-focused constitution from radiologist-written report pairs without requiring a formal preference dataset. This constitution, encoding conventions of tone, diction, uncertainty calibration, and report structure, is incorporated into the supervised fine-tuning of a MedGemma-4B base model on 25,245 CheXpert Plus training pairs. Constitutional fine-tuning produces a substantial increases in text alignment (BLEU-4: 0.006 to 0.308; ROUGE-L: 0.171 to 0.484) relative to the untuned baseline. These gains show a qualitative shift in structural and lexical alignment rather than marginal improvement, as the baseline model produces near-zero scores due to format mismatch. Overall, we establish corpus-level style characterization and constitutional modeling as an effective and data-efficient strategy for producing radiology reports that conform to authentic radiologist writing conventions.

</details>


### [231] [The Attribution-Compression Frontier in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14245)
*Deepanshu Mody*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统测量了检索增强生成中不同上下文压缩方法的引用归因质量，发现在固定生成器与评估器下，摘要式压缩的引用精度在溯源到原文时大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文压缩虽能减少生成器输入，但仅用答案质量无法刻画引用归因是否真正源于原文。作者希望填补压缩方法与预算对引用归因影响的测量空白。

Method: 在ASQA与QASPER上，固定生成器和主蕴含评估器，对比重排序、抽取式选择、摘要式压缩、token剪枝以及提取-聚类-重写构造；通过"再归因协议"将引用从摘要映射回源span，并做声明验证；另进行200问TRUE T5-XXL审计。

Result: ASQA名义0.25预算（实际压缩0.08）下，RECOMP式压缩器引用对摘要精度0.86，但对源span仅0.12；抽取式选择的有据精度在0.43–0.49之间，答案质量下降；源恢复后声明验证不支持率0.88，而对摘要检查仅0.17；TRUE审计同样发现生成-有据差距。

Conclusion: 压缩方法的引用质量被严重高估，摘要式压缩尤其如此；该差距超出结构性缺失溯源的解释，但结果依赖评估器，且缺乏独立人工校准，支持率仍需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Context compression reduces generator input in retrieval-augmented generation, but answer quality alone does not characterize citation attribution. We measure citation attribution across compression methods and budgets, comparing reranking, extractive selection, abstractive summarization, token pruning, and an extract-cluster-rewrite construction on ASQA and QASPER under a fixed generator and primary entailment evaluator. On ASQA at a nominal 0.25 budget (achieved compression 0.08), a RECOMP-style compressor's citations score 0.86 precision against its summaries but 0.12 against source spans under our re-attributability protocol. These estimates depend on a shared NLI model for span recovery and citation scoring and lack independent human calibration. Extractive selection's observed grounded precision ranges from 0.43 to 0.49 across nominal budgets from one-half to one-tenth of the ASQA context, while answer quality declines. For the same RECOMP setting, claim verification after source recovery yields an unsupported rate of 0.88 versus 0.17 when checking summaries. This gap persists beyond structural rejection of missing provenance, but remains evaluator-dependent. A 200-question TRUE T5-XXL audit also finds emitted--grounded gaps under both fixed and recomputed source mappings, without establishing human-calibrated support rates.

</details>


### [232] [Document Topic Alignment Metrics for Evaluating Topic Models of Short-Text Public Health Communications on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14256)
*Wangjiaxuan Xin,Shuhua Yin,Yaorong Ge,Shi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出DoTA（Document-Topic Alignment）指标，一个关注主题分配一致性的评估框架，用于衡量文档与其被分配主题之间的语义对齐程度，并引入基于边界和判别式的变体以捕捉分配置信度和可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题模型广泛用于分析公共卫生相关的社交媒体短文本，但现有评估主要依赖仅关注生成主题本身的指标，缺乏能够定量评估"分配的主题是否真正代表对应短文本帖子"的指标。

Method: 提出DoTA评估框架，包含衡量文档（帖子）与其被分配主题之间语义对齐的多个指标，并设计基于margin和判别式的变体以刻画主题分配置信度与可区分性。在来自X（推特）的三个公共卫生相关社交媒体数据集上，对五种主题模型进行评估，并将DoTA指标与传统基于主题的指标进行对比。

Result: DoTA提供了具有互补性的评估线索，并与人类评价具有有意义的一致性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了面向分配评估的必要性，并表明引入DoTA能够为短文本主题建模性能评估带来更全面、更具实际意义的评价。

Abstract: Topic models are widely used to analyze public health-related social media short texts, yet their evaluation remains dominated by metrics that focus entirely on generated topics alone. There is a lack of metrics that quantitatively assess whether assigned topics meaningfully represent the corresponding short-text posts. We propose Document-Topic Alignment metrics (DoTA), an assignment-aware evaluation framework comprising metrics that measure semantic alignment between documents (posts) and their assigned topics. We also introduce margin-based and discriminative variants that capture topic assignment confidence and distinguishability. We evaluate DoTA across five topic models on three public health-related social media datasets from X and compare DoTA metrics with conventional topic-based metrics. Results show that DoTA provides complementary evaluation cues and aligns meaningfully with human evaluations. These findings establish the need for assignment-aware evaluation and demonstrate that the addition of DoTA enables a more comprehensive and practically meaningful evaluation for assessing short-text topic modeling performance.

</details>


### [233] [DenMark: Robust Semantic Watermarking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14257)
*Tianhao Ma,Weihao Xuan,Dong-Dong Wu,Farshid Nooshi,Takashi Ishida,Gang Niu,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DenMark，一种针对扩散语言模型（DLM）的语义水印框架，通过将密钥相关信号注入去噪过程实现鲁棒水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义水印方法主要面向自回归语言模型（ARLM），依赖在生成前对完整候选单元进行生成与打分。而扩散语言模型的语义单元在中间去噪步骤中并不完整，且词元更新顺序灵活，因此该范式无法直接迁移到 DLM。

Method: DenMark 将输出划分为固定词元区域，并利用临时 rollout 作为语义前瞻：通过条件补全估计不完整区域的最终语义，从而选择语义水印得分更高的局部更新。在多个去噪步骤中重复该过程，逐步累积水印证据。检测阶段采用针对候选单元大小的校准扫描，以应对语义攻击带来的边界偏移。

Result: 在四个 DLM、三个数据集和四种语义攻击的 48 种骨干-数据集-攻击组合中，DenMark 在所有报告的检测指标上均取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: DenMark 为扩散语言模型提供了一种有效的鲁棒语义水印机制。

Abstract: Semantic text watermarks encode signals in meaning rather than surface token choices, offering robustness to paraphrasing and other semantic-preserving edits. Existing semantic watermarking methods are primarily designed for autoregressive language models (ARLMs), where completed candidate units can be generated and scored before generation proceeds. This paradigm does not naturally extend to diffusion language models (DLMs), where semantic units remain incomplete during intermediate denoising steps and tokens may be updated in flexible orders. We propose DenMark, a semantic watermarking framework that injects key-dependent signals directly into the DLM denoising process. DenMark partitions the output into fixed token regions and uses temporary rollouts as semantic lookahead: conditional completions estimate the eventual semantics of an incomplete region, enabling DenMark to select local updates with higher estimated semantic watermark scores. Repeating this procedure across denoising steps progressively accumulates watermark evidence in the final output. For detection, DenMark uses calibrated scanning over candidate unit sizes to remain robust to boundary shifts introduced by semantic attacks. Across four DLMs, three datasets, and four semantic attacks, DenMark achieves the best results across all reported detection metrics in all 48 backbone-dataset-attack combinations. These results demonstrate that DenMark provides an effective mechanism for robust semantic watermarking in DLMs.

</details>


### [234] [Editorial routing shapes how computational results are qualified in AI-assisted scientific writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14288)
*Jihan Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在计算证据固定的条件下，模型对研究流程中"比较任务"的存放位置敏感：存放在组内仓库时更常省略数值限定，存放在补充信息或自用笔记时则保留更多；针对性放置规则可基本恢复限定表述，泛化准确性提醒则无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已参与计算结果分析与稿件撰写，可靠沟通与正确分析同样重要。作者关注一个被忽视的问题：即使计算证据不变，仅仅改变研究流程中比较任务的分派位置，是否会影响稿件中结果的表述方式。

Method: 在固定计算证据的前提下，采用受限句子写作任务，将同一比较分别指派到组内仓库、补充信息或模型自用工作笔记等不同位置，观察模型（Claude Sonnet 5 与 Claude Opus 5）是否保留数值限定；并检验针对性放置规则与泛化准确性提醒的干预效果，以及更长篇幅贡献中的表现。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5 在比较被指派到组内仓库时经常省略数值限定，而在指派到补充信息或自用笔记时保留更多；Claude Opus 5 敏感度较低。该效应不遵循简单的可及性排序。针对性放置规则基本恢复了句子层面的限定，泛化准确性提醒则无效。较长篇幅的贡献保留了数值限定，但部分跨计算设置的摘要仍被重定向至仓库。

Conclusion: 在AI工作流中记录上下文，并不能保证其在该结果被读者阅读之处得到传达；沟通可靠性取决于信息在工作流中的位置安排，而非仅取决于记录与否。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly analyze computational results and draft manuscripts, making reliable communication as important as correct analysis. Using fixed computational evidence, we tested whether assigning comparisons across modeling choices elsewhere in a research workflow changes manuscript reporting. In constrained sentence-writing tasks, Anthropic's Claude Sonnet 5 often omitted numerical qualifications when detailed comparisons were assigned to a group repository, but retained them more often when the same comparison was assigned to Supporting Information or its own working notes; Claude Opus 5 was less sensitive. These effects did not follow a simple accessibility ordering. A targeted placement rule largely restored sentence-level qualification, whereas a generic accuracy reminder did not. Longer contributions retained numerical qualifications, although some summaries across computational settings were still redirected to the repository. Thus, documenting context within an AI workflow does not ensure its communication where readers encounter the result.

</details>


### [235] [E2A-Bench: Benchmarking Evidence-to-Action Reliability in Financial Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14302)
*Xiaoya Wang,Yutong Xu,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 E2A-Bench 基准，从证据到行动的完整链条评估金融图表视觉语言模型，揭示单一半信分数掩盖的三类失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融 VLM 幻觉评估多为"claim-centric"，只判断生成陈述是否有支持，无法追踪证据在推理、置信度与最终行动之间是否可溯源，因此需要端到端的证据到行动评估。

Method: 构建 969 条查询的 E2A-Bench，来自 323 只沪深300成分股，包含三种输入模态与基于 OHLCV 的确定性证据锚点；提出 UCR、RCI、ECI、NDR 四项指标，分别评估 grounding、推理-行动一致性、证据-置信度校准与方向覆盖；评测 20 个 VLM。

Result: 低 UCR 模型因方向覆盖仅 6.4% 而在 NDR 上接近垫底；oracle 辅助验证减少无支持陈述但会压缩覆盖；金融微调使严格基座-微调配对中 BUY:SELL 比例放大 4.21–4.68 倍。

Conclusion: 金融 VLM 评估应追踪完整证据到行动链条，而非依赖单一幻觉分数。

Abstract: Can financial vision-language models (VLMs) turn chart evidence into reliable action recommendations? Existing hallucination evaluations are mostly claim-centric; they assess whether generated statements are supported, but not whether evidence remains traceable through rationale, confidence, and final action. We introduce E2A-Bench, a 969-query benchmark for financial chart reasoning, constructed from 323 HS300 constituents under three input modalities with deterministic OHLCV-derived evidence anchors. E2A-Bench evaluates grounding, reasoning-action consistency, evidence-confidence calibration, and directional coverage through UCR, RCI, ECI, and NDR, where NDR measures coverage-aware evidence-to-action reliability rather than realized trading performance. Evaluating 20 VLMs reveals three failures hidden by scalar hallucination scores: the lowest-UCR model ranks near the bottom by NDR due to only 6.4% directional coverage; oracle-aided verification reduces unsupported claims but can collapse coverage; and financial fine-tuning amplifies the BUY:SELL ratio by factors of 4.21 to 4.68 across strict base-fine-tuned pairs. These results show that financial VLM evaluation should trace the full evidence-to-action chain rather than rely on a single hallucination score. Code and data: https://github.com/wanng-ide/E2A-Bench

</details>


### [236] [SpectralShift: Effective Context Window Extension of Gated DeltaNet via Spectral Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14320)
*Zian Liu,Yiwen Hu,Zican Dong,Tian Xie,Wayne Xin Zhao,Yucheng Ding,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从转移矩阵的谱视角研究 Gated DeltaNet 的长上下文扩展，提出 SpectralShift 谱重参数化方法，通过重塑衰减谱改善长程信息检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力层逐渐取代 softmax 注意力用于长上下文建模，但现有上下文扩展方法通常直接进行继续预训练而不修改这些层，忽略了线性注意力状态动态的谱性质，导致长程信息检索能力不足。

Method: 从转移矩阵的谱视角分析 GDN，识别出两个控制长程信息检索的关键因素：与目标依赖长度对齐的足够宽的慢谱带，以及保留用于状态清除与上下文切换的快衰减模态。据此提出 SpectralShift：重参数化 alpha 投影的初始化以增强慢传播能力从而重塑衰减谱，并对 alpha 投影引入学习率缩放以促进长上下文训练。

Result: 实验表明 SpectralShift 在训练过程中持续提升长上下文能力，为扩展线性注意力模型的上下文窗口提供了有效且高效的方案。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过谱视角的初始化重参数化与学习率缩放，SpectralShift 能有效且高效地扩展 GDN 等线性注意力模型的上下文窗口。

Abstract: Recently, linear attention layers have been increasingly adopted to replace softmax attention at scale for long-context modeling. However, existing context extension approaches typically apply continued pretraining directly without modifying these layers, overlooking the spectral properties of linear attention state dynamics. In this work, we study long-context extension of Gated DeltaNet (GDN) from a spectral perspective of transition matrix and identify two essential factors governing long-range information retrieval: (1) a sufficiently broad slow spectral band aligned with the target dependency length, and (2) the preservation of fast-decaying modes for state clearing and context switching. Based on this observation, we propose SpectralShift, a spectral reparameterization approach for long-context continual pretraining of GDNs. Specifically, SpectralShift reparameterizes the alpha projections initialization to reshape the decay spectrum by enhancing slow propagation capacity, and further introduces a learning-rate scaling for alpha projections to facilitate long-context training. Experiments show that SpectralShift consistently improves long-context capabilities over training, providing an effective and efficient solution for extending context windows of linear attention models. The code has been open-sourced at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/GDN-SpectralShift.

</details>


### [237] [Formal Properties of Language as Constraints on Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14384)
*Elliot Murphy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"神经可容许性纲领"（NAP），用语言的代数性质（非结合性层级分组、交换性、递归闭合、子结构访问、结构化工作区转移）作为神经机制必须满足的不变量，并据此评估绑定操作。研究表明熵优化的绑定门仅在狭窄承诺带内保持分组，而新提出的"Meld"操作（共享突触收敛、次线性整合、饱和）是最接近句法Merge的神经可行组合律，满足所有NAP不变量。关键结论：解码精度最高的法则反而先验不可容许，说明解码准确性无法单独裁决机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多通过用语言学变量标注刺激、测试哪些电极/体素/语言模型层能预测神经活动来推断机制，但预测成功对机制的约束不足。作者希望用语言的代数性质来界定神经机制必须保持的不变量，从而为认知的神经实现提供更严格的评估标准。

Method: 提出神经可容许性纲领（NAP），对句法结构构建进行代数分析，并为每项要求提出候选机制：内容可寻址的工作区记忆、图结构瞬态动力学调度结构构建操作（如稳定异宿通道）、以及记录分组的相位耦合密封操作。通过仿真检验校正后的Marcolli-Berwick熵优化绑定门，并提出新的神经绑定操作"Meld"（两个成分群体通过共享突触收敛、次线性整合并饱和）。

Result: 仿真显示校正后的熵优化绑定门仅在狭窄的"承诺带"内保持分组。Meld则满足全部NAP不变量，使用已知皮层操作，在测试的所有深度与温度下都能恢复层级结构。它预测有效群体维度能区分不同的括号化方式，且复合体依赖于成分间的不一致。最关键的是，解码括号化最准确的法则在先验上是不可容许的。

Conclusion: NAP通过明确神经动力学如何忠实于语言结构，改变了评估认知神经实现的标准。解码准确性本身不足以裁决不同机制，机制的可容许性（是否保持语言代数不变量）应成为核心判据。

Abstract: What must a neural system be capable of to implement language? Current research annotates stimuli with linguistic variables and tests which electrodes, voxels, or language-model layers predict neural activity. Yet predictive success leaves mechanisms under-constrained. Here, we show that algebraic properties of language specify invariants that mechanisms must preserve: non-associative hierarchical grouping, commutativity, recursive closure, access to substructures, and structured workspace transitions. We term this the Neural Admissibility Program (NAP). Syntactic structure building is analyzed algebraically, with candidate mechanisms offered for each requirement: content-addressable workspace memory, graph-structured transient dynamics scheduling structure-building operations (e.g. stable heteroclinic channels), and a phase-coupled sealing operation recording grouping. Simulations show that a corrected Marcolli-Berwick entropy-optimized binding gate preserves grouping only within a narrow commitment band. As an alternative, we propose a novel neural binding operation we term 'Meld': two constituent populations converge through shared synapses, integrate sublinearly, and saturate. Meld is, to our knowledge, the closest neurally plausible composition law to syntactic Merge. It preserves every NAP invariant, uses known cortical operations, and recovers hierarchical structure at every tested depth and temperature. It predicts that effective population dimensionality separates alternative bracketings and that the composite depends on constituent disagreement. Importantly, the laws decoding bracketing most accurately are a priori inadmissible, showing that decoding accuracy alone cannot adjudicate between mechanisms. By specifying how neural dynamics can remain faithful to linguistic structure, the NAP changes the criterion by which neural implementations of cognition are evaluated.

</details>


### [238] [Policy Loopholes in Agent Evaluation: When Policy Ambiguity Masquerades as Agent Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14400)
*Hongliu Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 智能体基准测试假设每条策略对应唯一正确动作，但自然语言策略存在沉默、歧义、矛盾等漏洞，导致多个可辩护的合法解读。作者审计两个τ²-bench领域，构建漏洞分类体系，证明受影响的策略会产生跨模型、跨试验不稳定的不可靠评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准以策略合规性为评估核心，隐含假定每条策略唯一决定正确动作。然而自然语言策略并非形式化规范，因沉默、歧义或矛盾而允许多种合理读法，单一黄金轨迹无法涵盖，动摇了评测分数的可信度。作者希望揭示这一根本隐患并说明其后果。

Method: 审计两个τ²-bench领域，构建自然语言策略漏洞的分类体系；通过跨模型比较与重复试验一致性检验评估漏洞对分数的影响；并进行跨领域对比，以分离策略歧义与工具权限宽松两个因素的相对作用。

Result: 含有漏洞的任务会产生不可靠分数：以不同方式压低不同模型的得分，并降低所有模型在重复试验中的一致性。跨领域比较表明，可利用性需要同时具备策略歧义与工具权限宽松；当策略复杂度超出工具所能约束的范围时，智能体对漏洞的解读不一致，评分随即变得不可靠。

Conclusion: 策略规范的质量决定了评测质量的上限。基准开发者应在收集黄金标注之前先审计策略，以识别沉默、歧义和矛盾等问题，避免黄金轨迹固化了偏差读法，从而提升评测的可靠性与公平性。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks evaluate policy compliance but assume each policy determines a unique correct action. Natural-language policies can violate this assumption through silence, ambiguity, or contradiction, admitting multiple defensible readings that a single gold trajectory cannot capture. Auditing two $τ^2$-bench domains, we develop a taxonomy of such policy loopholes and show that affected tasks produce unreliable scores: they lower scores across different models in different ways and make every model less consistent across repeated trials. A cross-domain comparison reveals that exploitability requires both policy ambiguity and tool permissiveness: when policy complexity exceeds what tools can enforce, agents resolve gaps inconsistently and scores become unreliable. Policy specification quality sets the ceiling on evaluation quality. Benchmark developers should audit policies before collecting gold annotations.

</details>


### [239] [NeuroActiSep: Detecting Factual Hallucinations from Feed-Forward Neurons in a Single Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14448)
*Ali Derogar Odolou,Reza Nazari,Mostafa Salehi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于前馈神经元筛选的幻觉检测方法，通过在最后一个提示token处排序神经元并迁移其身份，训练跨数据集幻觉分类器，并与内部状态探针性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉问题降低可靠性、阻碍应用；现有白盒研究多利用内部表示检测真实性，而基于前馈神经元与幻觉关联的研究较少。

Method: 构建自定义神经元选择数据集，在最终提示token处对前馈神经元进行排序筛选；将筛选出的神经元身份迁移用于在其他事实问答数据集上训练幻觉分类器。

Result: 使用所选神经元特征训练的探针，性能与基于内部状态训练的探针相当；并分析了所选神经元的分布及层深对检测性能的影响。

Conclusion: 提供了经验证据，表明筛选前馈神经元可有效检测幻觉，为白盒幻觉检测提供了一种可行且性能相当的新路径。

Abstract: Hallucination in large language models reduces their reliability and slows adoption. Various white-box studies have used internal representations to detect patterns of truthfulness and factuality. A less-studied approach is to identify feed-forward neurons correlated with hallucination. We propose a method to rank feed-forward neurons at the final prompt token using a custom neuron selection dataset. We transfer the selected neuron identities to train hallucination classifiers on other factual question answering datasets. Our work provides empirical evidence that probes trained using the features from the selected neurons perform on par with probes trained on internal states. We also analyze the distribution of selected neurons and the effect of layer depth on detection performance.

</details>


### [240] [Theseus in the Graph: Towards Traceable Multi-Hop Graph Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14528)
*Eduin E. Hernandez,Luis F. Garcia,Nurassyl Askar,Sergio A. Diaz,Stefano Rini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将多跳知识图谱问答重新定义为"问题条件化的图导航问题"（THESEUS），通过构建带证据路径标注的数据集、设计评估协议，并改造路径式智能体使其由自然语言问题引导，从而实现可追溯的多跳推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳KGQA系统只关注预测最终答案，不显式建模或验证中间推理步骤，导致无法判断正确答案是否来自忠实的多跳推理，缺乏可追溯性。

Method: 将KGQA重构为问题条件化的图导航：智能体接收知识图谱、问题和主题实体，沿关系序列遍历到答案，使推理路径显式化。具体包括三点：(i) 将KINSHIP和MQuAKE资源扩展为带证据路径标注和改写问题的导航式数据集；(ii) 设计评估协议衡量路径保真度、语言变化鲁棒性及多跳/多答案表现；(iii) 将MINERVA、MultiHopKG、SQUIRE等路径式KG补全智能体改造为基于完整问题嵌入（而非符号化单关系查询）运行。

Result: 构建了带证据路径和问题改写的导航式KGQA数据集，建立了评估路径保真度、鲁棒性和多跳/多答案性能的协议，并使多个路径式智能体能够在自然语言语义引导下运行。

Conclusion: 这些贡献推动KGQA研究向"可追溯性为核心"的方向发展：答案伴随显式推理路径，且其与参考证据的一致性可被系统评估。

Abstract: Multi-Hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) tasks require models to assemble relational evidence along paths in a KG to answer natural-language questions. However, existing KGQA systems typically focus on predicting the final answer without explicitly modeling or validating the intermediate reasoning steps, obscuring whether the correct answers arise from faithful multi-hop reasoning. To address this limitation, we re-frame multi-hop KGQA as a question-conditioned graph navigation problem. We refer to this formulation as THESEUS - Traceable Hop-wise Evidence SEarch in a Unified Semantics. In this setting, an agent receives a KG, a question, and a topic entity, and traverses a sequence of relations towards the answer, making the reasoning path explicit. To systematically study this formulation, we provide three key contributions. (i) We augment the existing KINSHIP and MQuAKE resources into navigation-ready KGQA datasets with annotated evidence paths and paraphrased questions. (ii) We design evaluation protocols to measure path fidelity, robustness to linguistic variation, and performance across multi-hop and multi-answer questions. (iii) We adapt established path-based KG completion agents - MINERVA, MultiHopKG, and SQUIRE - to operate on full question embeddings rather than symbolic single-relation queries, enabling their trajectories to be guided by natural-language semantics. Together, these contributions advance KGQA research toward systems where traceability is fundamental: answers are accompanied by explicit reasoning paths whose agreement with reference evidence can be systematically evaluated.

</details>


### [241] [Neyshekar: An Open Persian Read-Speech Corpus for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14542)
*Ahmad Amirivojdan,Farzad Nadiri,Abolfazl Alizadeh,Shaghayegh Yaraghi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Neyshekar 是一个开源波斯语朗读语音语料库（v6），含 62,279 条经审核录音、99.02 小时、来自 190 位贡献者，覆盖正式与非正式语言；在同等约 32 小时训练预算下，相较 Common Voice 显著降低 WER（域内 Whisper 降 9.5、XLS-R 降 11.6 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语缺乏同时覆盖正式/非正式语体、命名实体与长句的朗读语音资源，且现有语料（如 Common Voice）在录音量、说话人可追溯性与测试集划分上难以支持稳健的 ASR 评测。

Method: 文本提示来自人工撰写材料、情境化同形异义词与经审核的语言模型生成文本，并用 shekar 库做正式/非正式归一化；每条录音按统一验证量表审核。提供条目级评分者标签以估计一致性，采用不可逆的每片段贡献者标识实现可审计的说话人切分与贡献者聚类不确定性估计，并设置文本不相交测试子集。与 Common Voice 在相同处理流程下对比，用两种 ASR 架构、三个优化种子、WER/CER 及公开 PSRB 样本独立评估。

Result: 在约 32 小时的等时长训练下，域内 WER 相比 Common Voice：Whisper 降低 9.5 个百分点，XLS-R 降低 11.6 个百分点；在独立 PSRB 样本上两者均约降低 8 个百分点。自动分类显示约 24% 的片段为非正式（该标签未经人工验证）。迁移与混合训练的收益在不同架构和训练预算下并不一致。

Conclusion: Neyshekar 提供了可复现、可审计的波斯语正式/非正式朗读语音资源，在等时长条件下优于 Common Voice，且附带贡献者负载与无参考信号质量刻画；语料以 CC0 发布，代码与数据通过项目仓库开放获取。

Abstract: Neyshekar is presented as an open Persian read-speech corpus designed for coverage of both formal and informal language, named entities, and longer utterances. In version 6, 62,279 validated recordings totalling 99.02 hours are provided from 190 contributors, with 34,541 distinct recorded prompts. The prompt pool was assembled from human-written material, contextualised homographs, and reviewed language-model-generated text. Text entries were normalised with the shekar library, which supports both formal and informal Persian, and every submitted recording was reviewed against a common validation rubric. About 24% of released clips are classified as informal by an automatic classifier; these register labels are not human-validated. Item-level rater labels are provided for reproducible agreement estimation, opaque per-clip contributor identifiers make the speaker-disjoint partitioning auditable and support contributor-clustered uncertainty estimates, and a text-disjoint test subset is included for evaluation beyond previously seen prompts. Per-contributor recording load and reference-free signal quality are characterised for every released clip. Corpus characteristics are compared with Persian Common Voice under shared processing. Utility is assessed through two ASR architectures, three optimisation seeds, WER and CER, and independent evaluation on the public PSRB sample. Against duration-matched Common Voice training at approximately 32 hours, in-domain WER is reduced by 9.5 points for Whisper and 11.6 points for XLS-R, and by approximately eight points for both architectures on the independent PSRB sample. Transfer and mixture benefits are not consistently observed across architectures and training budgets. The corpus is released under CC0; code and data are made available through the project repository at https://github.com/amirivojdan/neyshekar.

</details>


### [242] [TATK: Triple-Aware Top-K Learning with Knowledge-Grounded Verification for LLM-based Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14565)
*Yuchen Guan,Jiaye Liu,Yifei Han,Zhenxi Zhang,Yixuan Weng,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TATK框架，将Top-K学习与知识图谱验证结合，用于LLM序列推荐的全目录排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM序列推荐常将下一项预测建模为文本生成，但这种接口与全目录top-K排序不匹配。

Method: TATK包含两部分：Top-K学习（TKL）结合上下文感知的元数据-KG提示接地与位置感知的top-K奖励；知识接地验证（KGV）在单次LLM前向传播后，基于同一元数据物品图对top-M候选做结构感知重排。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews 2023三个数据集上，TATK在全部36项指标上均超过R2ec复现；NDCG@10上Qwen提升8.05%、4.26%、3.78%，Gemma提升27.03%、10.52%、10.23%，推理延迟为Base RecPO的1.17倍以内。

Conclusion: 结构证据对具有可靠KG支持的可恢复top-M候选最有用，当元数据关系稀疏或噪声大时应进行门控。

Abstract: LLM-based sequential recommenders usually cast next-item prediction as text generation, but this interface is poorly matched to full-catalog top-K ranking. We propose TATK, a Triple-Aware framework that couples Top-K Learning (TKL) with Knowledge-Grounded Verification (KGV) for LLM-based sequential recommendation. Top-K Learning combines context-aware metadata-KG prompt grounding with position-aware top-K rewards, aligning training with ranking utility; Knowledge-Grounded Verification then applies structure-aware reranking over the top-M candidates after a single LLM forward pass, using the same metadata-derived item graph. We evaluate TATK on Musical Instruments, CDs and Vinyl, and Video Games from Amazon Reviews 2023 under a matched R2ec-style full-catalog protocol. Experiments use Gemma-2-2B-It and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct backbones, compare against sequential, generative, KG-augmented, and reasoning-enhanced baselines, and include component, reward-shape, sequence-perturbation, reranking, relation-quality, and candidate-pool diagnostics. TATK improves over the matched R2ec reproduction on all 36 reported metrics.
